### [1] [Video-DCI: How Far Are VLM Agents from Human Viewers in Direct Video Corpus Interaction?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09951)
*Garry Yang,Yuqiao Liu,Hao Lin,Luohao Pan,Xinru Chen,Wanqi Ren,Zhikang Xu,Yizhan Huang,Huaixiao Tou,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Video-DCI 基准与人类-智能体协议，用于诊断"直接视频语料库交互"中的多轮视觉证据寻求能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类回答复杂视觉问题靠的是"看短片段—更新认知—重新分配注意力"的迭代过程，但现有视频基准只提供相关视频、仅评测最终答案或孤立定位、允许文本捷径，使多轮视觉证据寻求（multi-round visual evidence seeking）缺乏研究。

Method: 构建 Video-DCI 诊断基准及功能对齐的人类-智能体协议，含两个子任务：DCI-Loc 做时间证据定位；DCI-QA 做跨视频证据的端到端发现、保持与组合。DCI-QA 中池内视频主题相关，但答案必须依赖多个视频中的隐藏时间证据；给定问题与视频池，人类与智能体迭代检视有界局部窗口、保留支撑证据并以带时间跨度引用作答；人类轨迹提供多轮证据获取与使用的参考曲线。并进行消融对比迭代搜索与一次性检索。

Result: 实验发现一致的"检索—落地"（retrieval-to-grounding）鸿沟：在 DCI-Loc 上，前沿 VLM 常能检索并暴露参考时刻，但转化为精确最终引用的比例远低于人类参考；在 DCI-QA 上，损失出现更早且跨视频累积。消融表明迭代搜索优于一次性检索。

Conclusion: 多轮搜索是必要但不充分的：当前智能体更易累积候选与观察，却难以获得精确且联合充分的证据。

Abstract: Humans answer complex visual questions by inspecting short moments, updating what they know, and redirecting attention toward missing evidence. Existing video benchmarks, however, often provide the relevant video, evaluate only final answers or isolated localization, or permit textual shortcuts, leaving multi-round visual evidence seeking underexplored. We introduce Video-DCI, a diagnostic benchmark and functionally matched human-agent protocol for Direct Video-Corpus Interaction. It comprises DCI-Loc for temporal evidence localization and DCI-QA for end-to-end discovery, retention, and composition of cross-video evidence. In DCI-QA, videos within each pool are topically related, yet every answer requires hidden temporal evidence from multiple videos. Given only the question and video pool, humans and agents iteratively inspect bounded local windows, retain supporting evidence, and answer with cited temporal spans. Human trajectories provide reference curves for evidence acquisition and use across rounds. Experiments reveal a consistent retrieval-to-grounding gap. On DCI-Loc, frontier VLMs often retrieve and expose the reference moment, yet convert far less of this exposure into precise final citations than the human reference. On DCI-QA, the loss begins earlier and compounds across videos. Ablation studies further confirm that iterative search outperforms one-shot retrieval. Multi-round search is therefore necessary but insufficient: current agents accumulate candidates and observations more readily than precise, jointly sufficient evidence.

</details>


### [2] [The RenAIssance: Caption-Guided Diffusion Models for Historical-Art Visual Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10599)
*Zhanyu Tuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RenAIssance 流水线：用 BLIP 为 DEArt 数据集生成约 1.5 万对图像—文本数据，训练 Stable Diffusion v1.5 的紧凑 LoRA 适配器，使生成结果向欧洲历史艺术分布靠拢，同时保留基座模型对新概念的渲染能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式 AI 可将文化藏品扩展为提示词驱动的视觉媒体，但历史艺术品普遍缺乏适配现代图像生成器所需的成对描述文本，难以直接微调。

Method: 构建可扩展流水线：以 BLIP 生成场景级图像描述，基于 DEArt 得到约 15,000 图像—文本对；在此基础上训练面向 Stable Diffusion v1.5 的紧凑 LoRA 适配器，将可复用的风格表征与基座生成器解耦。评估涵盖不同长度的历史性与当代提示词，采用 FID（以 DEArt 参考分布为基准）与 CLIP 文本—图像相似度，并辅以定性分析。

Result: 在所有提示词设置下 FID 相对 DEArt 参考分布均有改善，CLIP 文本—图像相似度仅轻微下降；定性结果显示同一适配器既能生成符合时代特征的题材，也能以连贯的历史视觉语言表现当代概念。

Conclusion: 将可复用风格表征与基座生成器解耦，可为可控历史艺术媒体创作、交互式创作流程与富媒体体验提供实用构件。

Abstract: Generative AI can expand cultural collections into prompt-responsive visual media, but historical artworks rarely provide the paired descriptions needed to adapt modern image generators. We present The RenAIssance, a scalable pipeline that turns a collection of historical European artworks into a reusable text-to-image media resource. The pipeline generates scene-level captions with BLIP and uses the resulting approximately 15,000 image--text pairs from DEArt to train a compact LoRA adapter for Stable Diffusion v1.5. The adapter shifts generated images toward the historical-art distribution while retaining the base model's ability to render novel concepts. On historical and contemporary prompts of different lengths, it improves FID to the DEArt reference distribution in every setting with only a modest decrease in CLIP text--image similarity. Qualitative results show that the same adapter can render both period-appropriate subjects and contemporary concepts in a coherent historical visual language. By decoupling a reusable style representation from the base generator, the approach offers a practical building block for controllable historical-art media creation, interactive authoring, and rich-media experiences.

</details>


### [3] [Counting Events, Not Seeing Them: Diagnosing and Repairing the Readout Failure in Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09949)
*Dongwu Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Video LLM计数失败的归因：计数信号存在但未被读出（存在-输出解耦），通过读出导向的修复（事件局部化监督、离散事件符号侧信道）可将同物体计数从46.7%提升至65.0%（Oracle 66.7%，随机基线33%），并揭示真实视频上的边界由检测器证据与读出器对噪声的匹配决定，而非读出器容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作将Video LLM在"物体穿越屏幕次数"计数上的显著失败归因于缺少物体永久性（object permanence）。作者认为该归因不完整：需要检验表征中是否存在可解码的计数信号，以及失败是否源于读出机制而非表征缺失。同时指出原测量装置存在数据完整性问题，掩盖了真实边界所在。

Method: 1) 构建构造式（construction-grounded）合成测试床，标签独立验证；2) 用线性探针分别解码逐窗口存在性与全局计数，分离表征与读出；3) 提出两种读出导向修复：事件局部化（episode-localization）监督与离散事件符号侧信道；4) 在7B–30B模型上验证，并做独立工厂的迁移测试；5) 审计测量装置，识别四项数据完整性问题；6) 构建真实视频基准REALCOUNT（n=18，盲审）；7) 消融阶数预算（1 epoch vs 3 epochs）、Oracle时间线与部署检测器时间线；8) 冻结特征边界探针（AUC与置换对照）评估信号强度与跨片段迁移性。

Result: 逐窗口存在性线性探针达93%，而全局计数解码坍缩为时长混淆；两种修复将同物体计数由46.7%升至65.0%（Oracle 66.7%，随机33%），跨独立工厂零成本迁移；真实视频上最佳仅55.6%（随机33.3%），符号侧信道零样本迁移失败；1-epoch域内适配为负（六种手段28–50%），3-epoch + 序列不相交Oracle时间线达94.4%，部署检测器时间线2-epoch达72.2%；冻结特征边界探针信号弱（AUC 0.62 vs 0.51置换对照），跨片段不迁移（窗内0.77 vs 跨窗0.62）。

Conclusion: Video LLM的计数失败并非单纯的物体永久性缺失，而是"表征存在、读出失效"的解耦问题，且在退出-重现事件计数中可通过读出导向修复；真实视频上的残余差距主要来自检测器证据质量与读出器对检测器噪声的匹配，而非读出器容量。同时提示相关评测需警惕测量装置的数据完整性问题。

Abstract: Video LLMs fail conspicuously at counting how many times an object crosses the screen, a failure attributed to missing object permanence. Using construction-grounded synthetic testbeds with independently validated labels, we show this attribution is incomplete: the count signal is present but unread - linear probes decode per-window presence at 93% while global count decoding collapses to a duration confound. Concurrent work reports the same representation-output dissociation for repetition counting; we show that in exit-and-reappear event counting it is repairable: two readout-directed repairs (episode-localization supervision and a discrete event-symbol side channel) raise same-object counting from 46.7% to 65.0% (oracle 66.7%) on a chance-33% benchmark across 7B-30B systems. Transfer to an independent factory costs nothing, and an audit of the measurement apparatus exposes four data-integrity failures that had obscured the boundary. A real-video audit (REALCOUNT, n=18, blind-audited) shows the boundary persists - best 55.6% at chance 33.3% - and symbol transfer fails zero-shot. A 1-epoch in-domain adaptation bench is negative (six levers at 28-50%), but a 3-epoch schedule shows this is budget-limited for the symbol channel: with a 3-epoch schedule and sequence-disjoint oracle timelines the reader reaches 94.4% on REALCOUNT, and with the deployed detector's timelines a 2-epoch run reaches 72.2%. The residual gap is detector evidence and the reader's match to detector noise, not reader capacity: a frozen-feature boundary probe finds the signal weak (AUC 0.62 over a 0.51 permutation control) and non-transferring across clips (0.77 within-clip vs. 0.62 across windows).

</details>


### [4] [Confusion-Adaptive Margin Learning for Handwritten Letter Recognition: A Controlled Study on EMNIST Letters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09953)
*Azam Nouri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Confusion-Adaptive Margin Learning (CAML)，通过混淆图与成对余弦间隔约束改变易混字符对的错误结构，但不引入额外推理参数；实验显示全局精度提升微小且不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于几何的手写字符识别中仍残留视觉相似类之间的混淆错误，作者希望从训练目标（损失函数）层面而非模型结构层面缓解这些成对混淆。

Method: 从试点训练中留出的发现集构建稀疏加权混淆图，在最终训练阶段对特定字对施加成对余弦分离约束；不增加分类器、路由规则或推理参数。在EMNIST Letters上与严格匹配的交叉熵CNN基线对比（同架构、同数据、确定性增强、同优化器与检查点选择），针对8个混淆对（I/L、G/Q、U/V、R/V、D/O、C/E、I/J、H/N）。

Result: 干净测试集精度由95.6346%升至95.6731%，macro-F1由0.956326升至0.956724；十种预设退化条件下平均精度由约95.3812%升至95.4303%；目标对内的对称错误由614降至605（1.47%）。但全局差异小且不显著：干净数据上修正124个基线错误同时新增116个（McNemar精确检验p=0.6515），经Holm校正后无退化条件差异显著。

Conclusion: CAML未能确立全局优越性，但表明在防泄漏控制下，成对特定的间隔目标可改变目标混淆对的错误结构，对已较强分类器的整体收益有限。

Abstract: Handwritten character recognition systems can achieve high accuracy while retaining concentrated errors among visually similar classes. Motivated by residual letter confusions in earlier geometry-based recognition work, we investigate whether these errors can be addressed through the training objective. We introduce Confusion-Adaptive Margin Learning (CAML), which constructs a sparse weighted confusion graph from a discovery partition held out from pilot training and applies pair-specific cosine-separation constraints during final training. CAML adds no classifier, routing rule, or inference-time parameters.
  We evaluate CAML on EMNIST Letters against a strictly matched cross-entropy CNN baseline, using the same architecture, final training data, deterministic augmentation, optimizer, and checkpoint selection. Eight confusion pairs are targeted: I/L, G/Q, U/V, R/V, D/O, C/E, I/J, and H/N. On the official clean test set, accuracy changes from 95.6346% to 95.6731%, and macro-F1 from 0.956326 to 0.956724. Mean accuracy across ten predefined corrupted conditions increases from approximately 95.3812% to 95.4303%; symmetric errors within targeted pairs decrease from 614 to 605 (1.47%).
  The global differences are small and not statistically reliable. CAML fixes 124 baseline errors but introduces 116 new errors on clean data (exact McNemar p=0.6515), and no corruption-specific difference remains significant after Holm correction. Thus, CAML does not establish global superiority, but shows that a leakage-controlled, pair-specific margin objective can alter targeted error structure with limited overall benefit for an already strong classifier.

</details>


### [5] [DiffusedSplitFed: Latent Diffusion-based Obfuscation for Reconstruction-Resilient Split Federated Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09954)
*Chamani Shiranthika,Parvaneh Saeedi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiffusedSplitFed框架，在客户端-服务器切分点引入潜在去噪扩散模型(LDDM)对传输表征进行混淆，在保持分割性能的同时提升对重建攻击的抵抗能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SplitFed训练中交换的中间特征与梯度可能通过重建攻击泄露敏感病人信息，需在联邦/拆分学习场景下实现隐私保护与分割性能的平衡。

Method: 在SplitFed的client-server切分点集成LDDM以混淆传输表征；设计V1–V3三种变体，采用不同的双重条件(dual conditioning)与全局特征融合组合，考察重建抵抗性、分割精度与计算复杂度之间的权衡；在HAM10K、Blastocysts、FHPsAOPMSB三个医学影像数据集上进行多次独立运行与配对统计分析，并提供条件收敛分析刻画LDDM梯度扰动与局部优化对训练的影响。

Result: 相较Baseline SplitFed，对评估的图像重建攻击具有更强抵抗性，同时保持有竞争力的分割性能；与现有基于扩散的分割方法相比在评估设定下精度相当；条件收敛分析表征了LDDM梯度扰动与局部优化的作用。

Conclusion: 潜在扩散式表征混淆是实现抗重建的协作医学图像分割的有效途径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL), Split Learning (SL), and Split Federated Learning (SplitFed) enable collaborative medical image analysis without sharing raw patient data. However, intermediate features and gradients exchanged during SplitFed training may reveal sensitive information through reconstruction attacks. To address this vulnerability, we propose \textbf{DiffusedSplitFed}, a SplitFed framework that integrates Latent Denoising Diffusion Models (LDDMs) at client--server split points to obfuscate transmitted representations while preserving segmentation performance. Three architectural variants (V1--V3) are developed using different combinations of dual conditioning and global feature fusion to investigate trade-offs among reconstruction resistance, segmentation accuracy, and computational complexity. Experiments on three medical imaging datasets (HAM10K, Blastocysts, and FHPsAOPMSB), supported by multiple independent runs and paired statistical analyses, demonstrate improved resistance to the evaluated image reconstruction attacks compared with Baseline SplitFed, while maintaining competitive segmentation performance. Comparisons with existing diffusion-based segmentation methods further demonstrate competitive segmentation accuracy under the evaluated settings. A conditional convergence analysis is also provided to characterize the effects of LDDM-induced gradient perturbations and local optimization on training. These findings highlight the potential of latent diffusion-based representation obfuscation for reconstruction-resilient collaborative medical image segmentation. The implementation is available at https://github.com/ChamaniS/DiffusedSplitFed.

</details>


### [6] [RadAI: Gaze-Intention Fusion Pipeline for Attention-Guided Educational Videos in Chest X-Ray Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09955)
*Kavish Mehta,Yuvan Siddharth Katamaneni,Akash Awasthi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RadAI——首个端到端框架，将预测的放射科医生注视序列转化为配语音讲解的EMME式教学视频，无需采集新的眼动数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片阅片错误常源于视觉搜索失败：受训者被指出后能识别异常，但自身未注视相关区域；而专家搜索策略（看哪里、顺序、时长）多为隐性知识。传统眼动建模示例（EMME）虽有效，但每例都需真实眼动采集，导致教学集受限于已录制会话，难以规模化。

Method: 仅输入胸片与报告，RadAI先预测专家注视序列，将序列各时间区间与所追踪的诊断所见关联，提取对应图像区域，并以合成语音对每个高亮所见进行讲解，渲染为EMME式视频。

Result: 在EGD-CXR数据集97例上评估，检验高亮区域是否与标注的异常位置一致。

Conclusion: 将RadAI定位为面向放射科住院医师与医学生可扩展生成教学内容的框架。

Abstract: Chest radiograph interpretation errors often arise from failures in visual search: although trainees may correctly identify an abnormality once pointed out, they frequently fail to fixate on the relevant region, while expert search strategies (where radiologists look, in what sequence, and for how long) remain largely implicit. Eye movement modeling examples (EMME), which replay a recorded expert's gaze over the image, have been shown to improve novice detection of findings on chest radiographs, but producing one requires an eye-tracking session on that specific case, so teaching sets are limited to recorded sessions by experts. In this work, RadAI is presented, the first end-to-end framework for transforming predicted radiologist gaze into narrated instructional videos, enabling scalable gaze-guided training without collecting new eye-tracking data. Given only a chest X-ray and its report, RadAI predicts an expert fixation sequence, associates each interval of that sequence with the diagnostic finding it pursues, extracts the corresponding image region, and renders the result as an EMME-style video in which each highlighted finding is narrated with synthesized speech. RadAI is evaluated on 97 cases from the EGD-CXR dataset, assessing whether the highlighted regions correspond to annotated abnormality locations. We position this work as a framework for scalable generation of educational content for residents and medical students in the field of radiology.

</details>


### [7] [MAPA: Mode-Aware Prototype Allocation for Latent Dataset Distillation in 3D Medical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09956)
*Weiqi Zhang,Hiroyuki Kubo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAPA：一种面向3D医学分类的隐空间数据集蒸馏方法，通过将原型分散到类内各模态以缓解"原型集中"问题，在等原型数量下聚合平衡准确率较最强隐空间基线提升4.2个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全容积样本合成的内存与优化成本限制了数据集蒸馏在3D医学分类中的应用；在存储等预算下改用冻结自编码器隐空间蒸馏可容纳更多原型，但分布匹配等类级匹配会使原型聚集于稠密隐区域、低估稀疏类内区域（即原型集中），造成类内表示失衡。

Method: 提出MAPA（Mode-Aware Prototype Allocation）：对真实隐特征聚类得到固定锚点以标定类内模态，通过软分配将原型分散到各锚点上，并辅以轻量抗坍缩正则项与课程式加权以稳定优化，同时保留类级匹配。

Result: 在五个3D医学分类任务上，相同原型数量下MAPA的聚合平衡准确率较最强隐空间基线提升+4.2个点；在MosMedData上的探索性严重程度分析显示最差分层召回率提升且正常病例特异度未下降。

Conclusion: 将原型按类内模态分配可在不增加存储与原型预算的前提下提升隐空间数据集蒸馏的类内均衡性与少数/难分层表现，为3D医学分类的轻量蒸馏提供有效方案。

Abstract: Dataset distillation (DD) synthesizes a small training set from a large one, but applying it to 3D medical classification is constrained by the memory and optimization costs of synthesizing full volumetric samples. Under a storage-equivalent budget, distilling in the latent space of a frozen autoencoder reduces this cost and fits more synthetic samples (prototypes) than in voxel space. However, class-level matching, such as distribution matching, causes prototypes to concentrate in dense latent regions while underrepresenting sparser intra-class regions, a consistent imbalance that we term prototype concentration. To address this, we propose MAPA (Mode-Aware Prototype Allocation), a latent distillation method that spreads prototypes across intra-class modes while preserving class-level matching. MAPA marks these modes with fixed anchors estimated by clustering the real latents and distributes prototypes over them through a soft assignment, together with a lightweight anti-collapse regularizer and curriculum weighting that stabilize the optimization. Across five 3D medical classification tasks, MAPA improves the aggregate balanced accuracy by +4.2 points over the strongest latent baseline while using the same number of prototypes. An exploratory severity analysis on MosMedData suggests improved worst-stratum recall without loss of normal-case specificity.

</details>


### [8] [HALMES: Knowing When to Intervene in LVLM Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09957)
*Ruining Hu,Jiaqi Lu,Xiao Liu,Ying Shen,Yu Wang,Lin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HALMES模块，通过注意力特征识别幻觉前兆片段并按需触发对比解码，将全阶段无差别干预转为选择性定向纠正，在多种LVLM架构与基准上进一步提升幻觉缓解效果并减少对正常输出的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比解码作为免训练推理期缓解方法，在每个解码步都干预输出分布，这种全阶段无差别干预可能破坏原本正确的生成、甚至诱发新的幻觉。

Method: 首先分析LVLM内部注意力特征与幻觉token片段的关系，发现幻觉相关异常在先前token片段中已逐步显现；据此设计轻量即插即用模块HALMES，在生成过程中从注意力特征中识别幻觉前兆片段，并选择性触发对比解码，可与多种全阶段对比解码方法结合。

Result: 在不同LVLM架构和多个基准上的大量实验表明，HALMES在提升现有对比解码方法幻觉缓解性能的同时，降低了全阶段干预对正常输出的负面影响。

Conclusion: 对幻觉易发片段进行建模并选择性干预具有重要价值，验证了按需定向纠正优于全阶段无差别干预。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in multimodal tasks. Nevertheless, their outputs remain prone to object hallucinations that are inconsistent with visual content. Contrastive decoding, as a training-free inference-time mitigation approach, typically intervenes in the output distribution at every decoding step. However, such indiscriminate full-stage intervention may disrupt originally correct generations and even induce new hallucinations. To address this issue, we first analyze the relationship between internal attention features and hallucinated token segments in LVLMs, revealing that hallucination-related abnormalities already emerge progressively in preceding token segments. Based on this observation, we propose HALMES, a lightweight plug-and-play module that identifies hallucination precursor segments from attention features during generation and selectively triggers contrastive decoding. HALMES can be integrated with various full-stage contrastive decoding methods, transforming indiscriminate intervention into on-demand targeted correction. Extensive experiments across different LVLM architectures and multiple benchmarks show that HALMES further improves the hallucination mitigation performance of existing contrastive decoding methods while reducing the negative impact of full-stage intervention on normal outputs, validating the importance of modeling and selectively intervening on hallucination-prone segments.

</details>


### [9] [HippoMamba: A Mamba-augmented network for hippocampal segmentation and serial volumetry in Alzheimers disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09958)
*Dawa Chyophel Lepcha,Sophie A. Martin,Pierrick Coupe,Shabbir Syed-Abdul*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HippoMamba：3D CNN编解码器+Mamba选择性状态空间瓶颈模块，用于跨访次MRI海马分割，Dice最优，但与FreeSurfer存在系统偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海马萎缩是阿尔茨海默病最早的结构标志之一，可靠的跨访次MRI海马分割是纵向测量萎缩的前提；现有卷积/Transformer方法在长程建模或序列访次处理上存在不足。

Method: HippoMamba由3D卷积编码器与解码器构成，瓶颈处插入Mamba选择性状态空间块，可处理同一受试者的一个或多个MRI访次。在MSD海马数据集（260例，五折交叉验证）上与六种卷积及Transformer方法在相同折划分与patch尺寸下比较；再不经重训练将五折集成模型应用于OASIS-3队列（150名参与者、600个T1加权session），与FreeSurfer对比。

Result: MSD上平均Dice 0.8861±0.0094，为对比方法中最高，且逐受试者Dice显著优于各方法（Holm校正Wilcoxon，p≤0.013）；95%HD 1.45 mm、ASD 0.44 mm为最低，但与Attention U-Net差异不显著，HD与SegResNet亦不显著。OASIS-3中满足配准质量条件的session上，预测海马体积与FreeSurfer排序一致（r=0.778，95%CI 0.699–0.838；ICC(3,1)=0.772），但系统性偏小约27%；随访≥2年者的年化体积变化与FreeSurfer中度相关（r=0.503，95%CI 0.319–0.659），平均变化率偏大。

Conclusion: HippoMamba可用于海马分割及队列内体积排序，但绝对体积与变化率存在系统偏倚，须相对参考方法解释。

Abstract: Hippocampal atrophy is among the earliest structural markers of Alzheimer's disease, and reliable hippocampal segmentation across serial MRI visits is a prerequisite for measuring it. We present HippoMamba, a network that combines a 3D convolutional encoder and decoder with a Mamba selective state space block at the bottleneck, designed to process one or more MRI visits of a subject. On the Medical Segmentation Decathlon hippocampus dataset (260 subjects, five-fold cross-validation), HippoMamba obtained a mean Dice similarity coefficient of 0.8861 $\pm$ 0.0094, the highest among six convolutional and transformer-based methods trained with the same folds and patch size, and its per-subject Dice was significantly higher than that of each method (Holm-corrected Wilcoxon tests, $p \leq 0.013$). Its mean 95th percentile Hausdorff distance (1.45 mm) and average surface distance (0.44 mm) were the lowest of the compared methods, although they did not differ significantly from those of Attention U-Net, and the Hausdorff distance did not differ significantly from that of SegResNet. Without retraining, the five-fold ensemble was applied to 600 T1-weighted sessions from 150 participants of the OASIS-3 cohort. In sessions meeting a registration quality criterion, predicted hippocampal volumes ranked consistently with FreeSurfer volumes (Pearson r = 0.778, 95% CI 0.699 to 0.838; ICC(3,1) = 0.772) but were systematically smaller by about 27%. In a pre-specified analysis of participants followed for at least two years, annualised volume change derived from per-visit segmentation was moderately correlated with that of FreeSurfer (r = 0.503, 95% CI 0.319 to 0.659), with a larger mean rate. These results support the use of HippoMamba for hippocampal segmentation and for ranking hippocampal volume within a cohort, with absolute volumes and rates of change interpreted relative to the reference method.

</details>


### [10] [A four-view smartphone video dataset of a robot-held flexible strip, with camera poses and a 3D LaGSplat baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09959)
*Louen Pottier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建了一个由机械臂驱动柔性条带的多视角视频数据集（4台iPhone同步拍摄10分钟，含相机位姿与逐帧夹爪位置），并用LaGSplat在其上首次取得真实多视角数据上的3D重建结果（留出视角26–33 dB PSNR，支持浏览器实时交互演示）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LaGSplat将低维潜状态同时作为可学习耗散拉格朗日系统的广义坐标与高斯泼溅解码器的条件变量，其3D拟合需要同步多视角真实素材，而原论文仅有合成渲染数据，缺乏真实数据验证。

Method: 搭建真实采集场景：机械臂沿一维轨迹摆动柔性条带作为受控形变激励；3台固定+1台手持iPhone同步拍摄10分钟；完成视频同步、基于场景的相机位姿标定，并提供逐帧夹爪位置作为系统输入；以夹爪加速度作为强迫项训练LaGSplat。

Result: 在留出视角上新视角合成达到26–33 dB PSNR，并可在网页浏览器中以实时交互demo运行。

Conclusion: 该工作以技术报告形式公开了数据集、检查点与训练代码，为LaGSplat等潜动力学+高斯泼溅方法提供真实多视角受控形变基准，验证了从合成到真实场景的可迁移性。

Abstract: This note describes a multi-view video dataset of a deformable object driven by a measured input, with the measured pose of every camera, and a first 3D result obtained on it with LaGSplat. LaGSplat learns from video a low-dimensional latent state that is both the generalized coordinate of a learned dissipative Lagrangian and the conditioning variable of a Gaussian Splatting decoder; fitting the decoder in 3D calls for synchronized multi-view footage, which the original paper only had from synthetic renderings. Here a robot arm moves a flexible strip by oscillating along a one-dimensional path, and four iPhones, three fixed and one handheld, film it for ten minutes. The videos are synchronized, the cameras are posed from the scene, and the position of the gripper is given per frame as the input of the system. LaGSplat fitted on this data with the gripper acceleration as a forcing term renders held-out views at 26 to 33 dB PSNR and runs as a real-time interactive demo in a web browser. Dataset, checkpoints and training code are public.

</details>


### [11] [SceneShifter: Training-free Multi-Scene Temporal Control for Audio-driven Human Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09960)
*Jianzhi Long,Rong-Cheng Tu,Hao Guan,Siyuan Liang,Shunyu Liu,Xiao Luo,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneShifter：面向音频驱动人体动画的免训练多场景时序控制框架，通过引导自注意力实现帧级场景切换与前景一致性保持，并提出评测基准 SceneShifterBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动人体动画虽真实感强，但缺乏有效的多场景时序控制能力：难以在精确时间戳完成背景切换，同时保持前景主体连贯。作者指出该问题源于扩散Transformer的自注意力机制，需同时具备两项能力——语义感知的时序解耦（隔离跨场景背景注意力、保留跨场景前景注意力）与前景运动定位（在大幅运动下准确跟踪前景）。

Method: 提出免训练框架SceneShifter，引导时空自注意力：抑制背景token间的跨场景注意力，保留前景token的跨场景注意力。为在大幅运动下维持该引导，从最能捕捉主体轨迹的自注意力层与注意力头中提取动态前景掩码。同时构建SceneShifterBench基准，用于评测场景时间精度、前景保持与视觉质量（覆盖大运动、多主体情形）。

Result: 实验表明SceneShifter实现帧级场景时序控制、强主体保持性与高视觉质量，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过从注意力机制层面分析与干预，可在不重新训练的前提下解决音频驱动人体动画的多场景时序控制问题；注意力掩码驱动的解耦策略与相应基准为后续研究提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Although audio-driven human animation has achieved impressive realism, it still lacks effective Multi-Scene Temporal Control: orchestrating background transitions at precise timestamps while preserving a coherent foreground subject. We show that this challenge stems from the diffusion transformer's self-attention mechanism, and addressing it requires two capabilities: (1) Semantic-Aware Temporal Decoupling, which isolates background attention across scenes while retaining cross-scene foreground attention for subject consistency; and (2) Foreground Motion Localization, which accurately tracks the foreground subject across latent frames, especially under large motion. To address these challenges, we introduce SceneShifter, a training-free framework for precise multi-scene control in audio-driven human animation. SceneShifter guides spatio-temporal self-attention by suppressing cross-scene attention among background tokens while preserving cross-scene attention among foreground tokens. To support this guidance under large motion, SceneShifter extracts dynamic foreground masks from the self-attention layers and heads that best capture subject trajectories. We further introduce SceneShifterBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate scene-timing accuracy, foreground preservation, and visual quality under large motion, multiple subjects. Experiments show that SceneShifter achieves frame-level scene timing, strong subject preservation, and high visual quality, outperforming existing baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/JamesLong199/SceneShifter.

</details>


### [12] [How Many Demonstrations Does a VLA Need? On the Sample Efficiency of VLA Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09964)
*Rahul Chand,Miguel Villa Floran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究六个VLA模型在微调适应阶段的"样本效率"（演示数据量与训练步数），揭示全数据微调会掩盖的模型间差异，并分析冻结VLM这一预训练因素及验证损失与闭环成功率的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA模型零样本性能强，但实际部署前常在目标场景演示上做后训练（微调）。然而现有对比多以零样本或全数据微调成功率进行，无法反映"适应所需的数据量与计算量"，掩盖了模型间的适应效率差异。

Method: 在四个LIBERO套件上，对六个VLA（π0、π0.5及GR00T N1–N1.7）系统变动演示数量与训练步数，刻画适应效率曲线；进一步通过微调时冻结VLM，考察预训练方式（VLM是否与动作专家联合训练）对适应效率的影响；并比较留出演示上的验证损失与闭环成功率的一致性。

Result: 全数据微调下差距≤2分的模型，在仅50条演示时可相差达3倍成功率；π0.5所需演示比π0少2–5倍，在每个预算下样本效率最高，长程套件上差距最大；GR00T内部N1.5在四个套件中的三个上比N1.6/N1.7适应更高效，尽管后者零样本更强；冻结VLM严重损害π系列，却几乎不影响GR00T；验证损失在同一套件内可追踪闭环成功率，但低估π0.5相对π0的样本效率优势。

Conclusion: 仅用零样本或全数据微调成功率评估VLA会显著误导模型选择；应报告适应效率（数据-计算-性能曲线）。VLM与动作专家联合预训练是影响适应效率的关键因素，且验证损失不能替代样本效率评估。

Abstract: Pretrained vision language action (VLA) models show strong zero-shot performance, but are rarely deployed directly and are often first post-trained on demonstrations from the target setting. Yet VLAs are typically compared by zero-shot or full-data fine-tuned success, revealing little about the data and compute required for adaptation. We study adaptation efficiency for six VLAs ($π_{0}$, $π_{0.5}$, and GR00T N1-N1.7) on four LIBERO suites, varying both demonstrations and training steps. This reveals differences hidden by full-data fine-tuning: models within two points on the full LIBERO-Spatial dataset differ by up to $3\times$ in success with 50 demonstrations. $π_{0.5}$ needs $2$--$5\times$ fewer demonstrations than $π_{0}$ and is the most sample-efficient model at every budget, with the largest gap on the long-horizon suite. Within GR00T, N1.5 adapts more efficiently than N1.6 and N1.7 on three of four suites, despite their stronger zero-shot performance. We study one pretraining factor behind these differences by freezing the VLM during finetuning: this severely degrades the $π$ models, whose VLM is jointly trained with the action expert during pretraining, but barely affects GR00T models. Finally, validation loss on held-out demonstrations tracks closed-loop success within a suite, but understates how much more sample-efficient $π_{0.5}$ is than $π_{0}$.

</details>


### [13] [Clinically Informed Colour Encoding Improves the Localisation and Stability of Explanations in Deep Learning ECG Models: External Validation in a UK Biobank Epilepsy Cohort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09971)
*Lauren Hutchinson,Moses Nkgari,Alaa Alahmadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究证明输入表示（伪彩色编码）可同时决定心电深度学习模型的预测性能与解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深层模型对心电图的解释质量是否受输入表示支配尚不清楚；QT间期与T波形态难以目视评估，更难核验模型推理，需寻找可控的设计变量。

Method: 基于临床电生理知识设计填充QT间期下方面积的伪彩色编码，将其颜色序列映射到经验证的QT-nomogram风险模型；训练两个配置相同、仅伪彩色有无不同的残差网络（单心搏图像，临床试验数据），并在UK-Biobank癫痫队列两次访视上无微调、无阈值调整地外部验证，用八种解释方法比较忠实性、定位性与稳定性。

Result: 伪彩色模型首访与随访AUC为0.903和0.914，单色模型为0.719和0.720；在相当特异度下检出高风险心搏数为其3–5倍。伪彩色编码几乎改善所有解释方法的定位性与稳定性，且在两访视间可复现。

Conclusion: 输入表示是被忽视但可控的预测性能与解释质量决定因素；将可解释性视为表示设计问题，可为临床医生提供可对照已知电生理知识核验的解释路径。

Abstract: Objective: It is not known whether the input representation governs the quality of explanations of deep learning models for electrocardiogram analysis. Risk of dangerous ventricular repolarisation is judged from the QT-interval and the shape of the T-wave, features that are hard to assess visually and harder still to verify in a model's reasoning. We evaluate a pseudo-colour encoding filling the area beneath the QT interval, its colour sequence designed from clinical electrophysiology knowledge and mapped to a validated risk prediction model, the QT-nomogram. Methods: Two identically configured residual networks were trained on single-heartbeat images from a clinical trial, differing only in whether pseudo-colour encoding was applied, so that any difference is attributable to the chromatic channel alone. To externally validate the effect, both models were applied without fine-tuning or threshold tuning to a previously unseen epilepsy cohort from the UK-Biobank at two visits, and eight explanation methods compared on faithfulness, localisation and stability. Results: The pseudo-colour model achieved areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve of 0.903 and 0.914 at the first and follow-up visits, against 0.719 and 0.720 for the monochrome model, recovering three to five times as many at-risk heartbeats at comparable specificity. Pseudo-colour encoding improved explanation localisation and stability for almost every method, and the improvement reproduced across both visits. Conclusion: The input representation is a controllable and previously overlooked determinant of both predictive performance and explanation quality. Significance: Treating interpretability as a representation-design problem offers a route towards explanations clinicians can check against known electrophysiology.

</details>


### [14] [ReelCache: An Efficient KV Cache Compression and Management System for Multi-Round Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09973)
*Tianyu Wang,Daiyang Yu,Zhiwei Ren,Junbo Li,Jitian Zhang,Longfei Shangguan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReelCache 是面向多轮长视频自回归扩散生成的 KV 缓存压缩系统，结合层内低秩分解+视频编码压缩与层间树状缓存管理，实现 14.46–21.13× 存储压缩、1.25–3.36× 加速且 VBench 指标下降 <1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在多轮生成中累积大量 KV 缓存：全量保留内存开销过大，丢弃后重建又会严重拖慢后续视频块的生成，因此需要兼顾存储效率与生成效率的缓存压缩方案。

Method: 提出双组件设计：(1) 块内压缩器——沿通道维做低秩分解，再对分解系数施加视频编码，并在线逐 Transformer 层预测分解秩以满足保留能量目标；(2) 块间缓存管理器——在连续块之间共享基方向，并以树结构组织块状态，使不同生成分支共享历史状态，同时支持恢复、回退与分叉。

Result: 在五种视频生成模型上，相对基线将 KV 存储降低 14.46–21.13×，下一块生成加速 1.25–3.36×，六项代表性 VBench 指标下降不足 1%。

Conclusion: 将缓存压缩（低秩+编码）与缓存生命周期管理（跨块基共享+树状状态）联合设计，可在几乎无损质量的前提下大幅降低长视频生成的内存与延迟成本，并支持交互式续写/回退/分叉等编辑场景。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models produce large KV caches during multi-round video generation. Keeping all these KV chunks would consume substantial memory, while rebuilding discarded caches would significantly delay video chunk generation. This paper presents ReelCache, a KV cache compression system for efficient multi-round, long video generation. ReelCache combines an intra-chunk compressor with an inter-chunk cache manager. The compressor applies low-rank decomposition across channels and video coding to the resulting coefficients, predicting the decomposition rank per transformer layer online to meet retained-energy targets. The cache manager shares basis directions across consecutive chunks and organizes chunk states in a tree, allowing generation branches to share historical state while supporting restoration for resume, rewind, and fork. Experiments across five video generation models show that ReelCache reduces KV storage by 14.46-21.13x relative to baselines and accelerates next-chunk generation by 1.25-3.36x, with less than 1% drop in six representative VBench metrics. GitHub: https://github.com/Tianyu9748/ReelCache

</details>


### [15] [Seeing Like a Shopper: Validating Vision-Language Models as Synthetic Consumers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09977)
*Woongki Kim,Seungkook Baek,Minhyeok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文检验视觉语言模型(VLM)能否作为"合成购物者"复现真实消费者的购物行为,并提出ShelfSight基准与因子模型,将产品偏好与"答案效应"(字母A偏好、图像位置偏差)分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成受访者多为纯文本模型,而实体购物依赖视觉货架浏览;因此需要检验作为天然合成购物者的VLM能否在购买层面与单品货架选择层面复现真实消费者行为,并识别/校正VLM的视觉选择题偏差。

Method: (1)购买层面:用家庭记录构建VLM购物者,依据商店目录规划行程、从货架图像选品,与三国六个公开数据集对比;测试俄罗斯按性别/年龄的酒类购买、台湾按门店距离的行程规模,以及情境、价格、陈列响应。(2)单品层面:构建ShelfSight——独立变化产品、位置、标签、视角、价格的货架渲染,配合因子模型分离产品偏好与标签/位置引起的"答案效应",按图像类型一次性估计并剔除。

Result: 合成购物篮命中留出购买的比例与家庭自身历史行程相当;群体差异与真实差异的相关强度为训练回归的两倍。无需拟合即可复现俄罗斯按性别与年龄的酒类购买、台湾按门店距离的行程规模,并在实测区间内对价格、陈列与情境作出响应。ShelfSight上,托管VLM能读取货架、遵循明示需求、跨视角保持产品偏好,并像预算型消费者一样对价格作出反应;存在对字母A及图像位置的系统偏好。

Conclusion: VLM是良好的合成购物者;剔除答案效应后,其货架选择能反映产品偏好,可用于消费者研究与市场调研。

Abstract: Language models are widely used as synthetic respondents in surveys, behavioral experiments, and market research. Most of these respondents read text, whereas shoppers in a store choose by looking at shelves, which makes vision-language models (VLMs) natural synthetic shoppers. We test whether VLM shoppers follow real consumers at two levels. At the purchase level, VLM shoppers built from household records plan their trips from a store directory and select products from shelf images, and we compare their purchases with six public datasets from three countries. The synthetic baskets hit held-out purchases as often as the households' own past trips, and synthetic group differences correlate with real ones twice as strongly as those of a trained regression. Without fitting, VLM shoppers reproduce alcohol purchases by gender and age in Russia and trip sizes by store distance in Taiwan, and respond to situations, prices, and displays within measured ranges. At the level of a single shelf choice, a model's answer confounds the product with its position and letter label. We therefore introduce ShelfSight, shelf renderings that vary products, positions, labels, viewpoints, and prices independently, together with a factor model that separates product preferences from the effects of label and position, which we call answer effects. On ShelfSight, hosted VLMs read the shelf, follow stated needs, keep their product preferences across views, and respond to price as budget shoppers. These answer effects, a preference for the letter A and for positions in the image, are estimated once per image type and removed. VLMs are thus good synthetic shoppers whose corrected shelf choices reflect product preferences.

</details>


### [16] [Preconditioned Implicit Midpoint Langevin Sampling for Non-Smooth Bayesian Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09980)
*Sichen Wang,Zhipeng Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出P-IMLA：一种预条件隐式中点Langevin采样器，在高维对数凹且含非光滑正则的目标上实现任意步长下的高斯精确性与以κ_eff为刻度的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵预条件允许大步长，但预条件Euler-Maruyama恰在大步长处放大稳态方差；非光滑正则与病态条件数（κ_f）进一步导致长瞬态，需要同时解决方差膨胀与条件数依赖问题。

Method: 采用隐式中点离散，借Cayley变换恒等式消除稳态方差盈余，使采样器对高斯目标任意步长精确；通过白化将IMLA的收缩率转移至有效条件数κ_eff；用后向误差分析在显式光滑性假设下给出仅漂移型（而非Euler型扩散）偏差；并以M-范数Moreau包络构造近端非光滑变体，配三项误差预算。

Result: 受控高斯实验与TV正则化成像实验验证了理论预测的分离现象：非预条件中点格式随κ_f增大保留长瞬态，而P-IMLA保持κ_eff主导且方差近乎精确。

Conclusion: 预条件与隐式中点结合可在大步长下兼顾方差精确性与条件数鲁棒性，并经Moreau包络自然扩展至非光滑正则目标。

Abstract: We introduce P-IMLA, a preconditioned implicit-midpoint Langevin sampler for high-dimensional log-concave targets with non-smooth regularization. The obstruction is that the large steps enabled by matrix preconditioning are precisely where preconditioned Euler-Maruyama inflates stationary variance. Implicit midpoint removes this surplus via a Cayley-transform identity, making P-IMLA Gaussian-exact at any step size. Beyond Gaussians, whitening transfers the sharp IMLA contraction rate to $κ_{\mathrm{eff}}$, and backward-error analysis gives drift-only rather than Euler-type diffusion bias under explicit smoothness assumptions. An $M$-norm Moreau envelope yields a proximal non-smooth variant with a three-term error budget. Controlled Gaussian and TV-regularized imaging experiments confirm the predicted separation: the unpreconditioned midpoint scheme retains long transients as $κ_f$ grows, whereas P-IMLA remains $κ_{\mathrm{eff}}$-governed and near variance-exact.

</details>


### [17] [QCVS: Question-Contrastive Visual Steering for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09982)
*Tong Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QCVS 通过双差分激活探针分离任务特定干预方向，并用双阈值门控选择性施加有界隐状态更新，在稀疏覆盖下实现高精度、低副作用的推理时视觉引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型的推理时引导（inference-time steering）把与查询相关的推理动态和低层感知噪声纠缠在一起，导致对原本正确预测的连带性退化（collateral degradation）。

Method: 提出 Question-Contrastive Visual Steering：1）双差分激活探针——在目标查询下对比空间布局扰动与通用描述参照，抵消任务无关的视觉伪影，无需真值监督；2）双阈值门控机制——同时评估相对交互强度与信号幅度，仅在必要时施加单次有界隐状态更新。

Result: 在 Qwen3.5（2B–9B）多尺寸与 DeepSeek-VL2 上，于 DynaMath 与 We-Math 基准取得 SOTA 准确率提升，覆盖稀疏（约22%–30%）；修复率最高 13.98%，连带损伤降至 0.41%。

Conclusion: QCVS 表明以对比方式隔离任务特定方向并结合门控式最小干预，可在保持计算效率的同时实现高精度引导，显著缓解推理时干预的副作用。

Abstract: Inference-time steering in vision-language models often entangles query-relevant reasoning dynamics with low-level perceptual noise, causing collateral degradation on correct predictions. We propose Question-Contrastive Visual Steering (QCVS) to isolate task-specific intervention directions via double-differential activation probing. By contrasting spatial layout perturbations under the target query against a generic description reference, QCVS cancels task-agnostic visual artifacts without ground-truth supervision. A dual-threshold gating mechanism then evaluates relative interaction strength and signal amplitude, selectively applying a single bounded hidden-state update only when necessary. Empirical evaluations across multiple scales of Qwen3.5 (2B to 9B) and DeepSeek-VL2 on DynaMath and We-Math demonstrate that QCVS achieves state-of-the-art accuracy gains under sparse coverage (approximately 22%-30%). Crucially, QCVS yields repair rates up to 13.98% while suppressing collateral damage down to 0.41%, proving high intervention precision and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [18] [Stochastic Deep Supervision for Gallery-Consistent Early-Exit Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09987)
*Guray Ozgur,Tahar Chettaoui,Eduarda Caldeira,Naser Damer,Fadi Boutros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SD-ViT训练框架，通过随机采样的中间层辅助监督，使ViT在每个深度都能产生可用于人脸识别的判别性嵌入，从而支持按预算选择的固定深度早退且无需重算参考库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT在人脸识别中表现强，但固定深度推理在资源受限场景难以部署。标准ViT仅靠反向传播间接监督中间层，中间表示并未被直接优化为可用的人脸身份嵌入，因此早退输出无法直接与全深度特征比对。

Method: SD-ViT在每次迭代随机选择一个中间block，将其输出送入与最后一层相同的embedding head，并以加权margin-penalty softmax损失训练；由于中间层与最终层共享head与分类器，两者损失共同作用，使中间token被映射到与全深度token相同的嵌入空间。本质上是通过共享分类器实现的随机采样深度监督。

Result: 在公开基准上，全深度性能与常规训练的同一backbone相差不超过0.4个IJB-C点；早退探针在50%计算量下可恢复自身全深度性能的96%以上，且无需修改参考库；全深度标定的单一验证阈值从四分之一深度起即可直接沿用。

Conclusion: 共享head的随机深度监督可使ViT各深度输出处于同一嵌入空间，实现即插即用的固定深度早退，兼顾精度与部署灵活性；人脸识别中的相似度匹配可直接跨深度进行，无需库重算或适配层。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) perform strongly in face recognition (FR), yet their fixed-depth inference limits deployment in resource-constrained settings. We propose SD-ViT, a training framework that gives a usable exit at every block depth by making every depth produce discriminative embeddings, for budget-selected fixed-depth exits. Standard ViTs supervise intermediate layers only through backpropagation, so their intermediate representations are not directly optimized to form usable identity embeddings. SD-ViT adds a stochastic auxiliary signal: at each iteration a randomly selected intermediate block is passed through the same embedding head as the final layer and trained with a weighted margin-penalty softmax loss. The objective is thus stochastically sampled deep supervision through a shared classifier. Because both loss terms share that head and classifier, intermediate tokens are trained to map into the same embedding space as final-layer tokens. This matters for FR, where matching is by similarity: a probe processed at an early exit can be compared directly against full-depth gallery embeddings, with no gallery recomputation or adaptation layers. On established benchmarks, SD-ViT stays within 0.4 IJB-C points of the same backbone trained conventionally at full depth, while its early-exit probes recover over 96% of their own full-depth performance at 50% of the computational cost without modifying the reference gallery. A single verification threshold calibrated at full depth remains usable from quarter depth onward.

</details>


### [19] [Retrieval-Grounded Multi-View Reasoning for Clinically Reliable Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09993)
*Wanshu Fan,Zeyu Wang,Zheng Tong,Qi Ai,Wencai Weng,Haifan Gong,Congyu Liao,Guoliang You,Xiaofeng Liu,Dongsheng Zhou,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出检索接地的胸片报告生成框架RG-MVR，通过证据接地、自适应多视角证据推理与临床提示学习缓解检索报告临床不一致和诊断标签失衡问题；MIMIC-CXR上临床标签F1为0.483。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片报告生成存在两个瓶颈：检索到的报告虽与当前图像相似，却在临床上与之不一致；同时诊断标签存在类别不平衡。这两点导致生成报告可信度不足。

Method: RG-MVR包含三个模块：(1) 检索增强证据接地（RAEG），将稠密记忆摘要与视觉门控的稀疏检索证据相结合；(2) 自适应多视角证据推理（AMER），按病例对图像、记忆与检索证据进行加权；(3) 临床提示学习器（CPL），将发现提及与其阳性/阴性/不确定状态分离建模。

Result: MIMIC-CXR上临床标签F1 0.483、BLEU-4 0.118；IU X-ray上分别为0.202与0.110。在22例检查的探索性外部队列中，临床标签一致性显著下降（F1 0.071），无依据陈述仍较常见，但医师判定的无依据时间比较（9 vs 20例）与器械相关陈述（7 vs 15例）少于基线。

Conclusion: 证据感知检索与结构化诊断提示有望减少特定类型的报告错误，但模型的跨中心泛化能力有限，仍需更广泛的外部验证。

Abstract: Chest radiograph report generation is hampered by retrieved reports that resemble the current image but are clinically inconsistent with it, and by diagnostic-label imbalance. We present RG-MVR, a retrieval-grounded framework comprising Retrieval-Augmented Evidence Grounding, which combines dense memory summaries with visually gated sparse retrieved evidence; Adaptive Multi-view Evidential Reasoning, which weights image, memory, and retrieved evidence per case; and a Clinical Prompt Learner, which models finding mention separately from positive, negative, or uncertain status. On MIMIC-CXR, RG-MVR achieved a clinical-label F1 of 0.483 and a BLEU-4 of 0.118 (IU X-ray: 0.202 and 0.110). In an exploratory external cohort of 22 examinations, clinical-label agreement declined markedly (F1, 0.071) and unsupported statements remained common, although physicians found fewer unsupported temporal comparisons (9 vs. 20 cases) and device-related statements (7 vs. 15) than with the baseline. Evidence-aware retrieval and structured diagnostic prompting may reduce specific reporting errors, but broader external validation is required.

</details>


### [20] [SurvAD: How Anomalies Survive Diffusion Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09997)
*Lexing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示扩散式异常检测中异常"泄漏"(leak)遵循精确的递归与连续生存律，并据此提出SurvAD方法提升异常定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式异常检测器在将测试图像重建为正常图像时，异常残留（泄漏）的程度及其规律尚未被刻 画，缺乏对采样过程与异常幸存量之间关系的理论理解。

Method: 1) 证明一阶确定性采样器沿异常方向服从精确递归，其连续极限为生存律：异常随噪声下降以由保留率r决定的指数速率衰减，且r仅依赖噪声水平而非步数。2) 基于该定律推出泄漏随步数增长源于暴露偏差（exposure bias），正常细节亦服从同一规律。3) 提出SurvAD：在类推理起点上微调重建器，并训练比较器为残差图补充证据而不破坏其余区域排序。

Result: 单一实测路径可在相近起点下预测其他步数、采样器与噪声调度的泄漏，误差在百分之几以内，于四个扩散模型与真实缺陷上验证。SurvAD在推理时间增加不到2%的情况下，将DeCo-Diff的多类像素平均精度(P-AP)从MVTec AD的76.8提升至80.7、VisA的57.5提升至60.0；以少9倍可训练参数匹配CostFilter-AD；在Dinomaly协议下于两个基准上均超过其报告的P-AP。

Conclusion: 异常泄漏可用保留率驱动的生存律统一刻画，改变采样路径或训练方式实质是在泄漏与细节误差之间权衡；SurvAD通过类推理起点微调与比较器增强，以极低开销显著提升像素级异常定位精度。

Abstract: Diffusion-based anomaly detectors reconstruct a test image towards normality, yet how much of an anomaly survives, its leak, remains uncharacterized. We show that a first-order deterministic sampler obeys an exact recursion along the anomaly, whose continuous limit is a survival law. The anomaly decays exponentially as the noise falls, at a rate set by the retention $r$, the share the model keeps on the states it visits. Because $r$ depends on the noise level and not on the step count, one measured path predicts the leak of other step counts, samplers and schedules from a nearby start to within a few hundredths, on four diffusion models and real defects. The law also shows that the leak's growth over steps is exposure bias and that normal details obey the same law, so changing the path or the training trades leak for detail error. We introduce SurvAD, which fine-tunes the reconstructor on inference-like starts and fits a comparator that adds evidence to the residual map without suppressing its ranking elsewhere. For under $2\%$ more inference time, SurvAD raises the multi-class pixel average precision (P-AP) of DeCo-Diff from $76.8$ to $80.7$ on MVTec AD and from $57.5$ to $60.0$ on VisA. It matches CostFilter-AD on the same reconstructor with $9\times$ fewer trainable parameters and, under Dinomaly's protocol, exceeds its reported P-AP on Dinomaly on both benchmarks.

</details>


### [21] [Quality-Governed Deep Learning Network for Brain Tumor MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09998)
*Asifiqbal Saiyed,Nitesh Chilakala,Jimmy Joseph,Praneetha Goli,Rohini Maskar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出质量门控深度学习网络QGDL-Net，利用切片质量统计对编码器特征进行门控微调，用于FLAIR MRI二分类肿瘤分割，在BraTS 2021上Dice达0.9747并优于U-Net与Attention U-Net。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI脑肿瘤分割是计算机辅助神经肿瘤学、治疗规划与随访的关键步骤。U-Net与Attention U-Net的跳跃连接在肿瘤边界异质、对比度弱或噪声大时会产生弱响应或噪声响应，影响分割精度。

Method: 构建QGDL-Net：基于对比度与信噪比（SNR）统计预测切片质量，并借助可学习的质量门（quality gates）在解码前对编码器特征进行微调；采用80:20训练-验证划分，在BraTS 2021的FLAIR切片上做二分类分割。

Result: Dice 0.9747、IoU 0.9506、精确率0.9755、召回率0.9739、F1 0.9747、准确率0.9982，均优于U-Net与Attention U-Net；EigenCAM热力图与分割叠加结果显示更好的肿瘤定位与可解释的病灶区激活。

Conclusion: 以切片质量驱动的门控特征调制可有效抑制跳跃连接中的弱对比与噪声响应，提升分割性能并提供可解释性。

Abstract: The segmentation of brain tumors from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) is an essential step for computer-aided neuro-oncology, treatment planning and follow-up. While effective encoder-decoder representations are achieved by U-Net and Attention U-Net, the skip sections of the network can generate weak-contrast or noisy responses in the case of heterogeneous boundaries of the tumor. In this paper, we introduce a Quality-Governed Deep Learning Network (QGDL-Net) that can be used for binary tumor segmentation from FLAIR MRI slices. The proposed model is able to predict the slice quality based on contrast and SNR statistics and to fine-tune the encoder features prior to decoding by learnable quality gates. We used the 80:20 train-validation split for experiments with BraTS 2021. QGDL-Net achieved a Dice of 0.9747, IoU of 0.9506, precision of 0.9755, recall of 0.9739, F1 of 0.9747 and accuracy of 0.9982, outperforming U-Net and Attention U-Net. The EigenCAM maps and segmentation overlays provide better tumor localization and interpretable activation around the lesion regions.

</details>


### [22] [Optimized Car Damage Detection Using CNN and Object Detection Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10001)
*Sania Ejaz,Jaison Shajan Thomas,Zaheeruddin Ahmed,Sushma Hans*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双阶段车辆损伤检测架构：先用CNN筛选完好/受损图像，再用目标检测定位并分类划痕、凹陷、玻璃碎裂、撞毁四类缺陷，采用迁移学习与统一模型以适配多场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆损伤检测在保险理赔、事故上报、车库进出入记录等场景中具有重要价值，作者希望以统一模型配合迁移学习覆盖多种用例，突破相关工作中分工化建模的局限。

Method: 两阶段流水线：第一阶段CNN二分类（完好/受损）过滤无损伤图像；第二阶段目标检测层对损伤做定位与四分类；均采用迁移学习。

Result: CNN分类准确率98%；目标检测层mAP约0.5，作者指出该指标因文中讨论的原因低估了实际性能。

Conclusion: 级联两模型可精简输入处理流程并提升整体效率；但目标检测评价指标存在偏差，需结合定性结果补充评估。

Abstract: Car damage detection systems play a vital role in scenarios, like the insurance claims process, accident reporting systems, or a documented write-up of the various damages a vehicle in a car garage exhibits upon entry and exit. This paper presents a two-fold model architecture encompassing a convolutional neural network used to classify the collected input samples as clear or damaged, thereby filtering out the undamaged images, before the input is passed on to the object detection layer for further localization and classification of defects into Scratch, Dent, Glass Shatter, and Smash. A deviation from the focus of related work, this paper poses the use of unified models while applying transfer learning techniques to augment performance that caters to multiple use cases. The design of this model has been made to optimize performance by incorporating the two models to streamline the processing of input samples. The model boasts significant performance with accuracies of 98% for the CNN layer and 0.5 mAP for the object detection layer. However, the metric for the object detection layer seems to understate its performance due to reasons discussed within the paper.

</details>


### [23] [Reliability-Aware Benchmarking of Self-Supervised Learning in Histopathology Under 1-10% Label Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10003)
*Alicem Koyun,Pranjali Changadeo Pingale,Sachin Mote,Gianluca Pizzuti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在PCam与PANDA上以1%/5%/10%低标注比例（冻结编码器线性探针）对比SimCLR、BYOL与MAE式掩码重建基线，除AUROC/Acc/F1外引入ECE、Brier、OOD置信度诊断、联邦vs集中式及跨域迁移，并进行多划分×多随机种子重复（n=9/n=3）与均值±标准差报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理专家标注成本高，低标注场景普遍；自监督学习可从无标注图像学可迁移表征，但仅看性能指标的评估会掩盖域偏移下置信度不可靠的问题，因此需要可靠性感知的基准。

Method: 在PCam（96×96）与PANDA（前列腺癌）上，于1%、5%、10%标注比例做冻结编码器线性探针；对比SimCLR、BYOL与MAE式掩码重建基线；ResNet-18用3划分×3种子（n=9），ViT-B/16因算力限制用1划分×3种子（n=3）；报告均值±标准差与逐次运行日志；指标含AUROC、Accuracy、F1、ECE、Brier score，并进行PCam↔PANDA跨数据集OOD置信度诊断、PCam上联邦与集中式SimCLR对比、以及向结直肠病理切片的跨域迁移。

Result: 在较可控的PCam任务上SSL提升标注效率；PANDA的patch级代理标签噪声更大，性能可接近随机水平（near coin-flip）且方差更高。

Conclusion: 病理SSL评估不应只看排序性能，还须考察校准性、鲁棒性与可复现诊断信息，方可进入临床解读。

Abstract: Expert annotation in digital pathology is expensive, and low-label regimes remain common in realistic histopathology workflows. Self-supervised learning (SSL) offers a practical way to learn transferable representations from unlabeled pathology images, but performance-only evaluation can hide unreliable confidence behavior under domain shift. We present a reliability-aware benchmark of SimCLR, BYOL, and an MAE-inspired masked-reconstruction baseline on PatchCamelyon (PCam; 96x96) and PANDA (prostate cancer) under 1%, 5%, and 10% labeled regimes with frozen-encoder linear probing. To address common single-run concerns, ResNet-18 results are repeated over 3 splits x 3 seeds (n=9) and ViT-B/16 over 1 split x 3 seeds (n=3) under practical compute constraints, with mean+/-std reporting and per-run logs. Beyond AUROC, accuracy, and F1-score, we report Expected Calibration Error and Brier score, OOD confidence diagnostics for cross-dataset PCam<->PANDA scoring, federated-vs-centralized SimCLR comparisons on PCam, and cross-dataset transfer to colorectal histology tiles. SSL improves label efficiency on the more controlled PCam task, while PANDA's noisier patch-level proxy can remain near coin-flip with higher variance. The study highlights that histopathology SSL should be assessed not only by ranking performance but also by calibration, robustness, and transparent reproducibility diagnostics before clinical interpretation.

</details>


### [24] [MATA: Motion-aligned Temporal Adapter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10004)
*Ashish Kumar Singh,Prakash Chandra Chhipa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出运动对齐时序适配器MATA，在冻结的CLIP骨干上仅用约3.6M可调参数实现少样本细粒度视频动作识别的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本细粒度视频动作识别中，视觉相似的动作常共享人物、场景、姿态与物体，仅靠运动变化的顺序、时序和短暂持续时间区分；同时需满足可调参数少、计算成本低、仅用视频模态三项实际约束。

Method: 在冻结CLIP骨干上构建参数高效适配器MATA，沿时间维度特化特征，包含两部分：(1) 通过可学习时序位置嵌入实现时序相位对齐；(2) 通过深度可分离卷积实现局部运动转移算子。

Result: 仅约3.6M可调参数即在多个细粒度基准上达到SOTA，并对视觉损坏与帧丢弃引起的时序扰动保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 以轻量的时序对齐与局部运动建模即可在冻结大模型上高效解决细粒度动作识别，兼顾性能、效率与鲁棒性；代码已开源。

Abstract: Few-shot fine-grained video action recognition requires recognizing novel action classes from only a few labeled examples. The difficulty is that visually similar actions often share actors, scenes, poses, and objects, differing only in the order, timing, and short duration of motion changes. We study this problem under three practical constraints: few tunable parameters, low compute cost, and use of the video modality alone. We propose Motion-aligned Temporal Adapter (MATA), a parameter-efficient adapter on top of a frozen CLIP backbone. MATA specializes CLIP features along the temporal dimension using two components: temporal phase alignment through learnable temporal positional embeddings, and a local motion transition operator through depth-wise convolution. With only ~3.6M tunable parameters, MATA achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple fine-grained benchmarks while remaining robust to both visual corruptions and temporal disruptions induced by frame dropping. Code is available at https://github.com/prakashchhipa/MATA-Motion-aligned-Temporal-Adapter

</details>


### [25] [EarthJev: Efficient Visual Evidence Acquisition and Decision for UHR Remote Sensing Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10005)
*Pengyu Dai,Yao Zhang,Jian Song,Junjue Wang,Yafei Ou,Yuzeng Chen,Junshi Xia,Hongruixuan Chen,Naoto Yokoya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EarthJev 框架，将区域评分与答案生成解耦，在冻结作答模型前提下用目标特定的决策信号实现低成本主动观察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高分辨率遥感 VQA 需在有限视觉预算下定位细粒度证据；现有主动观察方法依赖反复生成式判断导致区域搜索代价高，且其决策混淆了三类不同目标（空间指针有效性、证据存在性、推理正确性）。

Method: 1) 空间指针验证器利用视觉与几何线索判断是否引导探索或跳过搜索；2) 证据感知决策机制，在冻结表示上以轻量头批量打分紧凑候选视图，不生成文本式区域判断；3) 按格式的置信度校准，用于在基于记忆与基于概览呈现所得证据的候选答案间做选择。作答模型全程冻结。

Result: 五个基准上，Qwen3.5-9B 平均准确率 53.06%，较同作答模型的 ZoomSearch 每基准高 2.33–14.73 个百分点，平均延迟由 11.30 秒降至 1.67 秒/题；另在四个冻结作答模型上，模型特定拟合决策模块与校准映射带来 3.41–6.53 个百分点平均提升。

Conclusion: 将区域评分与答案生成分离、并对不同预测目标使用专门决策信号，可在不微调作答模型的情况下同时提升 UHR 遥感 VQA 的精度与效率，且具备跨模型可迁移性。

Abstract: Ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote sensing visual question answering (VQA) requires locating fine-grained evidence in large scenes under limited visual input budgets. Active observation addresses this challenge through selective exploration, but repeated generative judgments can make region search costly. Its decisions also concern distinct prediction targets: spatial pointer validity, evidence presence, and reasoning correctness. We propose EarthJev, a framework that separates region scoring from answer generation and uses target-specific decision signals while keeping the answering model frozen. A spatial pointer verifier uses visual and geometric cues to guide exploration or bypass search. An evidence-aware decision mechanism scores compact candidate views in batches using a lightweight head on frozen representations, without generating textual region judgments. Format-specific confidence calibration then guides selection between candidate answers generated from memory and overview presentations of the acquired evidence. Across five benchmarks, EarthJev achieves a mean accuracy of 53.06% with Qwen3.5-9B, outperforming ZoomSearch with the same answering model by 2.33 to 14.73 percentage points per benchmark while reducing mean latency from 11.30 - 1.67 seconds per question. With four additional frozen answering models, model-specific fitting of the decision modules and calibration mappings yields mean accuracy gains of 3.41 - 6.53 percentage points over the corresponding single-pass baselines. Code is available at https://github.com/Da1daidaidai/EarthJev

</details>


### [26] [Representation Learning for 3D Brain Imaging: A Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10007)
*Pierre Falconnier,Robin Trombetta,Nicolas Duchateau,Carole Lartizien*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了18个视觉编码器（8个自然图像监督/自监督预训练模型如ResNet、DINO、CLIP，10个医学图像训练的骨干网络如RadImageNet、BiomedCLIP、SimCLR），在3D脑MRI下游任务（年龄、性别预测及ADNI/PPMI疾病分类）中评估其表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然2D RGB图像与医学图像存在巨大域差距，直接迁移计算机视觉模型到临床受限；自监督预训练与基础模型虽有潜力，但在医学影像上面临3D体数据训练策略适配和缺乏标准化评测基准两大挑战。

Method: 构建统一基准，选取8个自然图像预训练模型与10个医学图像预训练骨干网络，冻结编码器提取潜表征，在ADNI与PPMI数据集的年龄预测、性别预测和疾病分类任务上进行线性/下游评估，代码开源。

Result: 在自然2D图像上预训练的编码器表现最佳，甚至优于在超过10万例脑MRI体数据上预训练的模型。

Conclusion: 当前3D医学影像的表征学习方法仍不够适配与鲁棒，亟需针对体数据设计更合适的表征学习策略；标准化基准对揭示域差距问题具有重要价值。

Abstract: Many deep learning architectures for computer vision tasks rely on features extracted by a pre-trained encoder on large databases of natural 2D RGB images. However, the wide domain gap with medical images hampers the direct transfer of optimized deep learning models from computer vision to clinical applications. Recently, self-supervised pre-training and foundation models emerged as promising alternatives to mitigate these limitations. Yet, constructing such models for medical imaging faces critical challenges, including designing and adapting training strategies for large 3D volume datasets, and the need for standardized benchmarks to evaluate feature extraction performance. In this study, we propose an extensive comparison of vision encoders with a focus on 3D brain MRI. First, we consider 8 fully and self-supervised models pretrained on natural images (e.g. ResNet, DINO, CLIP) and 10 backbone architectures trained on medical images, either based on supervised (e.g. RadImageNet) or self-supervised (e.g. BiomedCLIP, SimCLR) tasks. All pre-trained encoders are evaluated on various downstream tasks to assess the representation power of their latent representations, including age and sex prediction as well as disease classification on the ADNI and PPMI datasets. Our study shows that 2D encoders pre-trained on natural images perform the most favorably, even compared to models pre-trained on more than 100k brain MRI volumes, highlighting the need for more suitable and robust methods for representation learning in 3D medical imaging. The source code for this study is publicly available at: https: //github.com/PierreFalconnier/brain-representation-benchmark.

</details>


### [27] [From Video to Digital Twin: An Exploratory Study of World Models and Gaussian Splatting on In-the-Wild Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10013)
*Somesh Mohapatra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以一段公开的、未经整理的新闻式食品仓库参观视频为唯一输入，实证评估生成式世界模型与3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）在真实场景中的表现，并将结果作为数字孪生的几何基础，输出为OpenUSD资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型与3DGS宣称能把普通视频转为可探索的三维场景，但它们在"未整理的真实world footage"上的表现鲜有报告；作者希望检验在无图纸、无扫描、无现场访问条件下，这些方法究竟能恢复什么。

Method: 商业世界模型输入20帧，恢复三个区域的相机位姿与度量深度，融合为约747,000个高斯splat（成本200 credits，约0.2美元）；另一路线在CPU上本地优化splat模型，分别用64帧与43帧训练，并做8帧（世界模型可接受帧数）对比实验；评估在未被人物遮挡的像素上计算PSNR；消融去除训练器的背景填充与人体凸包（person-hull）扣减；尝试让世界模型外推视频未拍摄区域；并描述将世界模型输出修正为合规、度量化的OpenUSD资产（原生splat schema）所需的步骤，作为数据层、浏览器查看器、Blender与NVIDIA Omniverse的唯一接口。

Result: CPU本地优化的splat模型在被拍摄路径附近达到照片级真实，但离开路径迅速退化；在留出帧上，64帧与43帧训练分别达到21.2 dB与23.1 dB PSNR，仅用8帧时分别下降3.8 dB与5.4 dB。去除背景填充与人体凸包扣减反而提高了留出PSNR，说明清理运动人物背后的空间会牺牲可观测像素的保真度，其收益存在于无真值区域。世界模型生成未拍摄区域视图两次在服务端失败，外推无法评估，未观测空间改由脚本化模型填充。

Conclusion: 现有模型能以低成本、度量尺度恢复真实场景的被观测部分（约20美分），但少视角保真度与未观测空间外推仍是开放问题；该工作是方法探索而非已部署产品。

Abstract: Generative world models and 3D Gaussian Splatting promise to turn ordinary video into explorable 3D scenes, but little is reported on how they behave on uncurated, in-the-wild footage. We study what these models recover when the only input is a publicly available news walkthrough of a food bank warehouse, with presenters walking through every shot and no drawings, scans or site visit, and we use the result as the geometry of a digital twin. A commercial world model given 20 frames recovered camera poses and metric depth for three areas, which we fused into about 747,000 Gaussian splats for 200 credits (20 US cents). Splat models optimized locally on a CPU, at no credit cost, were photorealistic near the filmed path but degraded quickly away from it. On held-out frames, scored in pixels not covered by people, they reached 21.2 and 23.1 dB PSNR from 64 and 43 training frames, and lost 3.8 and 5.4 dB when given only the 8 frames a world model accepts. Removing the trainer's background fill and person-hull carving raised held-out PSNR, showing that cleaning the space behind moving people costs fidelity in observed pixels while its gain lies where no ground truth exists. Asking the world model to generate views of areas the video never showed failed on the provider's side twice, so extrapolation could not be evaluated and unseen space was filled by a scripted model instead. We also describe the corrections needed to turn world-model output into a valid, metric Universal Scene Description (OpenUSD) asset with its native splat schema, which serves as the only interface to a data layer, a browser viewer, Blender and NVIDIA Omniverse. The findings suggest that today's models recover the observed parts of a real scene cheaply and at metric scale, while few-view fidelity and extrapolating unobserved space remain open. The work is an exploration of these methods, not a deployed product.

</details>


### [28] [Nanopath: Fast, Fair, Open Experimentation for Pathology Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10014)
*Paul S. Scotti,Hassan Muhammad,Ryan Kim,Nima Ashjaee,Ratna Sagari Grandhi,Saman Rahbar,Raymond Biju,Anish Dulal,Chao Feng,Connor Lane,Benjamin Warner,Tanishq Mathew Abraham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Nanopath 是一个开源框架与基准，可在单张 H100 上约 1 小时训练、25 分钟内评估百万级 TCGA 病理切片的轻量病理基础模型，并提供固定数据/算力预算与固定下游协议的公平比较机制及其公共实验账本 Labless。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型研究受限于大规模数据与算力，难以快速、低成本、公平地迭代想法；需要一个可复现、可直接对比的小规模试验台。

Method: 构建开源代码库与数据自动下载流程；约束提交的固定数据与算力预算，下游评估协议固定；使用单张 H100 在小模型（约22M参数）上训练并在切片级/WSI级任务上快速评估；通过公共实验账本 Labless 记录与检索实验；并将快速评估结果与 THUNDER、HEST、PathoBench (CPTAC) 标准化基准对齐验证（6个 nanopath 模型与14个参考模型）。

Result: 上线以来（2026年6月起）收到来自20位贡献者的1,269次实验；快速评估套件与三大标准化基准高度一致；部分22M参数模型在部分下游任务上超越1.1B参数大模型；并据此进一步优化评估套件使其更贴近外部基准。

Conclusion: Nanopath 为学生、研究者与AI智能体提供了低成本、公平、透明、可直接比较的病理基础建模试验台，可作为小规模探索与后续大规模验证的前置筛选工具。

Abstract: We introduce nanopath, an open-source framework and benchmark for rapidly training and evaluating small-scale pathology foundation models. Using a single H100 GPU, a typical nanopath model trains in a little over an hour on up to one million TCGA pathology tile presentations and evaluates within 25 minutes on a broad suite of tile-level and slide-level tasks. Nanopath enables rapid exploration of research ideas at small scale, so promising training recipes can subsequently be tested with more data and compute. To enable fair comparisons between ideas, we constrain submissions to fixed data and compute budgets while keeping the downstream evaluation protocol fixed. Anyone may clone the codebase, automatically download the data, reproduce or modify existing models, and submit a run to Labless, our public experiment ledger where researchers and AI agents can search through prior attempts. Since its public release in June 2026, nanopath has received 1,269 experiments from 20 contributors. Here, we detail its design and assess how well its rapid evaluation tracks standardized THUNDER, HEST, and PathoBench (CPTAC) benchmarks across six nanopath and 14 reference foundation models. Nanopath's fast evaluation suite strongly tracked these standardized benchmarks, and a few 22M parameter nanopath models even outperformed giant 1.1B parameter models on a subset of downstream tasks. We subsequently discuss how we have since optimized nanopath's evaluation suite to be an even more faithful, quick proxy for external benchmarking. Nanopath provides an accessible testbed for students, researchers, and AI agents to explore pathology foundation modeling through fair, transparent, and directly comparable experiments.

</details>


### [29] [Beyond Hash Matching: AI-Based Detection of Previously Unidentified CSAM and its Legal Implications in the EU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10028)
*Emmanouela Kokolaki,Georgios Samaritakis,Paraskevi Fragopoulou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文综述并分类可补充哈希匹配的AI技术，涵盖实时音视频分析、图像视频分类标注与行为监测三类，并通过Google Cloud Vision AI与Sightengine的实验评估性内容分类与年龄估计能力，结合欧盟AI法案与GDPR讨论合规与人工监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统哈希匹配技术只能识别已确认的儿童性虐待材料（CSAM），对未识别的新内容检测能力有限，执法机构、在线服务提供商与儿童保护组织亟需可补充的技术手段。

Method: 文献综述与技术分类（实时图像视频分析、图像视频分类与标注、AI行为监测）；对通用计算机视觉平台、内容审核服务与专用CSAM检测工具进行比较分析；使用Google Cloud Vision AI与Sightengine开展性内容分类与儿童—成人年龄估计的实验评估；并分析欧盟AI法案、GDPR及基本权利、透明度、问责与人工监督等监管要求。

Result: 不同提供商在分类体系与输出上存在差异；年龄分类存在显著局限，尤其对青少年群体；监管义务与保障措施随部署场景而异，可能在互联的检测与上报流程中造成不一致。

Conclusion: AI评估应作为分析与优先级排序的补充工具，而非CSAM的最终判定依据；需重视提供商间分类体系差异、年龄估计可靠性不足以及跨场景监管一致性等问题。

Abstract: The detection of previously unidentified Child Sexual Abuse Material (CSAM) remains a significant challenge for law enforcement authorities, online service providers, and child protection organizations, as traditional hash-matching technologies are primarily designed to identify previously verified material. This paper reviews and classifies existing AI-based technologies that could complement hash-based approaches and support the detection, analysis, and prioritization of potentially harmful content. The reviewed approaches are organized into three categories: real-time image and video analysis, image and video classification and tagging, and AI-based behavioral monitoring. A comparative analysis examines the capabilities, limitations, and potential integration of general-purpose computer vision platforms, content moderation services, and specialized CSAM detection tools within existing detection workflows. An experimental evaluation was conducted using Google Cloud Vision AI and Sightengine to investigate sexual-content classification and child-adult age estimation. The results highlight differences in classification taxonomies and outputs across providers, as well as significant limitations in age classification, particularly for adolescents. These findings highlight the importance of using AI-based assessments as complementary tools for analysis and prioritization, rather than as definitive determination of CSAM. Finally, the paper examines the relevant European Union regulatory framework, with particular attention to the EU AI Act, the General Data Protection Regulation (GDPR), fundamental rights, transparency, accountability, and human oversight. The legal analysis highlights how regulatory obligations and safeguards may differ depending on the deployment context, potentially creating inconsistencies across interconnected detection and reporting workflows.

</details>


### [30] [GMD-Mamba: Lightweight 3D Medical Image Segmentation Using a Grouped Multi-Directional Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10032)
*Rahim Ullah,Hasan Abbas,Hazrat Ali,Syed Waqar Nabi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Grouped Multi-Directional (GMD) Mamba轻量网络，通过分组多方向扫描与仅在最深编码阶段使用Mamba，将参数量降至19.2M并取得更优分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mamba方法在每个分辨率层都采用全宽度的多方向扫描，导致模型体积大（64–138M参数、FLOPs高），缺乏轻量化设计。

Method: 核心为GMD scan：将输入通道C分为G组，每组在一次扫描中沿不同方向展平，从而以O(C²/G)复杂度获得多方向上下文，而非O(K·C²)（K为扫描方向数）；同时Mamba仅部署在最深的编码器阶段。

Result: 模型仅19.2M参数。在BraTS2023上取得最佳平均Dice 92.0%、最低HD95 2.7，参数量约为SegMamba-V2的1/7；在MSD Spleen数据集上亦取得有竞争力表现。

Conclusion: 分组多方向扫描结合深层稀疏部署可在显著降低参数与计算成本的同时保持或超越3D医学图像分割的当前最优性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Mamba has the capability to capture global context in linear time for 3D medical image segmentation. However, existing methods employ Mamba with full-width directional scans at every resolution, keeping the model large (64-138M parameters and high FLOPs). We propose Grouped Multi-Directional (GMD) Mamba, a lightweight network based on the GMD scan that divides input channels $C$ into groups $G$ and flattens each group in different directions in one pass. This gives multi-directional context at $O(C^2/G)$ instead of $O(K \cdot C^2)$, where $K$ is the number of scan directions. Additionally, we employ Mamba only at the deepest encoder stages, resulting in 19.2M parameters. On BraTS2023, it achieves the best average Dice of 92.0% and the lowest HD95 of 2.7 at $\sim$1/7 the parameters of SegMamba-V2. For model validation, we also ran experiments on the MSD Spleen dataset, achieving competitive performance. The implementation details and code are publicly available at https://github.com/2802990u/GMD-Mamba.

</details>


### [31] [Ultra-Lightweight 3D Medical Image Segmentation via Knowledge Distillation in Label-Scarce Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10033)
*Rahim Ullah,Hasan Abbas,Syed Waqar Nabi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过知识蒸馏将139M参数的SegMamba-V2压缩为约0.96M参数的TinyUNet3D，实现约145倍压缩，Dice仅下降一个点略多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba架构虽降低计算复杂度并适用于3D医学图像分割，但现有模型计算开销仍然很大，亟需在保持竞争力性能的前提下压缩模型。

Method: 采用知识蒸馏，将SegMamba-V2教师模型蒸馏到轻量级TinyUNet3D学生模型；并在相同实验设置下系统研究不同数据标注场景中蒸馏的作用。

Result: 139M教师压缩至约0.96M学生，约145倍压缩，Dice下降仅一个点略多；蒸馏优势在标注数据稀缺时最显著；在BraTS 2023、MSD Spleen、MSD Hippocampus三个数据集上验证了泛化性。

Conclusion: 蒸馏可有效压缩Mamba类3D医学分割模型并保持竞争力性能，尤其在少标注数据场景下收益明显；TinyUNet3D代码已开源。

Abstract: Mamba is a neural network architecture that reduces computational complexity, and has been shown to be effective for 3D medical image segmentation. However, existing models still remain computationally expensive. We propose compressing Mamba by distillation while achieving competitive performance. Specifically, we demonstrate compression of a 139M-parameter SegMamba-V2 teacher into a lightweight TinyUNet3D student with about 0.96M parameters, achieving about 145$\times$ compression with a performance drop of just over one Dice point. We further investigate the utility of knowledge distillation in a range of data-labeling scenarios under identical experimental settings, and show that its advantage is most prominent when labeled data is scarce. To demonstrate the generalizability of our approach, experiments are conducted on three datasets: BraTS 2023, MSD Spleen and MSD Hippocampus. The code for TinyUNet3D is publicly available at: https://github.com/2802990u/tinyunet3d-dist-kd.

</details>


### [32] [REFORM-3D: A Representation-Centric Evaluation Framework for 3D Medical Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10062)
*Maregu Assefa,Muzammal Naseer,Divya Velayudhan,Gedamu Alemu Kumie,Naoufel Werghi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出以表征为中心的三维医学基础模型评测框架 REFORM-3D，并发布按三种几何假设预训练的 Med3DINO 编码器族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维医学基础模型评测由下游任务分数主导，把表征质量与适配策略混在一起；体数据中间距与各向异性会显著改变图像统计量而解剖结构不变，因此需要一个与适配解耦、聚焦表征本身的评测方式。

Method: REFORM-3D 在冻结编码器上考察三个问题：采集鲁棒性（解剖不变时间距变化下特征是否稳定）、跨模态解剖对齐（平衡双向检索下标注解剖结构可否跨模态恢复）、解剖迁移（病例不相交评测下少量标注支持能否恢复器官身份），并指出这些亦是预训练问题。同时构建 Med3DINO 编码器族，在 62.6K 无标注 MR、CT、PET、CTA、CBCT 体数据上按各向同性归一化、体素相对采样、间距感知重采样三种几何假设预训练。

Result: 在 Med3DINO 与异构外部编码器上，REFORM-3D 显示出模块特定的优势而非单一模型排序；在受控的 Med3DINO 对比中，体素相对采样带来更低的嵌入漂移，间距感知重采样更有利于解剖迁移，语义迁移优势依赖数据集与特征类型。

Conclusion: 三维医学基础模型的评测应解耦表征与适配，从采集鲁棒性、跨模态对齐与解剖迁移多维度刻画；预训练时的几何呈现方式对所学表征有可测量的影响，且不存在全局最优的单一模型。

Abstract: Evaluation of 3D medical foundation models is typically dominated by downstream task scores, which entangle representation quality with adaptation choices. This is especially critical in volumetric imaging, where spacing and anisotropy can substantially alter image statistics while the anatomy itself remains unchanged. We therefore introduce \textbf{REFORM-3D}, a \textbf{R}epresentation-centric \textbf{E}valuation \textbf{F}ramew\textbf{OR}k for benchmarking frozen 3D medical vision foundation \textbf{M}odels across three questions: Acquisition Robustness, asking whether features remain stable when spacing varies while anatomy is fixed; Cross-Modal Anatomical Alignment, asking whether labeled anatomical structures remain recoverable across modalities under balanced bidirectional retrieval; and Anatomical Transfer, asking whether organ identity remains recoverable from limited labeled support under case-disjoint evaluation. These are also pretraining questions, because what a 3D encoder learns depends on how volumes are geometrically presented during self-supervised pretraining. We further introduce Med3DINO encoder family, pretrained on $62.6$K unlabeled MR, CT, PET, CTA, and CBCT volumes under three explicit geometric assumptions: isotropic normalization, voxel-relative sampling, and spacing-aware resampling. Across Med3DINO and heterogeneous external encoders, REFORM-3D reveals module-specific strengths rather than a single model ordering. Within the controlled Med3DINO comparison, voxel-relative sampling yields lower embedding drift, whereas spacing-aware resampling favors anatomical transfer; semantic-transfer advantages depend on the dataset and feature type. Project link: \href{https://endarzboy.github.io/reform3d-website/}{\highlightLR{REFORM-3D}}.

</details>


### [33] [VLM as an Editing Pilot: VLM-Steered Velocity Control for Flow-Based Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10066)
*Kuan-Shen Chiu,Jia-Wei Liao,Yu-Chieh Chao,Li-Xuan Peng,Wei-Yun Ma,Cheng-Fu Chou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练、VLM引导的速度控制框架V2C-Edit，通过指令级相似度动态选择不同耦合强度的速度场，并以自适应空间掩码保护非编辑区域，缓解FlowEdit的零编辑问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导图像编辑需在实现语义修改的同时保留无关内容。FlowEdit的免反演方案高效，但其源锚定耦合过于严格，易导致"零编辑"（输出贴近原图、指令修改未生效）。作者观察到源耦合直接支配"保留—编辑"权衡，而固定耦合调度无法适应不同时间步的编辑需求。

Method: V2C-Edit为免训练框架：构建互补的、耦合强度受控的多组速度场，借助指令级相似度（ILS）判断预测变换是否符合编辑指令，并据此动态选择速度；同时引入自适应空间掩码，保留与编辑无关的区域。整体由VLM引导进行速度控制。

Result: 在MagicBrush与PIE-Bench上，结合FLUX与Stable Diffusion 3，V2C-Edit相较FlowEdit持续提升指令对齐度，同时保持有竞争力的结构保留；ILS比目标描述CLIP相似度更能反映编辑有效性。

Conclusion: 以VLM引导、按需动态调节源耦合的速度控制，是缓解零编辑、改善编辑—保留权衡的有效途径；ILS可作为更可靠的编辑效果评估信号。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing requires realizing the requested semantic change while preserving content unrelated to the instruction. FlowEdit provides an efficient inversion-free solution, but its source-anchored coupling can become overly restrictive, causing zero-edit, where the output remains close to the source and the requested change fails to emerge. We find that source coupling directly governs the preservation--editing trade-off, while a fixed coupling schedule cannot adapt to timestep-specific editing needs. Motivated by this observation, we propose V2C-Edit, a training-free VLM-steered velocity control framework. V2C-Edit constructs complementary coupling-controlled velocities and dynamically selects among them using Instruction-Level Similarity (ILS), which evaluates whether the predicted transformation follows the editing instruction. Adaptive spatial masking further preserves regions unrelated to the edit. Experiments on MagicBrush and PIE-Bench with FLUX and Stable Diffusion 3 show that V2C-Edit consistently improves instruction alignment over FlowEdit while maintaining competitive structural preservation, and that ILS better reflects editing effectiveness than target-caption CLIP similarity. Our code is available at https://github.com/Kuanshen77/V2C-Edit.

</details>


### [34] [Same Pixels, Different Documents: Measuring Part-Level Editability in Image Vectorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10088)
*Boheng Zhu,Zehua Wang,Wei Chen,Ming-Hao Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Pass@k 编辑可达性评测，揭示图像矢量化中“渲染保真度高≠可编辑性好”，多形状部件任务在 Pass@1 下大量失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矢量化的评测主要基于渲染保真度，无法区分渲染结果相同但可编辑性不同的文档；而基于编辑的评测使用 oracle 聚合部件的碎片，掩盖了选择这些对象所付出的交互代价。

Method: 引入 Pass@k 指标：在指定交互模型下，检验预设编辑能否通过 k 次点击内可选中的对象完成。构建八项文档诊断指标刻画任务之外的编辑相关属性；通过受控扰动评估结构敏感性，通过设计师操作评估实用相关性，通过替代绘制评估参考依赖性。在 211 个参考样本上评测 12 种开源与商用方法，其中 93 个样本上设 635 个编辑任务。

Result: 在四组参考集（含两组设计师绘制）中，针对多形状组成的部件，oracle 通过的任务有 64–88% 在 Pass@1 下失败，而单一形状仅为 6–40%（跨配置汇总计数）。更高保真度并不保证更高 Pass@1；部分选定诊断指标与研究中设计师的实际操作投入相关。

Conclusion: 渲染保真度不足以衡量矢量化结果的可编辑性，需以交互代价敏感的 Pass@k 与结构化诊断共同评测；该方法能区分保真度相近但编辑性差异显著的方法，并与真实设计工作负担相关联。

Abstract: Image vectorization is often evaluated by rendering fidelity, which cannot distinguish documents that render identically but support different edits. Edit-based evaluation tests outcomes, but an oracle that gathers a part's fragments can conceal the cost of selecting them. We introduce Pass@k, which tests whether a prescribed edit can succeed using objects selectable within k clicks under a specified interaction model. Eight document diagnostics characterize properties relevant to editing beyond these tasks. We assess structural sensitivity through controlled perturbations, practical relevance through designer operations, and reference dependence through alternative drawings. We evaluate twelve open and commercial methods on 211 references, with 635 edit tasks on 93 of them. In each of four reference sets, including two drawn by designers, 64-88% of oracle-passed tasks on parts composed of multiple shapes fail at Pass@1, against 6-40% for single shapes, pooling counts over configurations. Higher fidelity does not guarantee higher Pass@1, while selected diagnostics are associated with designers' effort in our study.

</details>


### [35] [Adapting Frozen Promptable Segmenters to User-Defined Taxonomies via Few-Shot Proposal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10092)
*Khayrul Islam,Christopher Frederic Miller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SNAP是一种标注流水线：在一次冻结的SAM2.1前向传播基础上，用项目自身已标注图像训练轻量分类头SnapHead，从已有提案中读出该项目特有的类别边界，效果显著优于通用SAM 3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM类可提示分割器已将"点击或短语→实例掩码"变得廉价，昂贵部分转移到了类别标注上。但每个标注项目都有自己的类别边界（如人vs车、轿车vs卡车、不同轿车型号），通用模型的边界由其训练词表固定，难以适配项目需求。

Method: 对每张图像仅做一次冻结的SAM2.1前向，获得提案；基于这些提案拟合项目级分类头SnapHead，对每个提案做分类，并在每张新标注图像后数秒内重训练。若可用SAM 3，则由相同标签逐类决定是保留SAM 3的边界还是采用SnapHead的边界。

Result: 在八种相似细胞系的真值掩码上，SAM 3的Cohen's kappa仅0.038，SnapHead达0.754（60张标注图像）；仅更改类名时，SAM 3退化到随机水平，而SnapHead分数不变。端到端，20张标注图像下，SNAP在五个显微项目上超过在评测标签上调优的SAM 3 oracle（F1 0.484 vs 0.441），在五个留出数据集上不做改动即运行，其中四个优于原生SAM 3。

Conclusion: 冻结的基础模型特征已隐含通用词表所缺失的类别边界，且项目自身的少量标签足以将其读出，无需微调主干网络即可实现项目自适应标注。

Abstract: Promptable segmenters of the SAM family turn a click or a phrase into an instance mask, so the costly part of a label is now its class. However, each annotation project draws its own class boundary, often through objects these models already know: people from cars, sedans from trucks, one sedan model from another. A generic model's boundary is fixed by its training vocabulary. We present SNAP, an annotation pipeline that learns the project's boundary from the labels the annotator draws anyway. On one frozen SAM2.1 pass per image, we fit a per-project head, SnapHead, that classifies every proposal and refits in seconds after each labeled image. Where SAM 3 is available, the same labels decide class by class whether to keep its boundary or ours. Given the true masks of eight look-alike cell lines, SAM 3 assigns classes at Cohen's kappa = 0.038 and SnapHead at 0.754 (60 labeled images), and when only the class names change, SAM 3 falls to chance and SnapHead's score is unchanged. End to end, with 20 labeled images, SNAP beats a SAM 3 oracle tuned on the evaluation labels on five microscopy projects (0.484 against 0.441 F1), and run unchanged on five held-out datasets, it beats SAM 3 as shipped on four. Our results suggest that frozen foundation features already encode the class boundaries a generic vocabulary misses, and that a project's own labels are enough to read them out.

</details>
