Inspired by latent predictive world models, we introduce SurgWorld, a video-native, self-supervised framework for surgical representation learning, which treats a specified spatial or temporal shift as a pseudo-action and learns to predict the target latent representation from a partial observation. Specifically, we propose Out-of-Sight Dynamics Modeling (OSDM) as the pretraining objective for predicting representations conditioned on relative spatio-temporal shifts. We further regularize the learned latent dynamics from OSDM with a Geometric Visual Similarity (GVS) objective by aligning patch-level relational structures between predicted and target representations, preserving spatial geometry and local visual coherence for dense perception. These two objectives are integrated through an agglomerative training framework that consolidates complementary supervisory signals from multiple teachers to learn a unified latent representation of surgical dynamics and visual geometry. Extensive transfer learning experiments on 17 surgical benchmarks show that SurgWorld consistently outperforms existing vision foundation models across surgical workflow recognition, fine-grained action understanding, and dense visual perception. We further conduct tailored qualitative and quantitative analyses, demonstrating that SurgWorld effectively captures surgical dynamics beyond the directly observed context through latent prediction.

</details>


### [137] [Not All Layers Are Equal in Image-to-Video Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11147)
*Thinesh Thiyakesan Ponbagavathi,Constantin Seibold,Alina Roitberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STRAP：基于层间时间敏感度分析的无训练适配器放置规则，早期层放空间适配器、后期层放时间适配器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效图像到视频迁移方法在每层插入相同适配器，隐含假设所有层需要同等时间建模，作者通过层间敏感度分析证明该假设不成立。

Method: 对四类视频原生模型族、两种预训练范式做层间敏感度分析；据此提出无训练的放置规则STRAP，从范式匹配的视频模型中导出空间到时间的过渡边界，边界前用空间适配器、边界后用时间适配器，不改动适配器结构。

Result: 发现时间敏感度随深度递增且增长曲线由预训练范式决定（纯视觉模型渐变上升，视觉-语言模型先抑制后回升）。四个细粒度动作识别基准上，相比均匀放置最高提升11.8%准确率，可训练参数减少29%–48%，推理时间最多降低10%；标准基准上以更少参数达到同等精度。

Conclusion: 适配器应异质、按层敏感度放置；范式感知的层间分工可同时提升精度与效率，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient image-to-video transfer adapts frozen image foundation models to video via lightweight trainable adapters. Existing methods insert the same adapter at every transformer layer, implicitly assuming all layers require equal temporal modeling. A layer-wise sensitivity analysis across four video-native model families and two pretraining paradigms reveals that this assumption is wrong: temporal sensitivity increases with depth, and the pretraining paradigm determines its growth profile. Vision-only models exhibit a gradual rise, while vision-language models show early suppression followed by late recovery.
  This finding motivates STRAP, a training-free adapter placement rule that derives a spatial-to-temporal transition boundary from a paradigm-matched video model. Spatial adapters are assigned before this boundary and temporal adapters after it, without modifying the adapter architecture itself. On four fine-grained action recognition benchmarks, STRAP improves accuracy by up to 11.8% over uniform placement, reduces trainable parameters by 29 to 48%, and lowers inference time by up to 10%. On standard benchmarks, it matches accuracy with fewer parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/th-nesh/STRAP.

</details>


### [138] [Map2SLAM: From Streaming Map Prediction to Real-Time SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11169)
*Jian Gao,Lin-Zhuo Chen,Yihang Chen,Jiagang Chen,Jiacheng Dong,Xun Cao,Nan Xue,Yinghao Xu,Yao Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Map2SLAM，将冻结的流式 3D 基础模型（LingBot-Map）的推理接口封装为 SLAM 前端观测源，并把其预测转为可修正的外部持久 SLAM 状态，实现可闭环校正的实时 SLAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式 3D 基础模型虽能在线估计相机位姿与稠密几何，但预测一经输出即固定，无法通过回环闭合纠正累积漂移，因而尚不具备 SLAM 能力。核心问题在于：如何调度这类模型的推理，并使其输出成为可反复修正的状态。

Method: 保持 LingBot-Map 冻结，仅训练轻量检索头；前端以模型因果流式推理跟踪每帧并挑选关键帧写入持久状态；后端异步决定何时、对哪些历史帧以新上下文重新联合推理，回环观测在线校正轨迹、分段观测在序列后精化稠密几何；所有调用共享每图单次编码器前向，缓存特征同时用于回环检索。

Result: 在 KITTI、VBR、Oxford Spires 上达到 SOTA，平均 ATE 较最佳免标定基线降低 40% 以上；在 RTX 5090D 上以 518×154 分辨率保持 28 FPS 以上的实时跟踪。

Conclusion: 将流式 3D 基础模型视为可重复调用的观测生成器、把 SLAM 状态外置为持久可修正状态，是解锁其 SLAM 能力的高效范式，且不引入额外编码开销。

Abstract: Streaming 3D foundation models (3DFMs) estimate camera poses and dense geometry online, but they fall short of SLAM: their predictions are fixed once emitted, so loop closures cannot correct accumulated drift. With such models already providing strong geometric capability, a central question for SLAM becomes how to schedule their inference and how to keep its results as revisable state. We present Map2SLAM, which keeps LingBot-Map frozen, trains only a lightweight retrieval head, and builds SLAM on the model's inference interface, turning its predictions into observations of an external persistent SLAM state. The front-end tracks every incoming frame with the model's causal streaming inference and decides which frames are written to the persistent state as keyframes. The back-end asynchronously decides when and which past frames to re-observe, re-running the model's joint inference over them with fresh context; verified loop observations correct the trajectory online, and segment observations refine dense geometry after the sequence. All invocations share a single frame encoder pass per image, whose cached features also drive loop retrieval, so frequent re-invocation incurs no additional encoding cost. Map2SLAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on KITTI, VBR, and Oxford Spires, reducing mean ATE by over 40% compared with the best uncalibrated baselines, while maintaining real-time tracking at over 28 FPS at a resolution of $518\times154$ on an NVIDIA RTX 5090D GPU. Please visit our project page at https://robbyant-research.github.io/Map2SLAM/.

</details>


### [139] [Source-Anchored Visual Autoregression for Extreme Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11200)
*Bingxin Wang,Yuan Lan,Yang Xiang,Yiqing Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAVAR 是一种基于视觉自回归（VAR）的生成式编解码器，在极端低码率（<0.01 bpp）下同时传输早期尺度 token 与紧凑文本描述，通过分离"布局锚定"与"语义条件"两种作用，显著降低感知失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 <0.01 bpp 的极端低码率区间，图像压缩退化为受约束的生成问题：重建需在贴近原图的同时合理推断缺失细节。自回归 token 模型提供了高效的由粗到细先验，但所传输的早期尺度 token 往往无法确定物体身份与纹理，导致重建与信源不一致（source-inconsistent）。

Method: 提出 SAVAR：传输早期尺度 token 与一条紧凑 caption。token 提供图像特定的布局信息，caption 提供全局语义。具体地，(1) 由保留尺度重建部分 VQ-VAE latent；(2) 将该 latent 映射为序列起始（start-of-sequence）初始化；(3) 用同一 latent 在自适应 LayerNorm 前对 caption 嵌入进行条件化，从而将 VAR 式生成中通常由共享条件统一驱动的两种作用显式分离。

Result: 在 Kodak 与 CLIC2020 的极端低码率区间，相比匹配的 ARPC 保留尺度工作点，SAVAR 在更低 bpp 下将 LPIPS 降低约 8–9%，DISTS 最多降低 22%；在单张 NVIDIA L20X GPU 上重建 512×512 图像约需 218 ms，保持了快速的自回归解码。

Conclusion: 在极端低码率下，引入紧凑语义 caption 并将其条件作用与 token 布局锚定解耦，可在几乎不牺牲解码速度的前提下有效缓解源不一致重建问题，提升感知质量并降低码率。

Abstract: In the extreme-low bitrate range below $0.01$ bpp, image compression becomes a constrained generation problem: the reconstruction depends on inferring plausible missing details while staying close to the input image. Autoregressive token models provide an efficient coarse-to-fine prior for this setting, but the transmitted early-scale tokens often leave object identity and texture underdetermined, which can lead to source-inconsistent reconstructions. We introduce Source-Anchored Visual Autoregression (SAVAR), a generative codec built on Visual Autoregressive (VAR) modeling. SAVAR transmits early-scale tokens and a compact caption. The tokens provide image-specific layout information, while the caption supplies global semantics. SAVAR reconstructs a partial VQ-VAE latent from the retained scales, maps it to the start-of-sequence initialization, and uses the same latent to condition the caption embedding before adaptive layer normalization, separating two roles that are typically driven by a shared condition in VAR-style generation. On the Kodak and CLIC2020 datasets in the extreme-low range, SAVAR reduces perceptual distortion relative to matched ARPC retained-scale operating points, lowering LPIPS by about $8$--$9\%$ and DISTS by up to $22\%$ at a lower bpp. It reconstructs a $512 \times 512$ image in about $218$ ms on one NVIDIA L20X GPU, retaining fast autoregressive decoding in the evaluated setting.

</details>


### [140] [Viscot-steer: Disentangling and Transferring Visual Chain-of-Thought via Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11209)
*Ningning Wang,Beier Zhu,Xingyu Zhu,Kaiyun Yang,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练激活引导框架Viscot-steer，将visual-CoT解耦为grounding与reasoning两个向量并跨异构LVLM迁移，主指标平均超越最强基线1.86个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: visual-CoT能力依赖昂贵的后训练且与特定骨干紧耦合，跨模型迁移困难；现有迁移方法将其视为单一能力，用统一表示而忽视grounding与reasoning的功能区分，并引入与阶段无关的干预。

Method: 免训练激活引导：先将visual-CoT解耦为grounding向量与reasoning向量，从源模型对应能力相关层中提取；再将向量对齐到目标模型的激活空间，并在边界框预测阶段引导grounding层、在推理轨迹生成阶段引导reasoning层。

Result: 在高分辨率视觉理解、通用视觉感知与推理、数学视觉推理等基准上，可将不同visual-CoT模型的能力迁移到架构各异的LVLM，主评估指标平均优于最强能力迁移基线1.86个百分点。

Conclusion: 按能力解耦并进行阶段对齐的激活引导，是实现visual-CoT跨异构LVLM免训练迁移的有效途径。

Abstract: Visual chain-of-thought (visual-CoT), also known as "thinking with images", equips LVLMs with the ability to locate and use key visual evidence for reasoning. However, acquiring such visual-CoT capabilities requires costly post-training and tightly couples them to a specific backbone, making transfer to other LVLMs difficult. Existing cross-model transfer methods typically treat visual-CoT as a monolithic capability, using a unified representation that ignores the functional distinction between grounding and reasoning and introduces stage-irrelevant interventions. To address this problem, we propose Viscot-steer, a training-free activation steering framework for transferring visual-CoT capabilities across heterogeneous LVLMs. Specifically, Viscot-steer first disentangles visual-CoT into grounding and reasoning vectors and extracts them from the source-model layers associated with the corresponding capabilities. It then aligns the vectors with the activation space of the target model and steers the grounding and reasoning layers during bounding-box prediction and reasoning-trace generation, respectively. Across benchmarks covering high-resolution visual understanding, general visual perception and reasoning, and mathematical visual reasoning, Viscot-steer transfers capabilities from different visual-CoT models to architecturally diverse LVLMs and outperforms the strongest capability-transfer baseline by an average of 1.86 percentage points on the primary evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [141] [DynOLAT: OLAT Video Prediction for Controllable Dynamic Full-Body Relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11210)
*Taoming Liu,Yinji ShenTu,Guangzhao He,Yudong Jin,Yukang Ye,Siyu Zhang,Fan Jia,Wei Hua,Hujun Bao,Xiaowei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DynOLAT：从平光输入预测时序一致的OLAT基视频，再按环境贴图线性组合，实现更忠实于光照方向与相对强度的动态全身人像重打光。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习式重打光方法虽能生成视觉上合理的视频，但光照响应不精确：阴影与高光偏离目标方向，亮度不随目标环境相对强度变化，更接近外观协调而非可控重打光。

Method: 将单次重打光分解为OLAT基：以平光视频为输入，预测时序一致的全身体OLAT基视频，再依据目标环境贴图的线性权重组合各基；方向响应显式编码于基中，相对强度由权重决定。同时构建动态采集流程与首个大规模动态全身OLAT数据集（224个身份-服装组合，超2400万帧）。

Result: 相比对比基线，DynOLAT在目标光照方向与相对强度变化下响应更忠实，可在保持细节与时序一致性的同时实现可控视频重打光。

Conclusion: OLAT分解式重打光框架配合大规模动态OLAT数据，可提升光照响应的可控性与保真度；数据集与处理工具将公开。

Abstract: Accurate lighting response is essential for realistic dynamic human relighting, because shadows, highlights, and color changes should follow the target illumination in both direction and relative strength. Current learning-based relighting methods can generate visually plausible videos, but they often exhibit imprecise lighting responses: shadows and highlights may drift away from the target direction, and the output brightness may not faithfully follow the relative intensities in the target environment, making the result closer to appearance harmonization than controllable relighting. To address this issue, we propose DynOLAT, a generalizable OLAT video prediction framework for dynamic full-body humans. Instead of directly synthesizing a single relit appearance, our method predicts temporally consistent One-Light-At-a-Time (OLAT) basis videos from flat-lit inputs, and then linearly combines the predicted bases according to the target environment map. This OLAT-based formulation makes the directional response explicit in the predicted bases, while the relative lighting strength is determined by the linear weights derived from the environment map. We further introduce a dynamic capture pipeline and the first large-scale dynamic full-body OLAT dataset, comprising over 24 million frames from 224 identity-outfit combinations, where different outfits of the same person define separate combinations. Experiments show that DynOLAT responds more faithfully to variations in target lighting direction and relative intensity than the tested baselines, enabling controllable video relighting while preserving fine appearance details and temporal coherence. The dataset and processing tools will be publicly released to foster future research.

</details>


### [142] [Disentangling Where and When:Factored Spatio-Temporal Explanations for Video Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11214)
*Bhavan Vasu,Giuseppe Raffa,Prasad Tadepalli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扰动的后设解释框架，将视频动作识别的空间外观与时序动态解耦为外观掩码与运动掩码，并用分离评估协议验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频动作识别的后设解释方法通常将空间外观与时序动态混为单一的显著性/重要性图，无法区分两类贡献。

Method: 在冻结的动作识别模型上，通过因子化扰动架构联合优化空间外观掩码（M_a）与时序运动掩码（M_m）；采用最近帧替换识别重要时序动态；加入分离惩罚（避免外观掩码在运动掩码已丢弃的帧上分配预算）与基于ReLU的面积约束（惩罚超出预算的解释）；并提出独立扫描空间像素与时序帧的分离评估协议，附理论证明。

Result: 在EPIC-Kitchens-55、EGTEA Gaze+、Something-Something V2三个数据集上，相对主流基线取得最低删除AUC，且掩码稀疏度约为基线的5倍。

Conclusion: 解耦外观与时序贡献可产生更忠实、更稀疏的视频解释，分离评估协议为视频解释方法提供了更细粒度的评价手段。

Abstract: Post-hoc explanation methods for video action recognition typically conflate spatial appearance and temporal dynamics into a single saliency or importance map. We propose a perturbation-based framework that disentangles these contributions into a spatial appearance mask ($M_a$) and a temporal motion mask ($M_m$). The two masks are jointly optimized against a frozen action recognition model through factored perturbation architecture, with nearest frame replacement to identify important temporal dynamics. A separation penalty discourages the appearance mask from spending budget on frames that the motion mask has already discarded. A ReLU-based area constraint penalizes explanations larger than an assigned budget. To evaluate these explanations, we introduce a separation protocol that sweeps spatial pixels and temporal frames independently, backed by proofs characterizing the proposed method. Across EPIC-Kitchens-55, EGTEA Gaze+, and Something-Something V2, our method attains the lowest deletion AUC against leading baselines while producing masks roughly five times sparser.

</details>


### [143] [Auto-GlaKG: Automated Biomarker Reasoning for Explainable Glaucoma Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11258)
*Cheng Huang,Zeyu Han,Yian Hu,Yaopeng Ding,Jingxi Qiu,Yadi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Auto-GlaKG 用 ResNet50 从眼底图像预测 CDR 与 6 项形态学体征，再由固定临床先验规则的知识图谱推理器聚合为青光眼评分；689 张图像五折交叉验证 AUC 0.951（95%CI 0.931–0.969），敏感度 0.970、特异度 0.779。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释青光眼检测需要在眼底图像、临床可解释生物标志物与诊断预测之间建立透明联系；知识图谱推理能提供这种结构，但以往基于标注生物标志物评测的性能并不能代表仅有图像输入时的真实表现，因此需评估纯图像端到端流程的可解释性与有效性。

Method: 提出 Auto-GlaKG 自动化框架，将图像级生物标志物预测与固定临床先验规则相结合：ResNet50 在生物标志物监督下回归杯盘比（CDR）并预测 6 项形态学体征；知识图谱推理器按带符号的规则贡献聚合为青光眼评分。采用分层、按组划分的五折交叉验证，在 689 张眼底图像上评测，并与匹配的图像分类器对比，另做消融式替换实验（用标注 CDR 替代预测 CDR）。

Result: 混合 AUC 0.951（95%CI 0.931–0.969）、敏感度 0.970、特异度 0.779；匹配的端到端图像分类器 AUC 同为 0.951；规则阶段每图像仅增加约 0.2 ms；以标注 CDR 替换预测 CDR 后，折均值 AUC 由 0.953 升至 0.995，显示 CDR 估计是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该框架在几乎不增加计算开销的前提下，为图像级预测提供规则级显式解释，性能与黑箱图像分类器相当；同时表明 CDR 预测精度是限制性能的关键因素，后续应使用独立测量的生物标志物对 CDR 估计进行重点验证。

Abstract: Interpretable glaucoma detection requires a transparent connection between fundus images, clinically meaningful biomarkers, and diagnostic predictions. Knowledge graph (KG) reasoning provides this structure, but performance measured using annotated biomarkers does not establish performance when only images are available. We present Auto-GlaKG, an automated framework that combines image-based biomarker prediction with fixed clinical-prior rules. A ResNet50 network predicts the cup-to-disc ratio (CDR) and six morphological signs using biomarker supervision, and a KG reasoner aggregates their signed rule contributions into a glaucoma score. On 689 fundus images evaluated using stratified, group-aware five-fold cross-validation, Auto-GlaKG achieves a pooled area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.951 (95\% confidence interval: 0.931--0.969), with sensitivity of 0.970 and specificity of 0.779. The matched image classifier also achieves an AUC of 0.951, while the rule stage adds 0.2~ms per image. Replacing predicted CDR with its annotation increases fold-mean AUC from 0.953 to 0.995. The framework provides an explicit rule-level account of image-based predictions and identifies CDR estimation as a priority for validation using independently measured biomarkers.

</details>


### [144] [DuSS-PET: Self-Supervised Low-Dose PET Reconstruction Using Pseudo-Dual-Domain Consistency Without Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11265)
*Shuijin Lin,Yuzhu He,Yongbo Wang,Jianhua Ma,Fan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuSS-PET：首个在重建图像与伪正弦图之间施加一致性约束的自监督低剂量PET重建框架，无需配对低/全剂量数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PET高质量成像需高辐射剂量；现有深度学习低剂量重建主要依赖全监督，需成对低/全剂量扫描，临床难以获取。

Method: 基于计算式Noisier2Noise范式，采用伯努利破坏策略在图像空间近似模拟低计数噪声模式，生成图像域伪正弦图；框架含三部分：用于伪正弦图恢复的自适应Swin Transformer、即插即用的图像增强模块、以及强制伪正弦图与图像表示一致的SSL一致性约束策略。

Result: 多中心数据集实验显示其重建性能优于SOTA自监督方法，在各剂量水平下与全监督方法相当甚至更优，并在不同扫描仪厂商间展现优异泛化性。

Conclusion: 图像空间生成的伪正弦图可作为稳健的计算一致性约束，实现无需全剂量训练扫描的增强成像；代码将开源。

Abstract: Positron emission tomography (PET) is pivotal in medicine and healthcare, but requires high radiation doses for quality imaging. Although deep learning has shown promise for low-dose PET reconstruction/enhancement, existing methods primarily rely on fully supervised learning, which requires paired low- and full-dose scans that are clinically impractical to obtain. To overcome this fundamental limitation, we propose DuSS-PET, a novel self-supervised PET reconstruction framework that operates without any paired low- and full-dose data. Our method is built upon a computational Noisier2Noise (Nr2N) paradigm, featuring a Bernoulli corruption strategy that serves as an image-space approximation to emulate low-count noise patterns in image-derived pseudo-sinogram representations. The DuSS-PET framework integrates three core components: an adaptive Swin transformer for pseudo-sinogram restoration, a plug-and-play module for image enhancement, and a consistency-constrained SSL strategy that enforces agreement between the pseudo-sinogram and image representations. Extensive experiments on multi-center datasets demonstrate that DuSS-PET achieves superior reconstruction performance, outperforming state-of-the-art SSL methods and achieving comparable or even better results than fully supervised approaches across various dose levels. In particular, it exhibits exceptional generalization across different scanner vendors. This work is among the first self-supervised low-dose PET reconstruction framework that enforces consistency between reconstructed images and pseudo-sinograms, where the pseudo-sinogram generated in the image space serves as a solid computational consistency constraint for enhanced imaging without full-dose training scans. The source code will be publicly available at https://github.com/ladderlab-xjtu/DuSS-PET.

</details>


### [145] [BiRefNet-V2: Rethinking Bilateral Reference for High-Resolution Dichotomous Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11272)
*Peng Zheng,Deng-Ping Fan,Binglu Wang,Nicu Sebe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BiRefNet-V2，将双参考分割重新表述为"受控信息恢复"，通过保局部保语义的细节注入、学习式边界流与双分支解码器，在13个评测集上取得SOTA并将训练轮数从600降至50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值图像分割需像素级前景/背景分离并保留细丝、锐边与复杂纹理。原始BiRefNet的双参考设计存在缺陷：向内参考丢失局部空间结构且缺乏语义引导，向外参考仅依赖单通道梯度注意力。

Method: 将范式统一表述为"受控信息恢复"：局部性决定保留的空间信息、语义选择性决定进入的信息、聚焦监督决定细化位置。(1) 通过space-to-depth变换、重叠patch与语义引导门实现保局部、语义引导的细节注入；(2) 学习式边界流配合聚焦监督，无需边界标注即可生成全通道残差注意力；(3) 双分支解码器结合深度可分离边界卷积与大核注意力。框架现代化：LayerNorm、GELU、Lion优化器与DINOv3骨干。

Result: 训练轮数由600降至50；在DIS5K、伪装目标检测和高分辨率显著目标检测的全部13个评测集上均达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过将双参考分割重构为受控信息恢复，并以模块化设计落实三个控制原则，BiRefNet-V2在精度、效率与泛化性上均优于原版及现有方法。

Abstract: Dichotomous image segmentation requires pixel-accurate foreground-background separation while preserving fine structures such as thin filaments, sharp edges, and intricate textures. BiRefNet addressed this challenge through bilateral references, combining image patches and gradient information, but its inward reference loses local spatial structure and lacks semantic guidance, while its outward reference relies on single-channel gradient attention. We recast this paradigm as controlled information recovery - locality determines what spatial information survives, semantic selectivity what information enters, and focused boundary supervision where refinement occurs - and propose BiRefNet-V2 to implement it: (1) locality-preserving and semantically guided detail injection through space-to-depth transformation, overlapping patches, and a Semantically Guided Gate; (2) a learned boundary stream with focused supervision that generates full-channel residual attention without requiring boundary annotations; and (3) a dual-branch decoder integrating depth-wise boundary convolutions with large-kernel attention. A modernized framework - LayerNorm, GELU, the Lion optimizer, and a DINOv3 backbone - cuts training from 600 to 50 epochs. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on DIS5K, camouflaged object detection, and high-resolution salient object detection across all 13 evaluation sets. Code is available at https://github.com/ZhengPeng7/BiRefNet-V2.

</details>


### [146] [TransArt3D: From Two States to Articulated 3D Reconstruction with Video Transition Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11304)
*Chenhao Guan,Liying Yang,Xinxing Yu,Hao Mo,Tianshun Han,Renjun Gao,Yanyan Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TransArt3D 是一个优化式框架，仅用两张端点图像重建可交互的铰接式三维资产，通过把视频扩散模型的转移动态先验转化为显式的部件结构与关节运动学约束来完成重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量图像重建铰接资产欠约束严重。现有优化方法在静态三维先验下独立评分每个状态，使逐状态重建误差渗入运动部件与关节，产生虚假运动；视频扩散模型蕴含物体运动信息，但仅在二维层面隐式表达。

Method: 1）转移动态耦合的三维一致性：对端点间生成的帧联合应用静态三维先验，稳定不变区域并初始化部件与关节；2）整流流运动学精化：以视频先验监督覆盖整个转移过程的三维运动场，同时用部件分配与共享关节在优化全程约束该场，避免逐状态误差污染关节建模；3）运动感知的规范外观融合：利用恢复的运动学将两幅端点图像映射到统一规范表面，为仅单一状态可见的区域补全纹理。

Result: 在 PartNet-Mobility 上，几何与关节精度优于使用相同两张端点的方法；在铰接状态几何、关节定位与外观上超过使用六个观测状态的方法。

Conclusion: 将二维视频转移动态先验显式转化为部件结构与关节运动学约束，可有效缓解逐状态误差泄漏，使极少观测下的铰接资产重建在几何、关节与外观上均取得更优表现。

Abstract: Reconstructing interactable articulated assets from a few images is key to robotics and simulation, yet highly underconstrained. Existing optimization-based methods fit an articulated asset under a static 3D prior that scores each state independently, so per-state reconstruction errors leak into the estimated moving part and joint as false motion. Video diffusion models capture how objects move between states, but only implicitly in 2D. To this end, we present TransArt3D, an optimization-based framework that reconstructs an articulated 3D asset from two endpoint images by translating this video transition prior into explicit part structure and joint kinematics. It first lifts frames generated between the endpoints with a transition-coupled 3D consensus that applies the static 3D prior jointly across frames, stabilizing unchanging regions and initializing the parts and joint. A rectified-flow kinematic refinement then lets the video prior supervise a 3D motion field over the whole transition, while part assignment and a shared joint constrain the field throughout optimization, keeping per-state errors out of the articulation. Finally, a motion-aware canonical appearance fusion uses the recovered articulation to map both endpoint images onto one canonical surface, texturing regions that only one state reveals. On PartNet-Mobility, TransArt3D outperforms methods given the same two endpoints in geometry and joint accuracy, and surpasses methods given six observed states in articulated-state geometry, joint localization and appearance.

</details>


### [147] [Beyond Similarity Entropy: A Rank-Guided Data-Free Quantization Framework for Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11320)
*Runqing Jiang,Ye Zhang,Pengpeng Yu,Zhuo Song,Changjun Li,Longguang Wang,Yulan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RankQ-ViT：基于秩引导的无数据量化框架，通过块感知自对比目标生成特征更丰富的校准样本，并按秩与语义一致性筛选样本，提升 ViT 低比特量化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT 的计算与内存开销大，难以部署到移动端；现有无数据量化（DFQ）方法通过最大化 patch 相似度的熵来合成校准样本，但相似度熵只描述相似度数值的分散程度，无法保证生成样本覆盖多样化的特征方向，从而限制校准效果。

Method: 先观察到高斯噪声生成的 patch 相似度矩阵秩低于真实图像；据此提出块感知自对比目标，在合成数据生成时促进特征多样性，且无需在每步优化中显式计算矩阵秩；随后依据秩与语义一致性选择校准样本，进行分块（block-wise）量化模型重建。

Result: 在 ImageNet、COCO、ModelNet40、ShapeNet 上跨多种架构与任务验证有效性，低比特量化下提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 以矩阵秩作为特征多样性的代理指标，可在无数据条件下生成更高质量的校准样本，为 ViT 无数据量化提供有效且通用的改进路径。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have demonstrated remarkable performance, yet their computational and memory demands pose significant challenges for deployment on mobile devices. Data-free quantization (DFQ) enables the compression of ViTs without access to their original training data. Existing DFQ methods often synthesize calibration samples by maximizing the entropy of patch similarity. However, similarity entropy describes the dispersion of similarity values without ensuring that the generated samples cover diverse feature directions, potentially limiting their effectiveness for quantization calibration. To address this limitation, we propose RankQ-ViT, a rank-guided DFQ framework for ViTs. We first observe that patch similarity matrices derived from Gaussian noise tend to have lower ranks than those derived from real images. Motivated by this observation, we introduce a block-aware self-contrastive objective that promotes feature diversity during synthetic data generation without explicitly computing matrix rank at each optimization step. We then select calibration samples based on their rank and semantic consistency and use them for block-wise reconstruction of the quantized model. Experiments on ImageNet, COCO, ModelNet40, and ShapeNet demonstrate the effectiveness of RankQ-ViT across multiple architectures and tasks, particularly under low-bit quantization. The code is available at https://github.com/Runqing-forMost/rankq-vit.git

</details>


### [148] [Conditional Diffusion-Based Proxy Representation Recovery for BCVA Prediction with Missing SLO Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11326)
*Chuyi He,Hua Zou,Fei Luo,Zixian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于条件扩散的SLO代理表征恢复与融合预测方法，在58.80% SLO缺失率下实现术后BCVA高精度预测（0.1 LogMAR内准确率0.9714，MAE 0.0423）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术前预测白内障术后最佳矫正视力（BCVA）有助于预后评估与个体化决策；多模态学习可整合OCT图像、SLO图像与临床文本，但真实临床数据中SLO常因采集或数据管理问题缺失，导致多模态输入不完整。

Method: 不重建原始SLO图像，而将SLO缺失建模为隐特征空间中的代理表征恢复：先融合OCT图像特征与临床文本特征构建OCT-文本条件表征，用以引导扩散模型反向去噪过程，估计样本特异的SLO代理表征；再以恢复的代理表征替代缺失SLO模态，输入下游多模态融合预测网络完成术后BCVA预测。

Result: 在2,141例术前多模态白内障样本（SLO缺失率58.80%）上验证；绝对误差≤0.1 LogMAR准则下准确率0.9714，平均绝对误差0.0423，优于代表性术后视力预测方法与缺失模态学习方法。

Conclusion: 表征层面的代理恢复可有效缓解SLO模态缺失对术后BCVA预测的影响。

Abstract: Preoperative prediction of postoperative best-corrected visual acuity (BCVA) can support prognosis assessment and individualized decision-making in cataract surgery. Multimodal learning can integrate preoperative optical coherence tomography (OCT) images, scanning laser ophthalmoscopy (SLO) images, and clinical text to characterize patient status. However, SLO images are often missing in real-world clinical data due to acquisition or data-management issues, resulting in incomplete multimodal inputs. To address this problem, this paper proposes a conditional diffusion-based method for SLO proxy representation recovery and fusion prediction. Instead of reconstructing raw SLO images, the proposed method formulates SLO missingness as proxy representation recovery in the latent feature space. Specifically, OCT image features and clinical text features are first fused to construct an OCT-text conditional representation, which guides the reverse denoising process of the diffusion model to estimate sample-specific SLO proxy representations. The recovered SLO proxy representation is then used as a substitute for the unavailable SLO modality and incorporated into the downstream multimodal fusion prediction network for postoperative BCVA prediction. Experiments were conducted on 2,141 preoperative multimodal cataract samples, with an SLO missing rate of 58.80%. The proposed method achieved an accuracy of 0.9714 under the criterion of an absolute prediction error within 0.1 LogMAR and a mean absolute error of 0.0423, outperforming representative postoperative visual acuity prediction and missing-modality learning methods. These results indicate that representation-level proxy recovery can alleviate the impact of SLO modality missingness on postoperative BCVA prediction.

</details>


### [149] [MoT3DVG: A Benchmark for Outdoor 3D Visual Grounding with Motion-Aware Descriptions and Temporal Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11334)
*Shijia Zhao,Xusheng Guo,Qiming Xia,Xun Huang,Kezheng Xiong,Zhihong Liu,Chenglu Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出面向动态室外场景的3D视觉定位数据集MoT3DVG及框架DynaVG，通过短长时窗动态编码建模运动线索，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉定位（3DVG）研究集中于静态室内物体，难以适用于动态室外自动驾驶场景；引入刻画运动物体时序动态的运动描述有望缓解这一问题。

Method: 构建大规模动态感知室外3DVG数据集MoT3DVG（基于nuScenes，850个场景、31,128帧、144,568条含运动描述的文本提示）；并提出DynaVG框架，在模态专属编码器之后、语言-点云对齐之前引入短长时融合动态编码模块SLIDE，通过短时窗编码捕获局部运动线索、通过长时窗滑动建模全局时序上下文。

Result: 在MoT3DVG上的大量实验表明DynaVG达到当前最优性能，验证了运动感知编码的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了室外3DVG的开放挑战与未来研究方向，凸显运动感知时序建模的重要性。

Abstract: 3D visual grounding (3DVG) localizes language-referred objects in 3D scenes, with broad applicability in both indoor and outdoor environments. However, existing 3DVG research focuses on static indoor objects, limiting its use in dynamic outdoor autonomous driving. Motion descriptions, which capture the temporal dynamics of moving objects, offer a promising way to address this challenge. We therefore introduce MoT3DVG, a large-scale dataset for dynamic-aware outdoor 3DVG with temporally evolving motion descriptions. It contains 850 scenarios with 31,128 frames from nuScenes dataset, and provides 144,568 language prompts with motion-aware descriptions for dynamic objects across time. We further propose DynaVG, a novel framework that effectively leverages temporal cues for outdoor 3DVG. In addition to the standard modality-specific encoders, DynaVG introduces a Short-Long Integrated Dynamic Encoding (SLIDE) module before the language-point cloud alignment. SLIDE models both local motion cues and global temporal context via short-term window encoding and long-term window shifting. Extensive experiments demonstrate its state-of-the-art performance on MoT3DVG, validating the effectiveness of motion-aware encoding. Our work reveals open challenges and promising directions for future research in outdoor 3DVG.

</details>


### [150] [NuJEV: Structured Visual Judgments for Consistent Nuclei Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11359)
*Yonghuang Wu,Pengfei Wang,Zhenyang Liang,Yize Ma,Pengfei Song,Guoqing Wu,Jinhua Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NuJEV，将核感知重构为结构化决策任务，在Qwen3-VL-2B中联合训练视觉LoRA与并行核预测头，实现核存在性、中心点与类型的定位和分类，计数由预测核汇总得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核定量不仅是给出数量，还需定位并分型，自由形式的文本生成难以保证结构化、可校验的输出；受"以结构化决策替代自由生成"思路启发，希望提升核感知的定位与计数精度。

Method: 在Qwen3-VL-2B上联合训练视觉LoRA与一个并行预测头，预测核的存在性、中心坐标与类型；单次图像编码同时输入核预测头和原始语言解码器，解码器负责解释区域与类型请求，计数由预测到的核直接统计得出。并做了匹配编码器的消融以验证视觉适配的作用。

Result: 在PanNuke 2,732张测试图上，五查询MAE为1.844，稠密图像上为5.268，定位/分型F1为0.816/0.682；匹配编码器消融显示视觉适配使稠密误差降低41.0%；语言请求场景下，请求MAE相对直接生成降低76.2%。

Conclusion: 结构化核预测头与视觉LoRA联合训练显著优于自由形式生成，在计数、定位与分型上均取得较好效果，代码将开源。

Abstract: Nuclear quantification requires locating and typing nuclei, beyond generating a count. Inspired by Jev's structured decisions in place of free-form generation, we introduce NuJEV for nuclear perception in Qwen3-VL-2B. We jointly train visual LoRA and a parallel head to predict nuclear presence, centers, and types. One image encoding feeds both the nuclear head and original language decoder. The decoder interprets region and type requests, with counts computed from the predicted nuclei. On 2,732 PanNuke test images, NuJEV achieves a five-query mean absolute error ($\mathrm{MAE}_5$) of 1.844 overall and 5.268 on dense images, with localization/typed F1 of 0.816/0.682. A matched encoder ablation shows 41.0\% lower dense error with visual adaptation. For language requests, NuJEV reduces request MAE by 76.2\% relative to direct generation. Our code will be released as open source.

</details>


### [151] [SegDis: Foreground Segmentation Followed by Type Discrimination for Salient and Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11378)
*Dingning Luo,Shuo Dou,Tong Jiang,Xianfeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SegDis，将显著/伪装目标检测的联合输出解耦为前景概率与给定前景的条件类型概率两个分支，实现前景恢复与类型判别误差的分离诊断，并以约1/8参数量在USC12K上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOD与COD共享前景支持，但现有联合方法仅输出二值掩码而无法区分类型；USC12K虽区分两类，其基线USCNet仍在单一归一化中将前景存在性与类型绑定，导致失败来源不可归因。交叉任务研究显示前景定位可在SOD/COD间迁移，而类型判别不可迁移，二者需独立机制。

Method: 提出SegDis框架，将输出分解为显式前景概率与条件类型概率（给定前景下SOD/COD的条件概率）；FLR模块提供高质量、任务无关的前景支持；RTA模块基于前景-背景关系精化类型分配，且不依赖任务标识符。由于前景支持显式化，每个GT前景像素可被判为正确类型、类型互换或漏检，从而分离诊断两类误差。

Result: 以PVT-v2-B2为骨干，SegDis在USC12K上达到68.98% FG-mIoU与78.35% mIoU，参数量约为USCNet的八分之一；CSCS指标为5.75。

Conclusion: 前景定位与类型判别应解耦为独立机制；显式分解输出不仅提升精度与参数效率，还提供了可归因的误差诊断方式。

Abstract: Salient and camouflaged object detection (SOD and COD) both recover foreground support, yet most joint methods output only a binary mask and therefore cannot distinguish the type. USC12K distinguishes the two types, but its baseline USCNet still binds foreground existence to the type within a single normalization, so the failure source cannot be attributed. Our cross-task study shows that foreground localization transfers between SOD and COD, whereas type discrimination does not: a COD-only model recovers foreground on SOD images but its type judgement follows the training distribution rather than the foreground-background relation, so the two decisions require separate mechanisms. SegDis factorizes the output into an explicit foreground probability and a conditional type probability given foreground: FLR provides high-quality task-agnostic support, and RTA refines the SOD/COD assignment from the foreground-background relation without a task identifier. Because support is explicit, each ground-truth foreground pixel is classified as correct-type, type-swapped, or missed, so foreground-recovery and type-assignment errors are diagnosed separately. With PVT-v2-B2, SegDis attains 68.98% FG-mIoU and 78.35% mIoU on USC12K at about one-eighth of USCNet's parameters; CSCS is 5.75.

</details>


### [152] [Let the Gallery Learn Itself: Training-Free Iterative Side-View Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11391)
*Zijun Huang,Suncheng Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GSE：免训练的人脸识别测试时库自进化框架，通过dedup-FIFO策展在成功识别后归档新样本并锁定注册模板，无需梯度更新即可显著提升侧脸与大FAR场景性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面部识别系统难以在部署后从每次识别经验中持续学习，通常依赖重训练或梯度更新，成本高（GPU、训练数据）。作者希望在测试时以极低成本让识别系统自适应提升。

Method: 提出Gallery Self-Evolution（GSE）无训练框架：核心为dedup-FIFO策展机制——在识别成功后归档新颖外观样本，同时永久锁定注册模板，每身份最多保留30个嵌入向量。

Result: 在CFP-FP与IJB-C上，45°偏航下侧视差距缩小23个点，TAR@FAR=0.001提升9.4个点；全部在CPU级成本下完成，无需重训练与GPU。

Conclusion: 证明了测试时仅通过库的在线演化即可获得显著识别增益，为低成本、可持续自适应的人脸识别部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: What if a face recognition system could learn from every encounter without a single gradient update? We present Gallery Self-Evolution (GSE), a training-free framework where the gallery itself adapts at test time. The core mechanism---dedup-FIFO curation---archives novel appearance samples after successful recognition while permanently locking the enrollment template, bounded at 30 embeddings per identity. On CFP-FP and IJB-C benchmarks, GSE closes the side-view gap by +23 points at 45° yaw and boosts TAR@FAR=0.001 by +9.4 points, all at CPU-level cost. No retraining. No GPU. Just a gallery that grows smarter every time it meets you.

</details>


### [153] [GenRM-Flow: Generators are Process-aware Reward Models in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11421)
*Siming Fu,Ruizhe He,Zheming Fu,Zeyue Xue,Fengkai Liu,Yufei Jiao,Jie Huang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Xuan Yang,Lihui Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将偏好调优视频生成器本身转化为过程感知的奖励模型（GenRM-Flow），用其速度预测误差直接充当奖励信号，无需外部VLM或辅助奖励头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频奖励模型依赖模型的表征能力（复用的VLM像素级打分器或冻结生成器特征上的辅助打分头），而生成器自身预测速度、去噪潜变量、生成视频的能力未参与打分，被作者认为忽视了离线偏好学习的结构性性质。

Method: 从KL约束下最优策略的解析解中分离奖励项，将精确生成对数似然映射到流匹配代理，使奖励确定性地表现为连续时间速度预测误差的相对差异；据此提出GenRM-Flow，在含噪潜空间中直接进行过程感知奖励读出，并证明DPO、IPO等对比式与RWR等非对比式目标均为通往同一最优策略的优化路径。

Result: 理论上建立了生成似然—流匹配速度误差—奖励之间的等价关系，并跨多种微调目标统一了奖励读出形式；提取的过程奖励可无缝替代FlowAWR中的外部VLM打分器，且仅依赖原生潜输出即可带来可测量的下游视频生成改进。

Conclusion: 视频生成器的生成能力本身即可作为奖励来源，GenRM-Flow提供了无需辅助奖励网络的过程感知奖励范式，为视频生成的人类偏好对齐提供了统一且高效的新路径。

Abstract: Aligning video generation models with human preference depends critically on the quality of the reward model that scores generated videos. Current video reward models, however, source their reward signal from a model's representational rather than generative capacity: they either repurpose vision-language models as pixel-space scorers, or train auxiliary scoring heads on top of frozen generator features. The generator's actual ability to predict velocities, denoise latents, and produce videos plays no role in scoring its own generations. We argue that this overlooks a structural property of offline preference learning. Any preference optimization objective that approximates an offline preference dataset mathematically targets a strictly defined KL-constrained optimal policy. By isolating the reward term from this analytical solution and mapping the exact generation log-likelihood to its flow-matching surrogate, the empirical reward emerges deterministically as the relative difference in continuous-time velocity-prediction errors. We formalize this equivalence into GenRM-Flow, a framework where a preference-tuned video generator inherently operates as a process-aware reward model directly in the noisy latent space. We further show theoretically that distinct fine-tuning objectives, whether contrastive like DPO and IPO or non-contrastive like RWR, are alternative optimization pathways toward the same optimal policy, each inducing a reward readout from the generator's velocity-prediction errors. Furthermore, the extracted process reward seamlessly substitutes external VLM scorers in FlowAWR. Operating purely on native latent outputs without auxiliary reward networks, this generative reward drives measurable improvements in downstream video generation.

</details>


### [154] [Flex-DDCM: Zero-Shot Task-Oriented Image Compression via Diffusion Trajectory Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11427)
*Yixin Gao,Runsen Feng,Xin Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Flex-DDCM 提出一种基于扩散轨迹引导的零样本任务导向图像压缩框架，通过任务引导的噪声选择将任务先验嵌入压缩表示，在不增加解码复杂度的前提下实现像素保真、感知质量与下游任务性能的灵活权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本图像压缩方法利用预训练扩散模型的强大先验，无需压缩专用训练即可获得感知友好的重建，但图像压缩不仅服务于人眼观看，也服务于机器分析；现有零样本编解码器在满足这些应用多样化重建需求方面灵活性有限。

Method: 在 Turbo-DDCM 基础上构建 Flex-DDCM：通过任务引导的噪声选择来适配编码的扩散轨迹，将任务引导效应嵌入压缩表示；引导项可单独或组合使用，并采用时间步相关的强度控制在去噪全过程中调节其影响，从而在不引入额外解码复杂度的情况下实现任务自适应。

Result: 实验表明该方法在像素保真度、感知质量与下游任务性能之间实现灵活权衡，性能可与经过训练的任务导向编解码器竞争，且解码速度优于 Diff-ICMH。

Conclusion: Flex-DDCM 证明零样本扩散压缩可通过轨迹引导灵活适配多种重建需求，兼顾人眼感知与机器分析任务，并保持较低解码开销。

Abstract: Recent zero-shot image compression methods exploit the powerful priors of pretrained diffusion models to produce perceptually pleasing reconstructions without compression-specific training. However, image compression serves not only human viewing but also machine analysis, while existing zero-shot codecs have limited flexibility in meeting the diverse reconstruction requirements of these applications. In this paper, we propose Flex-DDCM, a framework for zero-shot task-oriented image compression via diffusion trajectory guidance. Building on Turbo-DDCM, we adapt the coded diffusion trajectory through task-guided noise selection, embedding the effects of task guidance into the compressed representation. Guidance terms can be applied individually or combined, with timestep-dependent strengths controlling their influence throughout denoising. This design enables task adaptation without introducing additional decoding complexity. Experiments demonstrate flexible trade-offs among pixel fidelity, perceptual quality, and downstream task performance, with competitive performance against trained task-oriented codecs and faster decoding than Diff-ICMH.

</details>


### [155] [Unlearning at Speed: Rapid Activation Dampening for Efficient Machine Unlearning in Real-World Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11433)
*Rouqaiah Al-Refai,Leo Scheubel,Philipp Terhörst*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Rapid Activation Dampening（RAD），一种面向大规模开集人脸识别的快速定向遗忘方法，通过抑制与目标身份最相关的神经元实现遗忘，并提出Forget Score（FS）作为开集遗忘评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法多针对闭集分类、小规模模型与数据设计，无法推广到采用开集设定、大规模模型与数据的真实人脸识别系统；同时现有遗忘度量与开集问题不匹配。

Method: RAD通过近似遗忘身份与保留身份之间平均神经元激活的差异，识别与目标身份最相关的神经元，并借助敏感性控制的抑制机制选择性地压制这些神经元；此外提出Forget Score（FS），在表征空间中给出遗忘的概率化解释。

Result: 在多个近期大规模人脸识别架构与数据集上的实验表明，RAD在实现强遗忘效果的同时保持了模型整体效用，并显著提升遗忘速度。

Conclusion: RAD是一种可扩展且实用的人脸识别机器遗忘方法，适用于大规模开集场景。

Abstract: Machine unlearning has become increasingly important in face recognition, where regulations like the GDPR empower users to request the removal of their personal data from deployed models. However, most existing unlearning methods are designed for closed-set classification with small-scale models and training data, and therefore do not generalize to real-world face recognition systems, which operate in an open-set setting with large-scale models and data. In this work, we propose Rapid Activation Dampening (RAD), a fast and targeted unlearning method tailored specifically for large-scale open-set face recognition. RAD approximates differences in average neuron activations between forget and retain identities, identifying and selectively suppressing neurons that are most associated with the targeted identity through a sensitivity-controlled dampening mechanism. We further propose the Forget Score (FS), which addresses the mismatch between existing unlearning metrics and open-set problems by providing a probabilistic interpretation of forgetting directly in the representation space. Experiments across several recent large-scale face recognition architectures and datasets show that RAD achieves strong forgetting while preserving overall model utility and substantially improving unlearning speed. These findings establish RAD as a scalable and practical approach to machine unlearning in face recognition systems.

</details>


### [156] [How to "train" a surgeon? Benchmarking generalist agents in surgical scene understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11437)
*Gengyuan Zhang,Xiao Han,Shijie Zhou,Abdullah Koukash,Jingpei Wu,Jindong Gu,Yuqicheng Zhu,Nassir Navab,Volker Tresp,Ghazal Ghazaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一个两阶段评估框架来评估通用多模态大模型（MLLM）的手术场景理解能力：Stage I 构建诊断性基准 SurgKnowBench，揭示预训练模型的能��边界；Stage II 构建智能体环境 SurgAgentGym，检验通用 MLLM 智能体能否在推理阶段借助受限知识检索与视觉定位工具提升手术理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估通用 MLLM 在手术场景理解上的研究受限于两点：手术数据稀缺、评估协议碎片化。因此需要一个统一的评估框架来刻画模型现有能力边界，并探索在不额外进行手术训练的前提下能否提升表现。

Method: 两阶段评估框架。Stage I：SurgKnowBench，统一整合多个公开手术数据集，跨术式、跨任务地对闭源与开源 MLLM 进行评估。Stage II：基于 Stage I 暴露的能力缺口，并受手术培训"支架式"教学启发，设计 SurgAgentGym，为通用 MLLM 智能体提供对 SurgWiki 的受限知识检索以及通用视觉定位工具的智能体化环境，以工具中介的证据使用评估推理时的手术理解能力。

Result: SurgKnowBench 显示：现有模型保留了部分视觉先验，但在关系理解、过程理解与工作流评估方面仍较弱。SurgAgentGym 显示：智能体化推理主要缩小了感知类任务的差距，而细粒度定位与专家级评估仍是持续瓶颈。

Conclusion: 工具中介的智能体化推理可在无额外手术训练的情况下部分提升手术理解，但收益集中于感知密集任务；细粒度定位与专家式评估仍是核心难点，为后续智能体化手术理解研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Evaluating general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs) for surgical scene understanding remains limited due to scarce surgical data and fragmented evaluation protocols. We address these challenges through a two-stage evaluation framework for surgical scene understanding. In Stage I, we introduce SurgKnowBench, a diagnostic benchmark that harmonizes public surgical datasets across procedures and tasks and evaluates proprietary and open-source MLLMs to map the boundary of pretrained surgical competence. SurgKnowBench reveals that current models retain partial visual priors but remain weak on relational and procedural understanding and workflow assessment. Building on this failure frontier and motivated by the scaffolded nature of surgical training, we introduce SurgAgentGym, an agentic environment where general-purpose MLLM agents can use bounded knowledge search over SurgWiki and general-purpose visual grounding for surgical tasks. SurgAgentGym evaluates whether generalist agents can improve surgical understanding at inference time through tool-mediated evidence use, without additional surgical training. Our results show that agentic inference narrows the gap mainly for perception-heavy tasks, while fine-grained localization and expert-style assessment remain persistent bottlenecks. This opens a path toward future studies of agentic surgical understanding.

</details>


### [157] [Semantically Aligned KV Cache Compression for Efficient Agentic Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11452)
*Weizhong Huang,Huaijia Kou,Tianyu Xie,Xiawu Zheng,Liujuan Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SemanticKV：首个利用规划器语义约束指导 KV 缓存压缩的免训练方法，用于智能体式自回归视频生成，在 0.7 压缩比下保持甚至略超全 KV 质量，实现 1.22× 加速与 3.35× 显存降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体式自回归视频生成包含语义规划、序列生成与视觉验证，随生成推进，视觉历史不断增长导致 KV 缓存膨胀，内存与注意力计算开销上升，且需对多个候选 rollout 重复承担。现有压缩方法只针对单个生成器优化，未考虑规划器的语义约束，可能把支撑所请求属性/动作的关键状态判为低优先级而被丢弃。

Method: 提出免训练的 SemanticKV：用规划器提取的文本语义约束经文本交叉注意力定位约束相关视频位置，将这些位置的自注意力查询划分为若干组；组内对注意力权重求平均、跨组取最大，使被任一约束强使用的缓存条目获得高分；在内存预算下做单次 top-k 选择，保留条的 key、value 与位置不变。对固定查询组与等条目成本，该选择可最小化最差组被丢弃注意力的可加上界。方法在各候选内独立运行，无需重训练或额外视频生成。

Result: 在 4 个自回归视频骨干、3 个基准、5/30/60 秒生成长度上评估。Causal Forcing 在压缩比 0.7 时 VBench 总分 84.10，强于最强压缩基线 84.05 与 Full KV 83.99；相对 Full KV 加速 1.22×，KV 缓存显存由 6.04 GB 降至 1.81 GB（3.35× 降低）。

Conclusion: 将规划器语义约束引入 KV 缓存保留决策，可在显著压缩显存与提升效率的同时保持乃至略微改善生成质量，说明语义对齐的压缩策略对智能体式长视频生成有效且具通用性（跨骨干与时长均有效）。

Abstract: Agentic autoregressive video generation combines semantic planning, sequential generation, and visual verification for controllable synthesis. Growing visual histories enlarge the key-value (KV) cache, increasing memory use and attention costs across candidate rollouts. Existing compression methods primarily optimize individual generators without the planner's semantic constraints, risking low priority for states supporting requested attributes or actions. We introduce SemanticKV, a training-free method for semantically aligned KV cache compression. To our knowledge, SemanticKV is the first KV cache compression method for agentic autoregressive video generation that explicitly uses planner-extracted semantic constraints to guide retention. Text cross-attention identifies constraint-related video positions, whose self-attention queries are collected into separate groups. Attention weights are averaged within each group and maximized across groups, giving each cached entry a high score when strongly used by any represented constraint. A single top-k selection retains high-scoring entries under the memory budget, leaving their keys, values, and positions unchanged. For fixed query groups and equal entry costs, this selection minimizes an additive upper bound on worst-group discarded attention. The method operates independently within each candidate without retraining or additional video generations. We evaluate four AR video backbones on three benchmarks for 5-, 30-, and 60-second generation. On Causal Forcing at a compression ratio of 0.7, SemanticKV obtains a VBench Total Score of 84.10, compared with 84.05 for the strongest compressed baseline and 83.99 for Full KV. It achieves a 1.22x speedup over Full KV and reduces KV-cache memory from 6.04 GB to 1.81 GB, a 3.35x reduction.

</details>


### [158] [ForeAct: Geometry-Guided Action Learning from World Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11500)
*Wei Lu,Jiaxi Zhang,Yunheng Wang,Taowen Wang,Yuetong Fang,Zhenhui Zhang,Shuning Zhang,Zizhao Yuan,Yixiao Feng,Zecui Zeng,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForeAct 将预训练视频世界模型改造为单次前向的表示骨干，用受下一帧相对深度监督的可学习 token 注入几何与未来感知，实现低延迟、无需深度输入与外部几何骨干的几何感知机器人操作控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将"场景几何"与"交互中的动态演化"分开处理：世界动作模型依赖迭代扩散/rectified-flow 推理，计算开销大且表示缺乏显式空间结构；显式几何建模需额外模块，增加参数量与算力；几何中心策略只恢复观测到的空间结构，缺乏对未来几何变化的显式监督。

Method: 以预训练视频世界模型为单一表示骨干，单次前向直接处理当前观测的干净 latent，去掉未来帧合成与迭代去噪；不引入独立几何骨干，而用一组可学习 token，经下一帧相对深度监督，通过双向自注意力塑造共享视频表示；几何读出头仅训练时使用，部署时移除。

Result: 无需额外大规模预训练，在 LIBERO 上平均成功率 96.6%，SimplerEnv-Bridge 上 63.5%，90 次真实世界 rollout 成功率 74.4%。

Conclusion: 将世界模型的时空先验与轻量、训练期几何监督耦合，可在近乎零额外参数与低推理延迟下获得未来感知的几何表示，验证了单次前向世界模型骨干用于实时几何感知操作的可行性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation requires representations that capture both scene geometry and its evolution during interaction. Existing approaches have largely treated these two aspects separately. World action models capture such dynamics by coupling action prediction with future video generation, but their reliance on iterative diffusion or rectified-flow inference leads to high computational cost, while their representations lack explicit spatial structure. Explicitly modeling scene geometry typically requires auxiliary modules that increase both parameter count and computational cost. Conversely, geometry-centric policies primarily recover observed spatial structure and often lack explicit supervision for anticipating future geometric changes during interaction. To address these limitations, we introduce ForeAct, which repurposes a pretrained video world model as a single-pass representation backbone for low-latency control. ForeAct directly processes the clean latent of the current observation in one forward pass, eliminating future-frame synthesis and iterative denoising while retaining the backbone's pretrained spatiotemporal priors. Rather than introducing a separate geometry backbone, ForeAct uses learnable tokens supervised by next-frame relative depth to shape the shared video representation through bidirectional self-attention. This compact interface turns the spatial knowledge already encoded in the backbone into a future-aware representation for action generation, with little parameter overhead. The lightweight geometry readout is used only during training and removed at deployment, enabling low-latency, geometry-aware control without depth input or an external geometry backbone. Without additional large-scale pretraining, ForeAct achieves 96.6% average success on LIBERO, 63.5% on SimplerEnv-Bridge, and a 74.4% success rate across 90 real-world rollouts.

</details>


### [159] [AgentCT: A Vision-Language Agent for Universal CT Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11503)
*Zhihao Chen,Qi Gao,Chuang Niu,Qiegen Liu,Yi Zhang,Hongming Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AgentCT——一个视觉语言智能体，先识别CT退化类型与严重程度，再路由到对应的专用修复模型，在Mayo 2020腹部CT数据上取得最优PSNR/SSIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT修复方法（LDCT、SVCT、MAR）常共用单一网络，但三类退化的物理机制差异显著，单一模型难以同时最优适配。

Method: 用LoRA微调的Qwen3-VL-4B作为控制器，预测退化类型与严重程度、选择专家模型并复评修复结果；LDCT专家为图像域扩散模型，SVCT与MAR专家结合投影域校正、数据一致性、可微滤波反投影与图像精修。

Result: 控制器退化类型准确率95.7%、严重程度93.9%、JSON解析率100%；给定真值标签路由下整体PSNR/SSIM为46.18 dB/0.984，优于AMIR（44.93 dB/0.976）与TAMP（44.29 dB/0.979），九种设置PSNR均最高；SVCT双域专家以4.2M参数达46.04 dB，优于AMIR单任务44.43 dB与一体式AMIR（23.5M参数）43.57 dB。

Conclusion: 结果支持按退化类型分工的CT修复与基于控制器的模型选择范式；但自动路由下的端到端评估及独立临床数据验证仍待开展。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) restoration methods often use a single network for low-dose CT (LDCT), sparse-view CT (SVCT), and metal artifact reduction (MAR), despite the distinct physics of these degradations. We propose AgentCT, a vision-language agent that identifies the degradation and routes images to task-specific restoration models. A LoRA-adapted Qwen3-VL-4B controller predicts degradation type and severity, selects a specialist, and reassesses the restored image. The LDCT specialist uses an image-domain diffusion model, whereas the SVCT and MAR specialists combine projection-domain correction, data consistency, differentiable filtered back-projection, and image refinement. We evaluated the controller and specialists separately on patient-disjoint Mayo 2020 abdominal CT data across three severity levels for each degradation. The controller achieved 95.7% accuracy for degradation type and 93.9% for severity, with a 100% JSON parse rate. Using ground-truth degradation labels for routing, AgentCT achieved an overall PSNR/SSIM of 46.18 dB/0.984, compared with 44.93 dB/0.976 for AMIR and 44.29 dB/0.979 for TAMP, and attained the highest PSNR in all nine settings. For SVCT, its dual-domain specialist achieved 46.04 dB with 4.2 million parameters, compared with 44.43 dB for single-task AMIR and 43.57 dB for all-in-one AMIR with 23.5 million parameters. These results support degradation-specific CT restoration with controller-based model selection; evaluation under automatic routing and on independent clinical data remains necessary. Code is available at https://github.com/hao1635/AgentCT.

</details>


### [160] [Reaching Is Not Finding: Map-Anchored Visual Prompting for the Last Mile of Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11510)
*Ningbo Li,Kebin Liu,Jingcan Chen,Weichen Zhang,Rong Ding,Yunhao Liu,Mo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对空中视觉语言导航中"接近目标却停错位置"的共性问题，提出即插即用模块 MAP-VP：导航器停止后将指令中的地标轮廓投影到当前视图上，再由微调的多模态大模型定位目标物体，无需重训导航器即可显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中VLN导航器（从模仿学习器到微调7B视觉语言模型）普遍存在"到达却未找到"现象：它们将无人机带到目标附近后停在错误位置。替换指令中的目标物体后，各导航器仍停在原处，说明模型学到的是"到达地标"而非"识别被描述物体"，缺少可靠的最后一步目标定位能力。

Method: 提出 MAP-VP 即插即用模块，接在任意导航器停止之后：(1) 利用基准地图提供的各地标轮廓，将指令中命名的地标绘制到无人机当前视角图像上；(2) 用微调后的4B视觉语言模型在这些地标参照下定位目标物体。训练数据由基准标注自动生成，无需人工标注；同时验证了"地标绘制"与"模型微调"二者缺一不可。

Result: 在 CityNav 上测试的六个导航器均获得显著提升且无需任何重训：两个最强训练型导航器成功率约翻倍，HETT 由25.9%升至54.6%，FlightGPT 由24.2%升至48.9%；相比之下，CityNav 官方的基于检测器的最后一步仅为 HETT 带来不足2个百分点提升。

Conclusion: 空中VLN的关键瓶颈在于最后一步的细粒度目标识别而非路径规划；通过地标可视化与参照式定位的小模型微调相结合，可在不重训导航器的前提下大幅提升成功率，说明该模块具有通用性与即插即用价值。

Abstract: Aerial vision-language navigation (VLN) asks a drone to find an object described in natural language. The description usually locates the object relative to named landmarks, such as streets and buildings. We observe that current navigators often bring the drone close to the target and then stop at the wrong place: they reach the target without finding it. This gap is shared by navigators of very different designs, from imitation learners to a fine-tuned 7B vision-language model. A simple test reveals the cause. When we replace the target object in the instruction with another one next to the same landmarks, no navigator goes to it, and most stop where they did before. Navigators learn to reach the landmarks, not to identify the described object. We therefore propose MAP-VP, a plug-and-play module for the last step. After any navigator stops, MAP-VP draws the landmarks named in the instruction onto the drone's current view, using the landmark outlines that the benchmark provides on a map. A fine-tuned 4B vision-language model then locates the target relative to these landmarks. The training data are generated automatically from the benchmark annotations. Neither drawing the landmarks nor fine-tuning helps much alone, but together they are highly effective. Without retraining any navigator, MAP-VP significantly improves all six navigators we test on CityNav. It roughly doubles the success rate of the two strongest trained navigators, raising HETT from 25.9% to 54.6% and FlightGPT from 24.2% to 48.9%, whereas CityNav's official detector-based last step adds less than two points to HETT.

</details>


### [161] [InfCoiL: Coordinated Planner-Controller Learning for Closed-Loop Physics-Based Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11517)
*Yude Zou,Yifei Yao,Yiwei Hao,Hanqing Wang,Xing Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InfCoiL：通过规划器-控制器协同学习构建的闭环物理式人-物交互框架，以形态感知流匹配规划器+统一多形态控制器+SFPO在线优化实现高层意图到物理可行接触密集行为的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划器-控制器系统或开环执行规划，或闭环但固定规划器，执行反馈无法反过来重塑规划，导致文本/目标条件下的HOI难以同时保证物理合理性与接触丰富性。

Method: 1) 形态感知的flow-matching规划器与统一多形态控制器通过共享形态接口耦合，构成自回归"规划-执行"闭环；2) 两者在同一物理rollout上交替用强化学习微调；3) 提出Semantic Flow Policy Optimization (SFPO)，对选定去噪转移施加基于异构HOI特征通道级语义协方差的随机化，并利用完整闭环执行给出组相对、序列级奖励，实现流匹配规划器在线优化。

Result: 在留出的OMOMO序列上，InfCoiL在目标达成任务成功率与物理跟踪精度上均优于开环分层基线。

Conclusion: 将规划器与控制器纳入统一闭环并联合在线优化，可有效提升物理式HOI的执行反馈利用率与生成质量。

Abstract: Text- and goal-conditioned human-object interaction (HOI) provides an intuitive interface for specifying high-level intents in physics-based character animation. Mapping such intents to physically plausible, contact-rich behaviors naturally calls for a generative planner and a physics-based controller, yet existing planner-controller systems either execute plans open-loop or close the loop while keeping the planner fixed, so execution feedback never reshapes what is planned. We present InfCoiL, a closed-loop framework for physics-based HOI from coordinated planner-controller learning. InfCoiL couples a morphology-aware flow-matching planner with a unified multi-morphology controller through a shared morphology interface in an autoregressive plan-and-execute loop, and alternately fine-tunes both components with reinforcement learning from the same physical rollouts. To optimize the flow-matching planner online, we introduce Semantic Flow Policy Optimization (SFPO), which stochasticizes selected denoising transitions with channel-wise semantic covariance over heterogeneous HOI features and assigns group-relative, sequence-level rewards from complete closed-loop executions. On held-out OMOMO sequences, InfCoiL outperforms open-loop hierarchical baselines in goal-reaching task success and physical tracking accuracy. Project page: https://infcoil.github.io/

</details>


### [162] [Dropping Tokens Without Dropping Them: Causal Attribution for Diabetic Retinopathy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11547)
*Pramath Girish Haritz,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将ViT注意力分解为满足完备性公理的contribution $c_t$与刻画因果解释的pull $p_t$，在MAPLES-DR上验证$p_t$因果忠实性最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究视觉Transformer中基于注意力的归因图能否为糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）分类提供因果解释，并澄清注意力归因中"完备性"与"因果性"之间的差异。

Method: 提出注意力原理性分解：contribution $c_t$（满足xAI完备性公理）与pull $p_t$（量化因果解释），给出二者恒等关系以刻画$c_t$失效于因果性的条件；在MAPLES-DR上通过token删除/插入评估Necessity、Sufficiency与Faithfulness Correlation，并与Attention Rollout、Grad-CAM、AttnLRP及Occlusion对比。

Result: $p_t$因果对齐最佳（FC=0.53, N=0.71%, Su=3.0%），优于Attention Rollout、Grad-CAM、AttnLRP；Occlusion充分性更优（Su=1.6%）但慢约800倍；无像素级监督下$p_t$达到HRDecoder平均精度（AP）的70%。

Conclusion: pull $p_t$与contribution $c_t$构成计算高效的归因图，可在安全攸关的医学诊断中提供可靠的因果解释。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate whether attention-based attribution maps in a vision transformer provide causal explanations for diabetic retinopathy (DR) classification. We introduce a principled decomposition of attention into two attribution maps: the contribution ($c_t$), which satisfies the completeness axiom in explainable artificial intelligence (xAI), and the pull ($p_t$), which quantifies causal explanations. We define an identity relating $c_t$ and $p_t$ that characterizes when $c_t$ fails to capture causality. We test whether each map is causally faithful on the MAPLES-DR dataset by dropping/inserting tokens, and report Necessity ($N$), Sufficiency ($Su$), and Faithfulness Correlation ($FC$). The pull $p_t$ achieves the highest causal alignment ($FC = 0.53$, $N = 0.71\%$, $Su = 3.0\%$), outperforming Attention Rollout, Grad-CAM, and AttnLRP. Occlusion retains better sufficiency ($Su = 1.6\%$) but is about 800 times slower. Furthermore, on MAPLES-DR, $p_t$ achieves $70\%$ of the average precision (AP) of HRDecoder, a state-of-the-art supervised lesion segmentation model, despite the absence of pixel-level supervision. Together, these findings show that the pull $p_t$ and the contribution $c_t$ form computationally efficient attribution maps for reliable causal interpretation in safety-critical medical diagnostics. The code and demo are available at https://github.com/pramath-haritz/dr-xai.

</details>


### [163] [NightSteps: A Night-Time Dataset and Benchmark for Pedestrian Crossing Intention Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11583)
*Sjoerd Takken,Shaodi You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建并评估了夜间行人过街意图预测基准数据集 NightSteps，基于全球六座城市的夜间行车记录仪视频，包含行人轨迹 ID 与过街行为真值标签，并用 Gemini、ChatGPT 与人工评估验证其可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人过街意图预测（PCIP）模型多在光照充足的白天条件下训练，因缺乏夜间数据，其在夜间与低光条件下的鲁棒性尚不清楚，亟需一个夜间基准数据集来支撑评估。

Method: 采集全球六座城市的夜间行车记录仪第一视角视频，覆盖多样道路条件；标注行人位置原始数据、对应 track id 及准确的过街行为真值标签；采用 Gemini、ChatGPT 与人工评估在视频片段上预测过街行为，并与初始真值标签比对。

Result: 人工评估准确率 88.17%、F1 80.21%；Gemini 准确率 60%、F1 42.50%；ChatGPT 准确率 79.13%、F1 63.64%。

Conclusion: NightSteps 可作为未来 PCIP 模型评估的基准数据集，用于检验夜间场景下的预测性能，从而提升夜间道路安全性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles use pedestrian crossing intention prediction (PCIP) to participate safely in traffic. Existing models are largely trained under favourable daytime conditions with sufficient daylight. Little is known about the robustness of these models under night-time and low-light conditions, due to the lack of datasets containing night-time footage. This paper investigates how a dataset, named NightSteps, can be constructed and evaluated as a baseline benchmark. The dataset is based on nighttime dashcam footage collected from six different cities around the world, capturing a wide variety of road conditions. The dataset consists of dashcam-recorded point-of-view footage, raw data of pedestrians' locations with corresponding 'track ids' and ground-truth labels containing accurate information about pedestrian crossing behaviour, resulting in a diverse dataset. Gemini, ChatGPT, and human assessment are used to demonstrate the usability of the dataset by predicting crossings in clips from the dataset and comparing the results with the initial ground-truth labels. These results show that human input achieves an accuracy of 88.17% and an F1-score of 80.21%. Predictions from Gemini and ChatGPT perform lower, with accuracies of 60% and 79.13% respectively, and F1-scores of 42.50% and 63.64%. The evaluation demonstrates that the NightSteps dataset can be used for future model evaluation to test the performance of PCIP, with the aim of improving road safety at night.

</details>


### [164] [FastGRT: Accelerating Training of 3D Gaussian Ray Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11586)
*Meet Banthia,Xiaoteng Liu,Hexu Zhao,Aurojit Panda,Jinyang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FastGRT 通过解耦光线遍历与混合、前向复用反向、优化梯度累加与混合 BVH 更新，在 Vulkan 上实现首个支持训练与渲染的 GRT 系统，训练加速 1.9×–4.97×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ray Tracing 能实现光栅化方法（如 3DGS）难以达到的渲染效果，但 Gaussian-based Ray Tracing（GRT）训练成本远高于 3DGS，需要降低训练开销。

Method: 分析 Gaussians 光线追踪在 GPU 上的执行过程，发现训练开销很大比例源于计算组织方式；提出 FastGRT：解耦光线遍历与混合（ray traversal 与 blending）、前向计算复用至反向传播、快速梯度累加、混合 BVH 更新策略；基于 Vulkan 实现。

Result: 据称是首个 Vulkan 上同时支持训练与渲染的 GRT 实现；相比 3DGRT，训练时间提升 1.9× 至 4.97×，同时保持原有渲染公式不变；代码开源。

Conclusion: 通过重新组织 GPU 计算与复用计算，可在不改变渲染模型的前提下显著降低 GRT 训练成本，为高效可训练的光线追踪高斯表示提供可行工程路径。

Abstract: Ray Tracing enables rendering capabilities that are difficult to achieve with rasterization-based approaches such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS). However, the training cost of Gaussian-based ray tracing (GRT) is much higher than that of 3DGS. In this paper, we study how ray tracing using Gaussians executes on GPUs and show that a considerable fraction of the training cost arises from how the computation is organized. We develop FastGRT, which decouples ray traversal from blending and reuses work done in the forward pass for the backward pass. FastGRT further incorporates optimizations such as fast gradient accumulation and a hybrid BVH update strategy to tackle other major bottlenecks in the training pipeline. We implement FastGRT in Vulkan. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first Vulkan-based GRT implementation that supports both training and rendering. Compared to 3DGRT, FastGRT improves training time by 1.9$\times$ to 4.97$\times$, while preserving the original rendering formulation. Our code is open source.

</details>


### [165] [Martine: Benchmarking Multi-View 3D Surface Reconstruction Across Viewpoint Coverage, Resolution, and Lighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11602)
*Robin Bruneau,Baptiste Brument,Frederic Giraud,Bastian Sigrist,Sascha Jecklin,Philipp Fürnstahl,Bjoern Menze,Lilian Calvet*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Martine：面向受控多视角三维物体表面重建的基准数据集，含28个物体、84视角、9568×6376高分辨率影像、精确3D真值与属性掩码，可区分亚毫米级重建差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受控采集装置广泛用于高精度多视角三维表面重建，但基准评测困难：重建精度同时取决于重建范式、物体属性与采集条件。现有基准常限制视角覆盖、分辨率或光照，导致比较偏向适配特定设定的方法；部分基准缺乏精确3D真值或经量化的图像-几何对齐，难以进行度量级表面评估。

Method: 构建Martine基准：28个物体（含透明材料与精细结构），在球冠布局下以84个视角、9568×6376像素分辨率采集，涵盖单光源与多光源两种照明条件；提供与物体无关的相机标定、精确3D真值扫描、在测试图像上量化的标定与图像-几何精度，以及按材质属性、可见性、曲率与精细结构分析误差的3D属性掩码。

Result: 在摄影测量、经典多视图立体、神经隐式表面、基于高斯泼溅的重建及多视图光度立体等范式上实验：Martine支持带参考数据精度显式控制的采集条件感知评测；可分辨亚毫米级重建差异，可见表面上最优Chamfer距离为0.058 mm。

Conclusion: Martine以高分辨率、多光照、精确真值与属性级误差分析，为受控多视角三维表面重建提供可度量、可复现的评测基准，弥补现有基准在覆盖度与真值精度上的不足。

Abstract: Controlled acquisition setups are widely used for high-accuracy multi-view 3D surface reconstruction, as they allow sensor configuration, image resolution, viewpoint distribution, and illumination to be specified. Yet benchmarking remains difficult, since reconstruction accuracy depends jointly on the reconstruction paradigm, object properties, and acquisition regime. Existing benchmarks often restrict viewpoint coverage, resolution, or illumination, which can bias comparisons towards methods suited to the chosen setting. Others lack accurate 3D ground truth or quantified image-to-geometry alignment, limiting their use for metric surface evaluation. We introduce Martine, a benchmark for controlled 3D object surface reconstruction. Martine comprises 28 objects, including transparent materials and fine structures, captured at 9568 x 6376 pixels from 84 views over a spherical cap under both single-light and multi-light regimes. It provides object-independent camera calibration, accurate 3D ground-truth scans, calibration and image-to-geometry accuracy quantified on test images, and 3D property masks for analysing errors by material properties, visibility, curvature and fine structures. Experiments across photogrammetry, classical multi-view stereo, neural implicit surfaces, Gaussian-splatting-based reconstruction, and multi-view photometric stereo show that Martine enables regime-aware evaluation with explicit control over reference-data accuracy. The benchmark resolves sub-millimetric reconstruction differences, with a best observed Chamfer distance of 0.058 mm over the evaluated visible surface.

</details>


### [166] [Rethinking Geometric Depth in Monocular 3D Object Detection: A Projection-Consistent Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11607)
*Zhihao Zhang,Abhinav Kumar,Huaizhi Qu,Tianlong Chen,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出单目3D检测中经典的深度公式 z≈f·H/h_2D 存在投影诱导的系统性偏差——估计的是可见前表面深度而非中心深度；提出MonoPCA框架，用投影一致的重构PCDepth消除该偏差，并用GeoAlign蒸馏视觉基础模型的几何表征，在四个基准上达到SoTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典几何公式 z≈f·H/h_2D 虽简洁且直接源于投影几何，但将其中的2D框高 h_2D 代入后得到的是物体可见前表面的深度，而非物体中心深度，从而产生依赖于物体尺寸的低估误差，即使输入真值也无法消除。

Method: 提出MonoPCA统一框架：核心为PCDepth，保留可测量的 h_2D，通过由透视几何推导的、感知朝向与尺寸的解析校正项来补齐到中心深度的差距，避免引入病态回归目标。由于深度与预测的物体尺寸、朝向耦合，进一步提出GeoAlign——教师-学生框架，将视觉基础模型中的几何表征迁移到轻量学生网络以增强属性感知，且不增加推理开销。

Result: 在四个挑战性基准上取得SoTA：KITTI排行榜AP_3D提升+1.44%，Omni3D_OUT提升+2.80%。

Conclusion: 单目3D检测中的前表面偏差是可解析修正的系统性问题；投影一致的重构深度结合几何表征蒸馏，能在零额外推理成本下显著提升精度。

Abstract: Monocular 3D detection (Mono3D) hinges on recovering depth from a single image, for which the geometric formulation $z \approx f \cdot H / h_{2D}$ is widely adopted due to its simplicity and direct grounding in projective geometry. We show that this formulation harbors a systematic, projection-induced bias: substituting the 2D bounding-box height $h_{2D}$ recovers the depth of an object's visible front surface rather than its center, causing size-dependent underestimation that persists even under ground-truth inputs. We address this front-surface bias with MonoPCA, a unified framework built on a projection-consistent reformulation. At its core, PCDepth retains the measurable $h_{2D}$ and closes the gap to center depth through an orientation- and size-aware correction derived from perspective geometry, eliminating the bias without introducing ill-posed regression targets. Because PCDepth couples depth to predicted object size and orientation, attribute perception becomes essential. We therefore introduce GeoAlign, a teacher-student framework that transfers geometric representations from Vision Foundation Models into a lightweight student, strengthening attribute perception at no inference cost. Across four challenging benchmarks, MonoPCA achieves SoTA performance, including a +1.44% AP$_{3D}$ gain on the KITTI leaderboard and +2.80% on Omni3D$_{OUT}$. Code is available at https://github.com/alanzhangcs/MonoPCA.

</details>


### [167] [MICE: Multi-animal Interaction Context Encoder - A Hierarchical Foundation Model for Mouse Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11644)
*Yen-Shuo Su,Weihan Li,Anqi Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MICE 是一个面向小鼠行为的分层自监督基础模型，通过个体编码器与社会交互编码器联合在六个姿态数据集上预训练，单一权重可适配任意群体规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督姿态模型存在三方面不足：对每只动物独立编码而丢弃交互信号；硬编码动物数量；每次仅在一个数据集上预训练，难以覆盖攻击、求偶、爬跨、互相理毛等本质多动物的社会行为。

Method: 提出 MICE（Multi-animal Interaction Context Encoder）：分层个体编码器在多时间尺度上捕捉单鼠运动学特征；Perceiver 风格的社会编码器借助可学习潜在码本，使参数量与动物数量解耦，从而无需重训练即可服务任意组群规模；在六个姿态语料（1–4 只、7–27 个关键点）上联合预训练。

Result: 在覆盖六个数据集的四种评测协议中，MICE 达到或超过既有基于关键点的基线；逐层探针表明社会编码阶段在个体编码器基础上逐步提升特征可分性。

Conclusion: 分层+社会交互建模的联合预训练是构建通用小鼠行为基础模型的可行路径，码本机制实现了对群体规模的泛化；代码、权重与数据已开源。

Abstract: Quantifying mouse behavior from pose is a foundational tool in neuroscience, ethology, drug discovery, and animal welfare. Self-supervised foundation models are a natural fit, but a foundation model for mouse behavior must handle social behavior - many behaviors that matter (aggression, courtship, mounting, allogrooming) are inherently multi-animal. Existing self-supervised pose models fall short: they encode each animal independently and discard interaction signal, hard-code the animal count, and pretrain one dataset at a time. We introduce MICE (Multi-animal Interaction Context Encoder), a hierarchical foundation model for mouse behavior that pretrains jointly on six mouse-pose corpora spanning one to four mice and 7 to 27 keypoints. MICE combines a hierarchical individual encoder capturing per-mouse kinematics at multiple temporal scales with a Perceiver-style social encoder whose learned latent codebook decouples parameter count from animal count, so a single checkpoint serves any group size without retraining. Across four evaluation protocols spanning six datasets, MICE matches or exceeds prior keypoint-based baselines, and layer-wise probing shows the social stage incrementally improves discriminability beyond the individual encoder. Code, weights, and data are available at https://github.com/eric0708/MICE.

</details>


### [168] [SurfScaff3D: Bridging Geometric and Semantic Representations for LLM-based 3D Object Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11678)
*Anay Loya,Rishi Gupta,Arnav Samal,Anirban Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出层级面片骨架（Hierarchical Patch Scaffold, HPS）表示及其框架 SurfScaff3D，通过语义化、可解释的部件级抽象弥合LLM原生推理与传统3D参数化之间的错配，实现无需微调、开放域、可编辑的文/图到3D生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量3D内容创作依赖专业工具与经验，AI辅助创作生态需要易用直观的3D生成系统。LLM虽提供新思路，但现有3D表示与语言推理天然不兼容：现有方法要么依赖昂贵微调，要么引入强结构先验，牺牲表达力并排除有机形状。作者将LLM原生推理与常规3D参数化之间的不匹配视为LLM驱动3D生成的核心瓶颈。

Method: 提出HPS表示：以语义明确、可解释的部件级抽象为基本单元，继承B样条面片（B-spline patch）的几何灵活性，同时暴露便于基础模型开放世界能力调用的接口；层级部件级语义标签支持任意粒度的自然语言定向编辑；每面片显式控制表面细节。基于HPS构建交互式框架SurfScaff3D，支持文本与图像到3D生成。

Result: 与当前最先进的LLM基3D生成方法的大量实验对比显示，方法在几何保真度、语义对齐以及跨多样物体类别的灵活编辑方面表现优异。

Conclusion: HPS作为与LLM推理对齐的3D表示，可在不施加限制性几何先验、不进行昂贵微调的前提下实现富有表现力、可编辑、开放域的3D生成，为LLM驱动的3D创作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Creating high-quality 3D content has traditionally required specialist expertise and experience with complex tools. The growing ecosystem of AI-assisted creativity demands accessible and intuitive 3D generation systems. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) offer promising directions, yet extending their generative capabilities to 3D remains fundamentally challenging because existing 3D representations are not naturally compatible with language-based reasoning. Current approaches either depend on expensive fine-tuning or impose restrictive structural priors that sacrifice expressiveness and exclude organic shapes. We argue that the mismatch between LLM-native reasoning and conventional 3D parameterizations is a core bottleneck of LLM-driven 3D generation. To address this, we introduce the Hierarchical Patch Scaffold (HPS), a representation designed around semantically meaningful, interpretable part-level abstractions that are naturally understood by both humans and LLMs, enabling expressive, editable, and open-domain 3D generation without imposing restrictive geometric priors or expensive fine-tuning.
  Building upon HPS, we introduce SurfScaff3D, an interactive framework for text and image-to-3D generation. HPS inherits the flexibility of B-spline patches, while exposing an interface that leverages open-world capabilities of foundation models to reliably generate 3D. Its hierarchical part-level semantic labels enable targeted, natural-language edits at any level of granularity without re-running generation. Further, HPS offers explicit per-patch control over surface detail, enabling more expressive geometry capable of representing arbitrary objects. Extensive experiments against state-of-the-art LLM-based 3D generation methods demonstrate strong geometric fidelity, semantic alignment, and flexible editing across diverse object categories.

</details>


### [169] [LocateAnyMesh: Grounding 3D Mesh Queries in 2D Images with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11692)
*Chris Choy,Zhiding Yu,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LocateAnyMesh，一种以带纹理3D网格自身作为提示的视觉-语言模型，用于在图像中定位特定物体；LAM-Select版本在真实图像集和官方BOP YCB-V评测上提升定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带纹理3D网格同时编码几何与外观，是查找图像中特定资产的自然查询形式，但现有接地方法依赖渲染查询视图或视图模板匹配，且新物体往往需要针对性的微调。

Method: 用冻结的3D编码器将带颜色的表面点映射为紧凑token序列，经可学习连接器与图像token、指令token拼接，构成PPAT网格token。评测两种接地模型：LAM-Select选择图像区域候选框，LAM-Direct直接回归坐标。另做网格编码器对比实验，并用LAM-Direct研究表面颜色的作用。

Result: LAM-Select在所报告的真实图像数据集及官方BOP YCB-V评测上提升了定位效果；新增物体仅需一次编码器前向、无需物体级微调；单种子LAM-Select延续实验提供了指向与仅网格描述的证据。

Conclusion: 结果支持将网格token作为查询表示，但仍存在局限：存在性判定、身份绑定、候选框覆盖度与描述保真度。

Abstract: Textured 3D meshes specify object geometry and appearance, making them a natural query for finding a particular asset in an image. We introduce LocateAnyMesh, a vision-language model that uses the mesh itself as part of its prompt. A frozen 3D encoder maps colored surface points to a compact token sequence, which a learned connector places alongside image and instruction tokens. We evaluate two grounding models with PPAT mesh tokens: LAM-Select selects image-region proposals, while LAM-Direct predicts coordinates directly. Neither requires rendered query views or view-template matching. New objects require a single encoder pass and no object-specific fine-tuning. LAM-Select improves localization on the reported real-image suites and the official BOP YCB-V evaluation. Secondary experiments compare mesh encoders, and a LAM-Direct study isolates surface color. A single-seed LAM-Select continuation supplies pointing and mesh-only description evidence without replacing the reported localization model. The results support mesh tokens as a query representation, with remaining limitations in presence decisions, identity binding, proposal coverage, and descriptive fidelity.

</details>


### [170] [SurgTAP-3D: A Large-Scale Cross-Surgery Video Dataset with Contact-Aware 3D Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11697)
*Jiaxin Guo,Wenzhen Dong,Taoyu Wu,Haomin Kuang,Mengya Xu,Kejian Shi,Ziyi Wang,Tongfan Guan,Mingkai Jia,Yue Wang,Qi Dou,Yun-Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 构建 SurgTAP-3D 大规模活体伪3D手术运动追踪数据集与基准（520K帧、4,549片段、1.4M 2D/3D点轨迹），并提出将未标定单目手术视频转为结构化4D标注的统一管线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微创手术3D场景动态理解是物理智能与自主手术系统的基础，但现有公开数据集在规模与临床多样性上不足，且标注多仅针对组织或器械之一，忽略工具-组织交互，难以支撑AR引导、VLA模型与世界模型所需的大规模高质量3D监督。

Method: 采用统一的手术3D点追踪框架，将未标定单目手术视频经四个步骤转为4D标注：接触区域提示、活动器械分割、动态捆绑调整、全局4D细化；将基础模型先验适配到手术视频，输出时序一致轨迹、伪尺度几何、相机位姿与器械掩膜。数据覆盖机器人及手工操作，统一动作类别，并保留遮挡、烟雾、出血、器械快速运动等真实挑战。

Result: 在 SurgPose、SurgT 与 STIR 上建立覆盖器械与组织追踪的综合基准，完成器械位移估计、可变形表面重建与基础模型微调实验，实验与分析验证了数据集与标注框架在3D手术点追踪、重建及手术动作理解上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过可扩展的伪3D标注管线与大规模、多场景数据集，填补了手术工具-组织交互3D监督的空白，为手术VLA模型、世界模型及AR引导等下游任务提供数据与评测基础。

Abstract: Understanding 3D scene dynamics in minimally invasive surgery (MIS) is a fundamental step toward physically intelligent and autonomous surgical systems. However, existing public datasets offer limited scalability and diversity in real-world clinical scenarios. Moreover, annotations typically focus on either tissue or instruments alone, overlooking tool-tissue interactions. This gap limits applications such as augmented reality guidance and autonomous surgery driven by vision-language-action (VLA) models or world models, which demand large-scale, high-quality 3D supervision. In this work, we construct SurgTAP-3D, a large-scale in-vivo pseudo-3D surgical motion tracking dataset and benchmark, comprising 520K frames, 4,549 action clips, and 1.4M 2D/3D point trajectories from diverse robotic and manual procedures under unified action categories. To make annotation scalable, we design a unified framework for surgical 3D point tracking that converts uncalibrated monocular surgical videos into structured 4D annotations through contact-region prompting, active-tool segmentation, dynamic bundle adjustment, and global 4D refinement. This pipeline adapts foundation priors to surgical videos and produces temporally consistent trajectories, pseudo-scale geometry, camera poses, and instrument masks. The dataset captures realistic challenges inherent to surgical environments, including occlusions, smoke, bleeding, and rapid instrument motion. We establish a comprehensive benchmark on SurgPose, SurgT, and STIR, covering both instrument and tissue tracking. We demonstrate instrument displacement estimation, deformable surface reconstruction, and foundation-model fine-tuning. Extensive experiments and analysis demonstrate the effectiveness of our dataset and annotation framework for 3D surgical point tracking, reconstruction, and surgical action understanding. Homepage: https://wrld.github.io/surgtap-3d/

</details>


### [171] [Correctness Metric Matters: NWD-Based Calibration Loss for Tiny Person Detection in UAV Infrared Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11735)
*Tian Zhang,Zhirong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对无人机红外图像中极小目标检测的置信度校准问题，论文指出以IoU硬阈值为正确性定义的二值预测校准（BPC）在几何上不适定，提出用连续、尺度不变的归一化Wasserstein距离（NWD）替代IoU作为校准目标，在HIT-UAV上显著降低LaECE且mAP不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
