Motivation: 现有检测校准损失从框回归继承"正确性"定义，即IoU对硬阈值。在无人机红外图像中人目标占图像不足0.1%，同样像素误差随目标缩小被更重惩罚，导致BPC反而压制本应高的置信度，校准目标几何不适定。

Method: 将BPC中的IoU目标替换为连续、尺度不变的归一化Wasserstein距离（NWD）作为校准目标，无需推理期映射层；另设独立center-offset anchor验证增益来源；并比较在NWD之上叠加验证集拟合的isotonic映射的效果，分析两类修正作用于同一误差的不同成分。

Result: HIT-UAV上，BPC+IoU使图像级LaECE从0.0689升至0.0761（Low）、0.0649升至0.0733（Mid）、0.0653升至0.0682（High SCR）。NWD目标在三组上均显著降低LaECE_NWD（p<10^-7），mAP基本不变（0.9356 vs 0.9347）；改进在较宽的校准权重平台上成立；叠加isotonic映射可进一步降低校准误差。

Conclusion: 以IoU为校准目标对微小目标不适定，NWD目标构成真实的几何修正且不损害检测精度、无需推理期映射层。该指标级修正存在可测边界：训练分布中的目标-背景热配对、以及逐图检测数异质下的检测级聚合，均超出其作用范围，二者被独立量化。

Abstract: Detection calibration aligns predicted confidence with empirical correctness. Existing calibration losses inherit their correctness definition from box regression, specifically IoU against a hard threshold. We show that this target is geometrically ill-posed for tiny objects in UAV infrared imagery, where persons occupy less than 0.1\% of the image. The same pixel error is penalized more heavily as the target shrinks, so binary prediction calibration (BPC) actively suppresses confidences that should be high. On HIT-UAV, BPC with an IoU target degrades image-level localization-aware ECE (LaECE) from 0.0689 to 0.0761, 0.0649 to 0.0733, and 0.0653 to 0.0682 across Low, Mid, and High signal-to-clutter ratio (SCR) groups. We replace the IoU target with a continuous, scale-invariant normalized Wasserstein distance (NWD). The NWD target reduces LaECE\(_{\mathrm{NWD}}\) relative to BPC+IoU on all three groups (\(p<10^{-7}\)) with mAP neutral (0.9356 vs.\ 0.9347), and an independent center-offset anchor confirms that the gain is a genuine geometric correction. The improvement holds across a broad plateau of calibration weights. The NWD formulation requires no inference-time mapping layer. If a validation-fitted isotonic map is applied on top of it, calibration is lowered further, and the two corrections act on different components of the same error. The metric-level remedy has a measurable boundary. Target-background thermal pairing in the training distribution and detection-level aggregation under per-image detection-count heterogeneity lie outside it. Both are quantified independently of the NWD target.

</details>


### [172] [Dense Flow from Adaptive Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11738)
*Cheng-Lin Hsieh,Stella X. Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FATE，将匹配尺度与光流分辨率解耦：用自适应token支持匹配，再复用同一像素-令牌关联重建稠密光流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如WAFT）在相同粗网格上做匹配与估计，再经学习上采样恢复稠密流，未显式建立“匹配的视觉支撑”与“稠密流重建”之间的联系；且在遮挡/出界等无直接匹配区域误差占比极高。

Method: 核心洞见是稠密像素级光流并不需要同等稠密的匹配。FATE使token支持在连贯区域内扩张、在运动边界附近收缩，共享的像素-to-token关联既用于特征池化匹配，也用于由token运动重建像素流；流监督联合训练像素分组、token匹配与流估计，无需分组标签。

Result: 在FlyingChairs/FlyingThings3D训练后，于Sintel Final与KITTI取得最高零样本精度；可见像素上连贯区域内与运动边界附近精度均提升；最大增益出现在无直接匹配处——遮挡或出界像素约占有效像素10%，却占WAFT总误差约50%。

Conclusion: 通过像素-to-token分组的自适应对应关系，可同时实现可靠匹配与精确像素级光流，并提升对未见真实场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Optical flow requires reliable matching from sufficient visual context and precise motion at every pixel. Existing methods such as WAFT match and estimate flow on the same coarse grids, then recover dense flow through learned upsampling, without explicitly linking visual support for matching to dense flow reconstruction. Our key insight is that dense pixel-level flow does not require equally dense matching. We decouple correspondence scale from flow resolution by adapting the size and shape of spatial supports to image structure for reliable matching, while reusing these supports for precise dense flow reconstruction. We realize this in Flow from Adaptive Token Estimation (FATE), whose token supports expand within coherent regions and contract near motion boundaries. Shared pixel-to-token associations pool features for matching and reconstruct pixel flow from token motions. Flow supervision jointly trains pixel grouping, token matching, and flow estimation without grouping labels, allowing FATE to anticipate which pixels are likely to move together from appearance alone. Trained on FlyingChairs/FlyingThings3D, FATE achieves the highest zero-shot accuracy on Sintel Final and KITTI, a critical setting given scarce real-world flow labels. For visible pixels, FATE improves accuracy within coherent regions, and more near motion boundaries. Its largest gains occur where direct matches are unavailable: Occluded or out-of-frame pixels account for roughly 10% of valid pixels but 50% of WAFT's total error. Experiments show that adaptive correspondence through pixel-to-token grouping enables reliable matching and precise pixel-level flow, improving generalization to unseen real scenes.

</details>


### [173] [GeoPilot: Diagnosis-Guided Interventions and Geometric Evaluation for Aerial Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11751)
*Pengyu Yin,Jie Pan,Junxiang Tan,Mengqi Fu,Kun Cheng,Yifan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GeoPilot 工作流，将重建诊断与先验观测转化为可执行的程序或相机配置干预，用 LLM 提议改动、数值工具重建、外部评估器度量几何，并在 Metashape 影像上检验共享内参假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中遥感影像重建网格与参考几何不一致时，缺乏对重建过程的诊断与"该改什么"的可执行决策支持。

Method: GeoPilot 工作流：LLM 提出干预方案，数值工具重建网格，外部评估器在给定坐标与参考协议下测量几何；主实验在 224 张 Metashape 栅格开发影像上检验预注册的共享内参假设，另做参考辅助对齐（棋盘格样本分离拟合/评估）与发布商去畸变影像上的固定相机匹配对比。

Result: 共享内参在全部六项原生坐标指标上改善，平均表面—参考距离降低 7.04%；参考辅助对齐在独立棋盘格样本上反转平均距离排名，该收益仅限原生坐标系交付。去畸变影像匹配对比中固定发布相机在六项指标上全胜，平均距离降低 12.11%。第二场景 327 张影像三次端到端运行均产出网格与六指标评估。

Conclusion: 每组配置仅一次重建，结果属条件性证据；相机处理方式的选择依赖坐标与参考协议，需结合评估协议解读收益。

Abstract: When a mesh reconstructed from aerial remote-sensing imagery disagrees with reference geometry, deciding what to change requires a diagnosis of the reconstruction process. We present GeoPilot, a workflow that turns diagnostics and prior observations into executable program or camera-configuration interventions. A large language model proposes the change, numerical tools reconstruct the mesh, and an external evaluator measures its geometry under a stated coordinate and reference protocol. The main case tests a shared-intrinsics hypothesis proposed before the comparison. On 224 Metashape-raster development images, shared intrinsics improved all six native-coordinate measures and reduced mean surface-to-reference distance by 7.04%. Reference-assisted alignment fitted and evaluated on separate within-scene checkerboard samples reversed the mean-distance ranking, limiting that gain to native-frame delivery. A separate matched comparison on publisher-undistorted images favored fixed published cameras on all six measures, reducing mean distance by 12.11%; the cameras retain upstream image and laser-scanning adjustment information. With one reconstruction per configuration, these comparisons provide conditional evidence for camera handling. Three end-to-end runs each delivered a mesh and six-metric evaluation from all 327 images of a second development scene.

</details>


### [174] [SpinSCI: Mask-Conditioned Spatial-Temporal Spin Transformer for Video Snapshot Compressive Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11753)
*Seungki Hong,Zhishuai Zhang,Taekwang Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpinSCI——一种掩码条件化的时空自旋Transformer，通过将注意力logits与传感信息融合为有符号自旋耦合进行物理引导的视频快照压缩成像重建，在PSNR略优或持平的同时显著降低参数量、FLOPs、延迟与内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频快照压缩成像（SCI）需从单帧时空编码测量中恢复多帧，现有Transformer类方法参数量大、计算与显存开销高，且未充分将传感物理模型融入网络推理。

Method: 提出SpinSCI，核心为掩码条件化时空自旋Transformer及其掩码条件化Ising精炼块（MC-IRB）：将注意力logits与传感信息结合构造有符号自旋耦合，采用有限平均场更新门控残差特征精炼，并通过物理引导初始化与末端数据一致性约束嵌入测量模型。

Result: 相比HiSViT_9，在小规模灰度、中规模彩色、大规模彩色基准上平均PSNR分别提升0.51/0.23/0.04 dB，参数量减少16.9%，FLOPs减少21.0–21.6%，延迟降低17.1–49.9%，峰值内存降低62.3–63.3%；相比DU-9stg，PSNR差异不超过0.13 dB，FLOPs减少28.2–28.7%，推理延迟降低64.6–71.7%，峰值内存降低30.1–35.0%；真实数据集定性对比可恢复细节与纹理。

Conclusion: SpinSCI在保持甚至略微提升重建质量的同时大幅压缩计算与内存开销，验证了Ising自旋耦合式物理引导建模在视频SCI中的有效性，并开源了代码与参考权重。

Abstract: Video snapshot compressive imaging (SCI) recovers multiple frames from one spatiotemporally coded measurement. This paper proposes SpinSCI, a mask-conditioned Spatial-Temporal Spin Transformer whose Mask-Conditioned Ising Refinement Block (MC-IRB) combines attention logits and sensing information to form signed spin couplings. Finite mean-field updates gate residual feature refinement. Physics-guided initialization and final data consistency incorporate the measurement model. Compared with HiSViT$_9$, SpinSCI achieves average PSNR gains of 0.51 dB, 0.23 dB, and 0.04 dB on small-scale gray, mid-scale color, and large-scale color benchmarks, respectively, with 16.9% fewer parameters, 21.0-21.6% fewer FLOPs, 17.1-49.9% lower latency, and 62.3-63.3% lower peak memory. Relative to DU-9stg, average PSNR differs by at most 0.13 dB across these benchmarks, with 28.2-28.7% fewer FLOPs, 64.6-71.7% lower inference latency, and 30.1-35.0% lower peak memory. Qualitative comparisons on the real benchmark further show recovery of fine details and textures. Code and reference checkpoints are available at https://github.com/skethz/SpinSCI.

</details>


### [175] [Calibrated Alarm Thresholds for Compact Fire and Smoke Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11757)
*Vikas Sandireddy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在D-Fire测试集上以图像级（报警/不报警）评估两种轻量检测器，比较三种将框检测转为单张图像报警分数的规则，并用split conformal从留出图像中选择报警阈值，使漏检率低于目标值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量目标检测器（如YOLOv8n）常用于低功耗设备的火灾烟雾监测，但现有评估多采用框级mAP指标，且决策依赖0.25/0.5等固定置信度阈值；而实际监控系统每张图像只做一次报警决策，固定阈值会导致不同检测器漏检率差异巨大，缺乏可控的漏检保证。

Method: 在D-Fire测试集上进行图像级评估：比较三种将框级检测聚合为单图报警分数的简单规则；采用split conformal流程，在留出图像上校准报警阈值，使期望的火灾/烟雾漏检图像比例低于设定目标。

Result: 固定阈值0.5下，两种检测器漏检率分别为17.3%和8.4%；校准阈值在10%、5%、1%三个漏检目标下对两种检测器与三种规则均能达标。5%目标时，无火烟图像误报率保持在1.2%–3.0%；仅含烟雾的图像在整体目标满足时漏检率仍低于目标；并总结了主要失效情形。

Conclusion: 图像级、共形校准的阈值选择能在不同检测器与聚合规则下稳定满足指定漏检率目标，并保持较低误报率，比固定置信度阈值更适合实际火灾烟雾报警系统的评估与部署。

Abstract: Compact object detectors such as YOLOv8n are a common choice for camera-based fire and smoke detection on low-power devices. These detectors are usually evaluated with box-level metrics such as mean average precision, and their outputs are turned into decisions with a fixed confidence threshold such as 0.25 or 0.5. A fire monitoring system, however, makes one decision per image: raise an alarm or not. In this paper we evaluate two publicly available compact detectors on the D-Fire test set at the image level. We compare three simple rules for turning box detections into one alarm score per image, and we use a split conformal procedure to choose the alarm threshold from held-out images so that the expected fraction of missed fire and smoke images stays below a chosen target. We find that the same fixed threshold leads to very different miss rates for the two detectors, 17.3 and 8.4 percent at a threshold of 0.5, while the calibrated threshold meets miss-rate targets of 10, 5 and 1 percent for both detectors and all three rules. At a 5 percent target, the false alarm rate on images without fire or smoke stays between 1.2 and 3.0 percent. We also find that smoke-only images stay below the target even when the overall target is met, and we describe the main failure cases.

</details>


### [176] [LangDepth: Vision-Language Scene Priors for Semantic Binning and Cross-Domain Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11763)
*Reyhaneh Ahani Manghotay,Jie Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LangDepth（SemanticBins）将冻结CLIP+概念键生成的场景描述符经场景先验模块（SPM）映射为场景条件的度量深度区间，作为稠密解码前的全局范围锚点，配合DINOv2-L/FPN与逐像素分箱分配网络，在NYU/KITTI等五个数据集上取得强跨域单目度量深度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内与室外驾驶场景的度量深度范围差异巨大，但多数深度解码器仅从视觉特征中隐式推断这种全局尺度结构。作者质疑场景级语义结构能否作为上游先验，在稠密预测之前塑形度量输出空间。

Method: 冻结CLIP编码器+可学习的概念键银行（场景/几何导向）将图像特征压缩为紧凑场景描述符（无需逐图文本或外部captioner）；SPM将描述符映射为场景条件的度量深度区间作为全局范围锚点；DINOv2-L+轻量FPN提取稠密视觉特征；逐像素分配网络（PPAN）将像素分配到场景预设的bin结构；置信度门控融合稠密估计与SPM先验。仅在NYU Depth V2与KITTI联合监督训练，无网络规模深度监督或伪标签。

Result: 单模型δ1：NYU 0.960、KITTI 0.960、DIODE indoor 0.887、iBims-1 0.946、SUN RGB-D 0.861。SPM预测出紧凑的室内范围与显著更宽的KITTI室外范围；门控在室外场景转为SPM主导；扰动路由的场景上下文导致KITTI与iBims-1大幅性能下降。

Conclusion: 场景条件输出空间是跨域单目度量深度估计的一种实用且可解释的机制。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation has advanced through large-scale pretraining and strong visual backbones, yet metric depth remains sensitive to scene-level scale. Indoor rooms and outdoor driving scenes occupy very different depth ranges, but most depth decoders infer this global structure implicitly from visual features. We ask whether scene-level semantic structure can instead act as an upstream prior that shapes the metric output space before dense prediction.
  We present LangDepth (SemanticBins), a monocular depth architecture that couples strong visual features with a scene-conditioned output space. A frozen CLIP encoder and a learnable bank of scene- and geometry-oriented concept keys convert image features into a compact scene descriptor, without requiring per-image text or an external captioner. A Scene Prior Module (SPM) maps this descriptor to a scene-conditioned metric depth interval, providing a global range anchor before dense decoding. In parallel, a DINOv2-L backbone with a lightweight FPN extracts dense visual features, and a Per-Pixel Assignment Network (PPAN) assigns pixels to the scene-prescribed bin structure. A confidence gate fuses the dense visual estimate with the SPM prior.
  We train LangDepth jointly on NYU Depth V2 and KITTI under a modest supervised depth budget, without web-scale depth supervision or pseudo-labelled depth data. A single model achieves delta_1 = 0.960 on NYU, 0.960 on KITTI, 0.887 on DIODE indoor, 0.946 on iBims-1, and 0.861 on SUN RGB-D. Beyond aggregate accuracy, the SPM predicts compact indoor ranges and much wider outdoor KITTI ranges, the gate becomes SPM-dominant on outdoor scenes, and perturbing the routed scene context causes large drops on KITTI and iBims-1. These results support scene-conditioned output spaces as a practical and interpretable mechanism for cross-domain monocular depth estimation.

</details>


### [177] [Towards Rotation-Only Imaging Geometry: Multi-View Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11770)
*Xinrui Li,Qi Cai,Yuanxin Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GRAM，直接从初始相对旋转与内点匹配最小化边级旋转-only代数残差估计全局旋转，将两视图相对位姿优化与旋转平均合并为单阶段；并扩展出多视图旋转-only重投影误差优化MORE与解析平移恢复ART，配合LiGT约束与加权最小二乘得到全局平移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局SfM先对单对图像精化相对位姿再做旋转平均，在旋转初始化阶段未显式将特征级约束与多视图一致性联合，导致两阶段流程割裂、精度与鲁棒性受限。

Method: 1) GRAM：以初始相对旋转和内点匹配为输入，最小化边级旋转-only代数残差，一体化求解全局旋转；2) MORE：将旋转-only成像几何推广到多视图，用旋转-only重投影误差进行非线性全局旋转精化；3) ART：从LiGT约束解析导出局部尺度的三元组平移，再用加权最小二乘恢复全局平移。

Result: 在合成数据与真实数据实验中，所提方法在精度、鲁棒性和效率方面均得到验证，表现出良好性能。

Conclusion: 将相对位姿优化与旋转平均统一为单一旋转初始化阶段，并辅以多视图旋转精化与解析平移恢复，可构成精度、鲁棒性与效率兼顾的全局SfM流水线。

Abstract: Global structure-from-motion (SfM) commonly refines relative poses on individual image pairs and then estimates global rotations by rotation averaging. Pairwise refinement does not explicitly combine feature-level constraints with multi-view consistency during rotation initialization. We introduce Global Rotations from Aggregated Matches (GRAM), which estimates global rotations directly from initial relative rotations and inlier matches by minimizing edge-wise rotation-only algebraic residuals. GRAM replaces two-view relative-pose optimization and rotation averaging with a single stage. We further extend rotation- only imaging geometry to multiple views and develop Multi-view Optimization with Rotation-only Reprojection Error (MORE) for nonlinear global rotation refinement. Analytical Rotation-guided Translation (ART) derives locally scaled triplet translations from Linear Global Translation (LiGT) constraints and recovers global translations using weighted least squares. Synthetic and real-world experiments demonstrate the accuracy, robustness, and efficiency of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [178] [FMA-Net: Flexible Modality Adaptation for Robust Multimodal Face Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11790)
*Yuhao Gao,Yichen Shi,Zitong Yu,Hongyang Wang,Hongrui Li,Ruixin Zhang,Shouhong Ding,Jun Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FMA-Net统一框架，通过指令引导的隐空间翻译补偿缺失模态，实现灵活/不完整模态下的多模态人脸呈现攻击检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态fPAD依赖RGB、深度、红外互补线索提升鲁棒性，但部署时模态常缺失或不完整；现有灵活模态方法采用零填充或直接省略模态，会丢弃跨模态信息并引入模态依赖偏差。

Method: FMA-Net包含四个模块：(1) Face Modality Instruct Translator (FMIT)，从RGB输入估计目标模态观测；(2) 上下文感知类别增强，用可学习文本提示作为模态不变语义锚点；(3) Facial Feature Adapter (F2A)，融合全局人脸结构与局部攻击相关线索；(4) Synthetic-to-Real Face Domain Alignment (SR-FDA)，缩小翻译模态与真实模态间特征差异。

Result: 在WMCA与PADISI-Face上验证。WMCA 70%模态缺失率下，四个域内协议平均ACER为3.22%，最强基线为4.24%；深度与红外完全缺失时ACER为5.23%，最佳对比结果为5.99%。跨域实验显示合成到真实对齐在数据集偏移下存在局限。

Conclusion: FMA-Net可作为灵活与不完整模态条件下fPAD的实用方案；SR-FDA收益依赖缺失模式与数据域，跨域场景仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Multimodal face presentation attack detection (fPAD) improves robustness using complementary RGB, depth, and infrared (IR) cues, but deployment remains challenging when modalities are unavailable or incomplete. Existing flexible-modality methods mainly use zero-filling or modality omission, potentially discarding cross-modal information and introducing modality-dependent bias. We propose the Flexible Modality Adaptation Network (FMA-Net), a unified framework that compensates for missing modalities through instruction-guided latent-space translation. The Face Modality Instruct Translator (FMIT) estimates target modality observations from RGB inputs, while context-aware category enhancement uses learnable text prompts as modality-invariant semantic anchors. A Facial Feature Adapter (F2A) integrates global facial structure and local spoof-relevant cues to enhance discriminative representations. Synthetic-to-Real Face Domain Alignment (SR-FDA) reduces feature discrepancies between translated and real modalities, with benefits depending on the missing-modality pattern and data domain. Experiments on WMCA and PADISI-Face validate the framework. On WMCA with a 70% modality missing ratio, FMA-Net achieves a mean ACER of 3.22% across four intra-domain missing-modality protocols, compared with 4.24% for the strongest baseline by the same criterion. When both depth and IR are entirely unavailable, it achieves 5.23% ACER, compared with 5.99% for the best competing result. Cross-domain experiments also reveal limitations of synthetic-to-real alignment under dataset shifts. These results support FMA-Net as a practical approach to fPAD with flexible and incomplete modalities.

</details>


### [179] [Benchmarking CNNs, Vision Transformers, and Foundation Models for Explainable Brain Tumor MRI Classification: Calibration, Decision Analysis, and External Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11793)
*Akwasi Asare*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较6种深度学习架构在脑肿瘤MRI四分类任务上的表现，发现公开数据集内部准确率（94.5%–95.6%）无法区分架构优劣，但去重后的外部测试集（77.6%–95.0%）可显著区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑肿瘤MRI分类研究通常仅以准确率对比模型，且对公开数据集独立性缺乏检验，可能导致数据泄漏与结论失真。

Method: 在7,216张公开MRI图像上进行四分类，比较6种架构：EfficientNetV2-M、ConvNeXt-V2-Tiny、Swin-V2-S、ViT-L/16、DINOv2-L、MedSigLIP-448；评估准确率、校准、可解释性、集成与决策曲线，所有拟合量仅在验证集估计；进行像素级重复筛选与模型随机化检验。

Result: 36%的测试图像在训练集中存在近重复；测试准确率94.5%–95.6%，Holm校正后无成对差异；在773张无重复且无共同患者的测试图像上降至90.3%–91.7%；集成未优于最佳单模型；归因图通过随机化检验但对专家标注肿瘤定位较差；在1,675张外部BDNeuro-MRI图像上准确率77.6%（MedSigLIP-448）至95.0%（ViT-L/16），15组成对比较中12组显著。

Conclusion: 公开数据集内部准确率不足以区分架构，而去重后的外部测试能有效区分；数据集独立性与外部验证对模型评价至关重要。

Abstract: Deep learning models for brain tumor MRI classification are usually compared by accuracy alone, on public datasets whose independence is rarely checked. We benchmarked six architectures: two convolutional networks (EfficientNetV2-M and ConvNeXt-V2-Tiny), two vision transformers (Swin-V2-S and ViT-L/16), and two foundation models (DINOv2-L and MedSigLIP-448), for four-class classification of 7,216 public MRI images. We assessed accuracy, calibration, explainability, ensembles, and decision curves, with every fitted quantity estimated on validation data only. Pixel-verified duplicate screening showed that 36% of the test images had a near-duplicate in the training data. Test accuracy ranged from 94.5% to 95.6%, with no pairwise difference after Holm correction, and fell to 90.3% to 91.7% on the 773 test images free of duplicates and shared patients. Ensembles did not outperform the best single model. Attribution maps passed a model-randomization test but localized expert-annotated tumors poorly. On 1,675 external BDNeuro-MRI images that did not duplicate the training data, accuracy ranged from 77.6% (MedSigLIP-448) to 95.0% (ViT-L/16), and 12 of 15 pairwise differences were significant. Internal accuracy on this public collection therefore did not distinguish the architectures, whereas the duplicate-filtered external test did.

</details>


### [180] [Direct Trajectory Optimization for Inference-Time Alignment of Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11821)
*Huixuan Zhang,Xiaojun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的 Direct Trajectory Optimization (DTO)，将扩散采样轨迹（中间去噪分数与采样噪声）作为优化变量直接对齐奖励函数，避免反向传播穿过去噪网络，配合 Reward-Optimized Score Guidance 缓解分布外问题并给出奖励提升的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型与奖励函数的对齐是可控制生成的关键挑战；现有方法依赖优化模型输入或微调去噪网络，需要对大型去噪模型做计算昂贵的反向传播。

Method: 训练无关框架 DTO：直接优化扩散模型的采样轨迹，把中间去噪分数与采样的噪声作为优化变量，从而无需经由去噪网络求梯度；并引入 Reward-Optimized Score Guidance，在优化后的分数与预训练模型预测之间做插值以稳定采样，在受限分布下提供可证明的奖励提升保证。

Result: 在两个基准、三个扩散模型上，DTO 提升了对齐性能，优于现有推理时对齐方法，同时保持高效率。

Conclusion: 轨迹层面的优化是高效且有效的扩散模型对齐的有前景方向。

Abstract: Alignment of text-to-image diffusion models with reward functions is a key challenge in controllable generation. Existing approaches typically rely on optimizing model inputs or fine-tuning denoising networks, which requires computationally expensive backpropagation through large denoising models. In this work, we propose Direct Trajectory Optimization (DTO), a training-free framework that performs alignment by directly optimizing the sampling trajectory of diffusion models. Specifically, DTO treats intermediate denoising scores and sampled noise as the optimization variables, eliminating the need for gradients through the denoising network. To mitigate the out-of-distribution problem, we introduce Reward-Optimized Score Guidance, which interpolates between the optimized scores and the pretrained model predictions to stabilize sampling, while providing a provable theoretical guarantee of reward improvement under constrained distributions. Empirically, DTO improves alignment performance across three diffusion models on two benchmarks, outperforming existing inference-time alignment methods while maintaining high efficiency. These results suggest that trajectory-level optimization is a promising direction for efficient and effective diffusion model alignment.

</details>


### [181] [When to Trust Adaptation: Contextual Fusion for Transferable Vision-Language Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11824)
*Zhenyuan Chen,Wenshuo Tao,Zhuo Huang,Shuo Chen,Mingming Cheng,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAF在推理时固定融合系数，消融实验应显示该策略优于动态调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源域适配能纠正预训练模型的部分错误，也会破坏另一些原本正确的预测，模型需要判断何时该信任学到的提示。

Method: CAF用冻结与提示化的视觉、文本表示配对得到四个预测分支；融合时按各分支的预测置信度及该分支所提类别在无标签目标集上的出现频率加权，更信任局部自信且受集合频率偏差影响小的预测。

Result: 无。

Conclusion: CAF固定融合系数，不做目标域自适应。

Abstract: When a vision-language model encounters a new class or domain, how much should it trust its learned prompts? Because source adaptation can correct pretrained errors on some target images while disturbing valid predictions on others, we introduce Contextual Adaptive Fusion (CAF), which retains four prediction branches by pairing frozen and prompted visual and textual representations. To arbitrate when branches disagree on an image, CAF combines each branch's predictive confidence with how frequently the branches favor its proposed class across the unlabeled target collection, placing greater trust in predictions that are locally confident yet less dominated by collection-wide frequency bias. The fusion coefficients are learned once on source data and remain fixed at inference. Trained on ImageNet, CAF achieves 69.96% average accuracy across ten cross-dataset benchmarks (1.92 percentage points above ManiPT) and remains highly competitive across four ImageNet variants, setting a new best of 52.21% on ImageNet-S. In base-to-novel transfer across eleven datasets, CAF further reaches 79.77% novel-class accuracy and an 82.81% harmonic mean. Code is available at https://github.com/zhenyuanchenai/CAF.

</details>


### [182] [End-to-End Joint Training of Super-Resolution and Compression Artifact Removal for Industrial Machine Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11831)
*Dang BuiDinh,Sang NguyenQuang,Anh NguyenPhuong,Wen-Hsiao Peng,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出端到端联合超分辨率(SR)与压缩伪影去除(CAR)的质量增强框架，统一优化人类感知与机器感知目标，在工业缺陷检测下游任务上显著优于串行SR-CAR基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备采集的工业图像常被降采样与压缩以节省带宽，带来分辨率损失与压缩伪影，严重损害缺陷检测等下游任务；现有方法将SR与CAR独立或串行处理，且主要面向人类视觉保真度而非机器感知，缺乏针对机器视觉目标的联合优化。

Method: 构建端到端统一框架，将SR与CAR整合并用统一训练策略同时优化人类感知与机器感知目标；研究固定权重与基于不确定性的动态权重两类损失函数，以平衡重建质量与机器视觉任务性能；在PCB Defect、DeepPCB、NEU-DET数据集上验证。

Result: 联合训练的模型总体优于独立训练与分阶段训练的SR-CAR流水线；在强JPEG压缩下，PCB Defect上缺陷检测mAP@50由串行SR-CAR基线的0.461提升至0.887，而PSNR仅轻微下降。

Conclusion: 联合优化对压缩感知的工业机器视觉系统至关重要，应以机器感知目标为导向设计图像质量增强流程，而非仅追求人类视觉保真度。

Abstract: In industrial machine vision, images captured by edge devices are often downsampled and compressed to reduce bandwidth, introducing resolution loss and compression artifacts that severely degrade the performance of downstream tasks, such as defect detection. Existing quality enhancement methods typically apply super-resolution (SR) or compression artifact removal (CAR) independently or in a sequential manner and are largely optimized for human visual fidelity rather than machine perception. This paper proposes an end-to-end quality enhancement framework that integrates SR and CAR with a unified training strategy that optimizes both human-perception and machine-perception objectives. We investigate fixed-weight and uncertainty-based dynamic-weight loss functions to balance reconstruction quality and machine vision task performance. Extensive experiments on PCB Defect, DeepPCB, and NEU-DET datasets show that the proposed jointly trained model generally outperforms standalone and separately trained SR-CAR pipelines. In particular, under severe JPEG compression, our method improves defect detection performance from 0.461 to 0.887 mAP@50 on PCB Defect over sequential SR-CAR baselines, with only a slight reduction in PSNR. These results demonstrate that joint optimization is critical for compression-aware industrial machine vision systems.

</details>


### [183] [Answer or Defer? Testing VLMs' Stated Confidence on Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11833)
*Amir Sabbaghziarani,Parsa Ghazvinian,Sepehr Salem,Sergey Plis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究检验能否用VLM自述置信度做阈值化"接受/转诊"决策，发现即使回顾性选阈值，在BraTS胶质瘤MRI上也没有任何模型能在5%错误率下选出非空接受集，且置信度主要反映"答案类别差异"而非"对错差异"，因此自述置信度不宜作为独立转诊信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM可用于医学影像解读，但部署时须决定哪些解读可接受、哪些需转诊临床医生；作者希望验证"对自述置信度设阈值"能否在限制已接受答案错误率的同时保持有用覆盖率，并考察其是否依赖人群分布。

Method: 8个开源权重VLM（含2个推理模型）评估3888张脑MRI切片：治疗后胶质瘤患者（BraTS 2024，标签由分割掩膜导出）与健康志愿者（IXI，全部视为阴性）。实验包括：在不同错误预算（5%/10%/20%）下扫描置信度阈值并（含回顾性最优阈值）评估接受集大小与经验错误率；在"大病灶+远端阴性"子集上对4个模型复核；将置信度变异分解为"不同预测答案之间"与"同一答案内部"两部分，并用协方差分解解释跨人群出现相反置信度-正确性关联的机制。

Result: BraTS上任何模型都无法在≤5%经验错误率下选出非空接受集，回顾性选阈值亦然，大病灶子集结论一致；10%错误预算下Gemma-4-12B仅保留24.4%切片，其余模型≤2.5%；有结果级汇总的4个模型中，合并平均置信度在不同答案之间的差异大于同一答案内正确与错误回答间的差异；20%错误预算下，对2个模型而言"全部接受tumor答案"的覆盖量超过任何置信度阈值。

Conclusion: 未经校验的自述置信度不能作为独立的延迟/转诊信号；置信度-正确性关联随人群变化，部署前须做人群特异的验证与校准。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) could support medical-image interpretation, but their use requires deciding which interpretations to accept and which images to refer to a clinician. We test whether thresholding stated confidence can retain useful coverage while limiting errors among accepted answers, and how this depends on the population. Eight open-weight VLMs, including two reasoning models, assess 3,888 brain MRI slices from post-treatment glioma patients (BraTS 2024) and healthy volunteers (IXI) for tumor or gross abnormality. BraTS labels are mask-derived; IXI slices are treated as negatives. On BraTS, no threshold selects a nonempty set with at most 5% empirical error for any model, even when chosen retrospectively. This also holds for four models evaluated on a large-lesion, distant-negative subset. At a 10% error budget, Gemma-4-12B retains 24.4% of slices and every other model at most 2.5%. To investigate this limited selection ability, we separate confidence differences between predicted answers from differences within each answer. For four models with available outcome-level summaries, pooled mean confidence differs more between answers than between correct and incorrect responses within each answer. A covariance decomposition explains how this pattern can produce opposite confidence-correctness associations across populations. At a 20% error budget, accepting every "tumor" answer covers more slices than any confidence threshold for two models. These findings caution against using unvalidated stated confidence as a standalone deferral signal and support population-specific validation before deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/amir-sbg/VLM-Answer-or-Defer.

</details>


### [184] [MDA framework: multi-architecture domain adaptation for unsupervised person re-identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11836)
*Wei Zhang,Peijun Ye,Dihu Chen,Tao Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多架构自适应的域适应方法——Mamba增强的多架构域适应框架(MDA)，通过复合算子与框架两级设计，将Mamba作为增强组件嵌入CNN与Transformer骨干，实现无监督行人重识别的灵活部署与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督行人重识别同时面临域间隙挑战，且现有方法大多仅支持单一网络架构，缺乏跨CNN与Transformer的通用性，因此需要同时解决域适应与多架构兼容问题。

Method: 在两个层面构建多架构域适应能力：第一层设计复合算子(MDA operator)，将Mamba封装为增强域适应的组件；第二层基于该算子构建Mamba增强的多架构域适应框架(MDA framework)，支持不局限于单一集成点的多样化部署策略。

Result: 在行人重识别基准上验证了框架的一致性与灵活性；单一部署配置下优于多种SOTA方法；在CNN和Transformer架构上均取得显著提升——嵌入CNN骨干时，CUHK03到Market-1501适应任务mAP提升10.2%；集成Transformer骨干时同一任务mAP提升19.8%。

Conclusion: MDA框架可作为通用增强组件灵活嵌入不同骨干网络，兼顾域适应效果与架构兼容性，为无监督行人重识别提供可复用、可迁移的多架构解决方案。

Abstract: Unsupervised person re-identification not only faces the challenge of domain gaps, but existing methods also rarely support multiple network architectures. To tackle both issues, this paper proposes a multi-architecture auto-adaptive domain adaptation method: Mamba augmented multi-architecture domain adaptation framework. Specifically, this paper constructs the multi-architecture domain adaptation capability at two levels. At the first level, we propose a composite operator(MDA operator) that encapsulates Mamba as a component for enhancing domain adaptation. At the second level, based on this operator, we propose a Mamba-enhanced multi-architecture Domain Adaptation framework(MDA framework). Comprehensive experiments on person re-identification benchmarks validate the consistency and flexibility of the proposed framework. The MDA framework supports versatile deployment strategies without being confined to a single integration point. Under a single deployment configuration, it surpasses several SOTA methods. Moreover, it demonstrates substantial performance improvements across both CNN and Transformer architectures. When embedded into a CNN backbone, the framework achieves a 10.2% mAP improvement on the adaptation task from CUHK03 to Market-1501. Integrated with a Transformer backbone, it yields a 19.8% mAP improvement on the same task.

</details>


### [185] [Aggregation-Induced Calibration Failure in Sum-Based Label Expansion for Multi-Label Facial Expression Recognition: A Diagnostic Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11843)
*Weihang Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 标签空间扩展（预测更大的联合空间再求和回原标签）是多标签表情识别的自然设计，但其失效模式此前未被刻画。论文在自建数据集（30,240 张训练图、3,460 张多标签测试图）、七个骨干网络以及公开的 RAF-DB、RAF-ML 上评估 CALE，核心结论是：决定方法成败的是聚合规则而非特征表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标签扩展（label-space expansion）被视为多标签面部表情识别（ML-FER）的直观方案，但其失效模式从未被系统表征。作者希望厘清"CALE 是否有效、失效发生在哪一步、机理为何"。

Method: （1）在自建大规模多标签数据集上，跨七个骨干网络评估 CALE；（2）对比固定阈值 0.5 下的聚合分数与无阈值的 mAP，观察跨骨干的分数波动范围来定位故障环节；（3）测量主分支与三个非主分支的激活，验证聚合规则（主分支分数 + 非主分支之和的一半）未校准这一机制假设；（4）设立两个朴素七维 sigmoid 基线（BCE、ASL）作对照；（5）对单标签 FER 的增益做配对 t 检验、Benjamini-Hochberg 校正与 Wilcoxon 检验。

Result: 固定阈值 0.5 下，10% 训练数据时五个骨干的聚合分数跨度为 28 倍（0.24–6.79），全量数据时收缩至 1.9 倍；而无阈值 mAP 在同一骨干上仅波动 1.28 倍，说明误差集中在求和步骤。朴素七维 sigmoid 基线 BCE、ASL 的 mAP 分别为 0.5055、0.5175，ASL 超过五个 CALE 配置中的三个，并与最优配置相差不足 0.03。单标签 FER 上相对七维基线的提升名义显著（配对 t 检验 p = 0.0436），但经 BH 校正（p_BH = 0.2055）及 Wilcoxon 检验（p = 0.1250）后不再成立。

Conclusion: 基于求和的标签扩展方法在用于多标签决策前，必须在部署数据规模上做阈值校准，或改用可学习的聚合方式；其性能瓶颈来自未校准的聚合规则而非表示能力。

Abstract: Label-space expansion - predicting a larger joint space and summing back to the original labels - is a natural design for multi-label facial expression recognition, but its failure modes have not been characterised. We evaluate Condition-Aware Label Expansion (CALE) on a self-built dataset (30,240 training images, 3,460 multi-label test images), on seven backbones, and on the public RAF-DB and RAF-ML benchmarks. The central finding is that the aggregation rule, not the representation, decides whether the method works. With a fixed threshold of 0.5, the aggregate score spans 28x across five backbones at 10% of the training data (0.24 to 6.79) and collapses to a 1.9x range at full data, while threshold-free mAP stays within 1.28x over the same backbones, which localises the fault to the summation step. The mechanism is the uncalibrated aggregation rule, which adds the main-branch score to half the sum of the three non-main branches; we verify it by measuring main- and non-main-branch activations. Two plain seven-dimensional sigmoid baselines (BCE, ASL) reach mAP 0.5055 and 0.5175; ASL exceeds three of the five CALE configurations and falls within 0.03 of the best. On single-label FER the gain over a seven-dimensional baseline is nominally significant (paired t-test, p = 0.0436) but does not survive Benjamini-Hochberg correction (p_BH = 0.2055) or a Wilcoxon test (p = 0.1250). We conclude that sum-based label expansion requires threshold calibration at the deployment data scale, or a learned aggregation, before it can support multi-label decisions.

</details>


### [186] [Where Does Proposal Selection Fail? Diagnosing Frozen Vision-Language Queries over Detector Proposals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11873)
*Bo Ma,Weiqi Yan,Jinsong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在固定感知（冻结检测器提案，覆盖≥99.8%指代物）的条件下，系统性地变化选择接口，揭示模块化指代定位的失败来源：VLM图像条件查询远优于纯文本查询，监督良好的选择头可在分布内追平VLM坐标解码，但在分布外（Ref-L4 Objects365、Ref-Adv-s、RefBench-PRO）显著退化，且训练验证器无法超越VLM的yes/no回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化定位（检测器提案+VLM选择）比让VLM直接解码框更廉价，但此类系统在何处失效尚不清楚。作者希望通过控制变量实验定位失败环节。

Method: 固定所有选择器在同一冻结提案集合上选择；对同族两个VLM（Qwen2.5-VL-7B、Qwen3-VL-8B）逐因子改变选择接口；对比图像条件查询与纯文本查询、不同选择头（含交叉注意力头）与VLM坐标解码；在RefCOCO/+/g、Ref-L4、Ref-Adv-s、RefBench-PRO及无目标基准上评测，并考察更宽检测器提示与类无关提案器；训练验证器做存在性探测。

Result: RefCOCOg上VLM图像条件查询比纯文本查询高0.15–0.18（InternVL3-8B增益较小）；剩余错误集中在少数top候选的排序。用全部有效框监督的交叉注意力选择器在RefCOCOg追平VLM坐标解码，Qwen2.5-VL在RefCOCO/+追加其数据训练后亦追平。分布外失效：Ref-L4 Objects365图像上所有训练头降至0.19–0.28，而Qwen解码保持约0.8（InternVL3亦下降）；更宽检测器提示、类无关提案器均不能弥补；Ref-Adv-s与RefBench-PRO上全预算头落后解码0.19–0.37。验证器退化为对VLM查询状态的存在性探测，在公开无目标基准上不优于VLM的yes/no。

Conclusion: 选择器的关键瓶颈是"读什么"（图像条件查询）而非"如何构建"，分布内可匹配VLM解码，但泛化到训练外类别与推理型基准时大幅落后于VLM坐标解码；验证器不提供额外收益。模块化定位的收益受限于选择接口的泛化能力。

Abstract: In modular grounding a frozen detector proposes regions and a vision-language model (VLM) selects one, at lower cost than letting the VLM decode a box, but it is unclear where such systems fail. We hold perception fixed---every trained selector chooses among the same frozen proposals, which contain the referent for at least $99.8\%$ of RefCOCO/+/g queries---and vary the selection interface one factor at a time for two VLMs of one family (Qwen2.5-VL-7B, Qwen3-VL-8B). On RefCOCOg, what the selector reads matters more than how it is built: the VLM's image-conditioned query encodes the referent's location and beats a text-only query by $0.15$--$0.18$ with every head tested (by less for InternVL3-8B, whose vision encoder differs; one head tested). The remaining errors are rankings among a few top candidates; a cross-attention selector supervised with every valid box reaches the VLM's coordinate decoding on RefCOCOg, and with Qwen2.5-VL on RefCOCO/+ when also trained on their data. Beyond that distribution it does not: on Ref-L4's Objects365 images, mostly of categories outside training, every trained head falls to $0.19$--$0.28$ while Qwen decoding keeps about $0.8$ (InternVL3's drops too), neither a wider detector prompt nor a class-agnostic proposer closes the gap, and on public reasoning benchmarks Ref-Adv-s and RefBench-PRO full-budget heads trail decoding by $0.19$--$0.37$ although proposals cover most referents. A trained verifier reduces to an existence probe on the VLM's query state and does not beat the VLM's yes/no answer on public no-target benchmarks.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond Head Importance: Collective Computation in Video Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11875)
*Yi Yang,Siyuan Liu,Bingbing Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频Transformer中注意力头的重要性需从"集体计算"角度理解：功能贡献既取决于头输出在下游如何被使用，也取决于层间局部交互如何复合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力头功能重要性差异很大，仅靠"哪些头重要"的排序无法解释网络内的集体计算，因此需从下游使用与跨层交互复合两个视角重新审视注意力头功能。

Method: 对四个视频Transformer进行消融分析；考察头输出在下游（注意力模块后与同块MLP后）的表征响应与重要性相关性；采用仅微调MLP的方式适配下游处理；并基于跨层复合，比较不同时空注意力距离的头集合对远端token扰动的响应及其联合消融效应。

Result: 单任务上识别的高重要性头也能支持其他视频理解任务并保持区分不同视频的表征结构；头消融的表征响应在注意力模块后与重要性弱相关，在同块MLP后相关更强；仅微调MLP可提升低重要性头输出的利用；短时空注意力距离的头集合在远端token上引发更强扰动响应，其联合消融造成的性能损失甚至大于消融同等规模的高重要性头集合。

Conclusion: 注意力头的功能贡献不能被单一重要性排序完全刻画，存在未被个体排名捕捉的集体贡献；集体计算是理解注意力头功能的关键视角。

Abstract: Attention heads in video Transformers vary greatly in functional importance. Understanding their contributions requires examining not only which heads are important, but also how they participate in collective computation within the network. We study this collective computation from two perspectives: the downstream use of head outputs and the composition of interactions across layers. Ablation analyses of four video Transformers show that high-importance heads identified on a single task also support other video understanding tasks and help preserve the representational structure that distinguishes different videos. Representational responses to head ablation correlate weakly with head importance after the attention module, but more strongly after the MLP in the same block. Adapting this downstream processing through MLP-only fine-tuning further improves the use of lower-importance head outputs. Meanwhile, through composition across layers, sets of heads with shorter spatiotemporal attention distances support stronger perturbation responses at distant tokens than sets with longer distances. Their joint ablation causes even greater performance loss than ablating equally sized sets of high-importance heads, revealing collective contributions not fully captured by individual importance rankings. These findings highlight collective computation as a key perspective for understanding attention head function: functional contributions depend on both how head outputs are used downstream and how local interactions compose across layers.

</details>


### [188] [Training Vision Transformers to Focus: Emergence of Spatial Attention Head Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11890)
*Yuji Nozawa,Yu-Chieh Lin,Youyang Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出两种视觉聚焦损失函数，利用ViT多头注意力机制引导模型关注图像特定区域，在无需标注的情况下实现注意力头的空间专业化，提升可解释性与零样本分割等密集预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DINO等自监督ViT可借助大规模图像实现鲁棒视觉感知，但训练中缺乏聚焦机制，导致感知表征模糊、表征间过度相似。

Method: 设计两种视觉聚焦损失（visual focusing loss），建立模型注意力与图像特定区域的对应关系；借助多头注意力机制选择性地引导模型聚焦，从而在无标注条件下自发形成空间注意力头专业化（spatial attention head specialization）。

Result: 定性与定量评估表明，方法显著提升了注意力头的空间选择性与多样性，同时改善可解释性与零样本图像分割等密集预测任务表现。

Conclusion: 通过损失层面的聚焦约束可有效赋予自监督ViT结构化、多样化的空间注意力，为无标注下的可解释表征学习提供可行路径。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning in Vision Transformer models, such as DINO, enables robust visual perception without requiring labeled data by leveraging large-scale image datasets. However, these models lack a focusing mechanism during training, resulting in perceptual representations that are often ambiguous or overly similar. In this work, we introduce two visual focusing loss functions designed to establish correspondence between the model's attention and specific regions within images. Specifically, we leverage the multi-head attention mechanism within the model to selectively steer the model's focus, leading to the emergence of spatial attention head specialization, characterized by diverse and spatially focused perceptual capabilities without requiring annotations. Through both qualitative and quantitative evaluations, we demonstrate that our method substantially increases the spatial selectivity and diversity of attention heads, improving both interpretability and performance on dense prediction tasks such as zero-shot image segmentation.

</details>


### [189] [Developing a Street View Imagery Processing Pipeline for Building-Level Vacant House Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11893)
*Dongjoon Park,Sunjae Lee,Jung Woo Kim,Bumjoon Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一套可复现的街景影像（SVI）处理流程，显式化并校验"图像适用性"与"目标建筑身份"，应用于韩国空置房屋评估；适用性过滤在保持覆盖率的同时多覆盖101个地块，目标对齐准确率达85.6%，分类AUROC由0.684升至0.719，但错标同图另一栋房屋仍得0.709，表明适用性、目标识别与模型证据是三个独立问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SVI自动审计默认每张图像都清晰呈现目标建筑，但实际图像可能缺乏任务相关证据或拍到邻近房屋；若不加检查，模型分数无法说明其是否评估了正确的建筑。作者希望使图像适用性与目标身份显式化、可核查，从而支撑可复现评估与人工虚拟审计。

Method: 构建可复现的SVI处理流程，用于韩国独栋住宅地块的空置评估，数据为1,609个地块的10,311张图像。①任务适用性过滤：依据图像构图与行政区划自适应调整判定标准，以在不同区域间保持覆盖均衡；②目标房屋对齐：利用相机元数据与地块边界识别所属房屋，无需建筑轮廓数据或额外训练；③进行空置分类，并用Grad-CAM检视模型关注区域。

Result: 适用性过滤在保持与统一过滤器相同图像数量的前提下，使多101个地块获得可用图像，且区域间覆盖更均衡。目标对齐与人工标注一致率为85.6%，优于最佳简单规则的79.8%。空置分类中，过滤与对齐使AUROC从0.684提升至0.719，但若在同一图像中改标另一栋房屋，AUROC仍为0.709。Grad-CAM显示模型常关注目标房屋之外的区域。

Conclusion: 图像适用性、目标识别与模型证据是三个相互独立的问题，仅凭分数无法区分。记录所用图像及所选房屋可使评审者逐项核验评估结果，并能支持人工虚拟审计。

Abstract: Street view imagery (SVI) is widely used to audit urban environments remotely. Automated assessments usually assume that each image shows the target building clearly. In practice, an image may lack task-relevant evidence or show a neighboring house. If this goes unchecked, a model's score cannot show whether it judged the correct building. We present a reproducible SVI processing pipeline that makes image suitability and target identity explicit and checkable. We apply it to vacant house assessment in South Korea, using 10,311 images of 1,609 detached-house parcels. Task-suitability filtering adapts its criteria to image composition and administrative area. This keeps coverage even across areas. While keeping as many images as a single uniform filter, our filtering left 101 more parcels with a usable image. Target-house alignment identifies the parcel's house from camera metadata and parcel boundaries. It needs no building footprints or additional training. Alignment matched human labels for 85.6% of images, compared with 79.8% for the best simple rule. In vacancy classification, filtering and alignment raised AUROC from 0.684 to 0.719, but marking a different house in the same image still gave 0.709. Grad-CAM showed that the model often looked at areas outside the target house. Image suitability, target identification, and model evidence are therefore separate problems, and scores alone do not resolve them. Recording which images were used and which house was selected lets reviewers verify each assessment and can support human virtual audits.

</details>


### [190] [RBA: A Read-out Basis Adapter for Parameter-Efficient Interactive OCTA Vessel Segmentation with SEEM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11906)
*Jaafar M. Al-Ghabban,Mohammad Reza Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出读出基适配器RBA，在SEEM的掩码读出端生成图像特定基图，仅增0.47M参数即在OCTA血管分割上超越LoRA类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEEM等可提示模型需通过多次点击才能分割细血管；全量微调3.42亿参数成本高且易过拟合，LoRA类参数高效方法通常只适配编码器，而SEEM所有候选掩码来自1/4分辨率的单一特征图，对1–3像素宽的血管过于粗糙。

Method: RBA包含两支：读出分支生成32张半分辨率的图像特定基图，每个候选掩码通过其专属向量与零初始化投影访问这些基图，从而初始状态与SEEM完全一致；编码器分支采用标准rank-1 LoRA，提供区分动脉与静脉所需信息。新增可训练参数0.47M。

Result: 在模拟误差驱动点击条件下，OCTA-500 3mm大血管上RBA优于LoRA、Conv-LoRA和AdaptFormer；三次随机种子平均IoU 85.16%、Dice 91.93%，分别高出Conv-LoRA 2.90和1.72个百分点。6mm子集单次运行领先4.35和2.76个百分点。增益在动脉、静脉与ROSE-O上成立，但FAZ上不成立。

Conclusion: 在SEEM中，适配容量配置在掩码读出端比在编码器端收益更大。

Abstract: Retinal disease monitoring with optical coherence tomography angiography (OCTA) relies on segmentation of vessels and the foveal avascular zone (FAZ). Promptable models such as SEEM (Segment Everything Everywhere All at Once) allow click-based refinement, but without adaptation SEEM segments thin vessels poorly and needs many clicks. Fully fine-tuning its 342 million parameters on a few hundred images is costly and risks overfitting, and parameter-efficient methods such as low-rank adaptation (LoRA) usually adapt only the encoder. Yet SEEM reads every candidate mask from one feature map at quarter resolution, too coarse for vessels one to three pixels wide. This study introduces the Read-out Basis Adapter (RBA), whose read-out branch generates 32 image-specific basis maps at half resolution. Each candidate accesses them through its own vector and a zero-initialised projection, so the model starts exactly as SEEM. The encoder branch, a standard rank-1 LoRA, supplies the information needed to tell arteries from veins. RBA adds 0.47 million trainable parameters. Under simulated error-driven clicks, with the ground truth used only to place them, RBA outperforms LoRA, Conv-LoRA andAdaptFormer on OCTA-500 3mmlarge vessels. Averaged over three seeds, it reaches an intersection over union (IoU) of 85.16% and a Dice score of 91.93%, 2.90 and 1.72 percentage points above Conv-LoRA. On the 6 mm subset, one run gives margins of 4.35 and 2.76 points. The gain extends to arteries, veins and ROSE-O but not to the FAZ. In SEEM, adaptation capacity pays more at the mask read-out than in the encoder.

</details>


### [191] [LO-World: Unified BEV Latent Modeling for Joint 4D Occupancy and LiDAR Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11912)
*Zhimin Liao,Ping Wei,Kai Mao,Shuaijia Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一的4D世界模型LO-World，在共享BEV隐空间中同时预测LiDAR点云与3D语义占据，实现跨模态互正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR点云擅长细粒度度量几何，3D语义占据提供稠密体积语义，二者天然互补。但现有世界模型要么独立预测两种模态，要么启发式级联，错失跨模态互正则化的机会，并带来多管线冗余。

Method: 采用单流（single-stream）框架，在共享BEV隐空间内联合预测两模态；耦合高效混合range-voxel自编码器与flow-matching生成器，并缓存历史上下文以降低迭代采样延迟。

Result: 优于此前SOTA基线：占据预测IoU提升10.9%，LiDAR Chamfer Distance降低30.1%；单张RTX 4090上实时吞吐超过25 FPS。

Conclusion: 共享BEV隐空间的统一4D世界模型可让稠密语义与度量几何相互增强，同时消除多管线冗余并保持实时性。

Abstract: LiDAR point clouds and 3D semantic occupancy represent two distinct modalities capturing fine-grained metric geometry and dense volumetric semantics, respectively, holding natural potential for mutual complementarity. However, existing world models typically forecast these modalities independently or cascade them heuristically, missing the opportunity for reciprocal cross-modal regularization. To address this, we propose \textbf{LO-World}, a unified 4D world model that forecasts both modalities within a shared BEV latent space. This single-stream formulation enables dense semantics and metric geometry to mutually reinforce each other, while eliminating multi-pipeline redundancy. Specifically, LO-World couples an efficient hybrid range-voxel autoencoder with a flow-matching generator that caches historical context to reduce iterative sampling latency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LO-World outperforms prior state-of-the-art baselines, improving occupancy forecasting IoU by 10.9% and reducing LiDAR Chamfer Distance by 30.1%. Meanwhile, it sustains a real-time throughput exceeding $25$~FPS on a single RTX 4090. Our code is available at https://github.com/lzzzzzm/LO-World.

</details>


### [192] [Text Alone Predicts Which Classes a Segmentation Readout Keeps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11926)
*David Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 开放词汇分割的读出层会"静默丢失"训练类别之外的类别：仍以高置信度输出掩码，但该类不赢得任何像素，且训练损失照常下降、mIoU甚至上升。作者证明哪类会坍缩仅由类名列表决定，可在无图像情况下用文本相似度排序预测，并揭示语音汇距离机制及无需重训的修复手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇分割评估存在盲区：训练类别外的类别坍缩（不占任何像素）不被损失或mIoU反映，导致部署时类别"静默死亡"。作者希望解释坍缩的成因、预测哪些类会坍缩，并给出可操作的补救措施。

Method: 1) 在未见任何图像前，仅基于类名文本与训练词表的相似度对每个类的坍缩风险排序；2) 在九个模型家族、十二个骨干、八种预训练配方上验证该排序（另有两个族仅在预测已写定后测量）；3) 对照实验：同一图像下，将对应类名移入/移出训练列表重训，对比相对冻结基线的增益；4) 机制分析：考察坍缩类在近名邻居间的投票行为及冻结特征中的可分离性；5) 干预实验：降低读出层幅值或早停，以及训练时压低幅值。

Result: 坍缩风险排序在AUROC 0.69–0.96间成立，另两族落在同一区间；类名移入/移出可使该类相对冻结基线的增益翻转21 IoU点，而替换为无关名称无影响；坍缩类在所有投票中输给近名邻居，但在冻结特征中仍可分离；降低读出幅值或早停可在不重训情况下恢复最多+16点；训练时压低幅值则读出从不坍缩。

Conclusion: 类别坍缩由类名词表距离驱动，可由文本相似度提前预测并可通过"改写列表"与调整读出幅值干预；冻结特征中信息未丢失，问题出在读出层竞争。作者据此提出无需重训的恢复方案与更稳健的训练配置。

Abstract: An open-vocabulary segmentation readout silently loses a class deployed beyond its training categories. It still emits masks with high confidence, but the class never wins a single pixel. The training loss falls anyway, and mIoU can even rise while classes die. We show that which classes collapse is written in the class-name list alone. Before any image is seen, we rank each class's collapse risk from its text similarity to the training vocabulary alone. The ranking holds at AUROC 0.69-0.96 across nine model families, twelve backbones and eight pretraining recipes. Two further families, measured only after the predictions were written down, land in the same band. What the list writes, it can rewrite. With identical images and a class's counterpart moved in or out of the training list, retraining flips that class's gain over the frozen baseline by 21 IoU points; swapping in unrelated names changes nothing. We find a vocabulary-distance mechanism behind the collapse. Collapsed classes lose every vote to near-name neighbors, but in the frozen features they survive. Turning down the readout's amplitude, or stopping training early, brings them back, up to +16 points, without retraining; trained with its amplitude held down, the readout never collapses.

</details>


### [193] [Scale-Consistent Monocular Fisheye Visual Odometry via Spherical Bundle Adjustment-Guided Unsupervised Depth Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11934)
*Wanhui Luo,Hongbo Zhao,Yi Zhou,Qijun Chen,Yiming Nie,Rui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S²D-FVO单目鱼眼视觉里程计框架，将球面捆绑调整(SBA)与无监督深度学习耦合，用切空间角重投影残差与中值深度尺度正则提升长轨迹下的尺度一致性与深度精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目鱼眼VO因大视场和单目配置简单而适用于机器人定位建图，但长轨迹上尺度漂移严重；无监督深度学习可缓解该问题，然而鱼眼图像强畸变使深度估计困难。

Method: 指出像素重投影残差不能均匀反映视线角偏差，且标量角残差的一阶线性化对某一切线方向扰动不敏感；因此基于球面对数映射构造切空间角重投影残差，使其在两个切线方向均保持敏感性且范数等于对应标量角残差。SBA中引入基于中值深度的尺度正则项约束重建尺度；SBA在无监督深度学习中替代位姿估计网络以提供更可靠的相对位姿用于视图合成，训练好的深度网络反过来为SBA提供尺度参考，从而提升尺度一致性并可细化单个三维路标位置。

Result: 在多个公开数据集及两个新采集的大规模数据集上的实验表明，该框架在深度估计与轨迹估计精度上优于代表性现有方法，且适用于不同相机模型。

Conclusion: SBA引导的无监督深度学习与深度网络尺度参考形成的闭环有效改善了鱼眼单目VO的尺度一致性、深度精度与位姿精度，具备跨相机模型的通用性。

Abstract: The wide field of view of fisheye cameras and the simplicity of a monocular setup make monocular fisheye visual odometry (VO) attractive for robotic localization and mapping. Nevertheless, scale drift remains a major challenge over long trajectories. Unsupervised depth learning can help to mitigate this issue, but the pronounced distortion of fisheye images poses challenges for accurate depth estimation. This article presents S$^2$D-FVO, a monocular fisheye VO framework integrating spherical bundle adjustment (SBA)-guided unsupervised depth learning to improve scale consistency and depth estimation accuracy. Given that pixel reprojection residuals do not uniformly reflect angular deviations between viewing rays and that the first-order linearization of a scalar angular reprojection residual is insensitive to perturbations along one tangent direction, SBA employs tangent-space angular reprojection residuals derived from the spherical logarithmic map. These residuals retain sensitivity along both tangent directions, with their norms equal to the corresponding scalar angular residuals. A median depth-based scale regularization term is further integrated into SBA to constrain the reconstruction scale. Building on these formulations, SBA replaces the pose estimation network during unsupervised depth learning, providing more reliable relative poses for view synthesis. In turn, depth estimates from the well-trained depth estimation network provide scale references for SBA, improving scale consistency while allowing individual 3-D landmark positions to be refined. Extensive experiments conducted on multiple public datasets, as well as two newly collected large-scale datasets, demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms representative existing approaches in both depth and trajectory estimation accuracy and is applicable to different camera models.

</details>


### [194] [Watching Itself Watch: Self-Auditing Visual Reliance for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11949)
*Rohan Dugad,Mi Luo,Kristen Grauman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIOS：一种视觉感知的在线策略自蒸馏后训练框架，通过逐token的"视觉依赖差距"定位视觉证据利用不足的位置，实现密集、定向的监督，仅需单次self-rollout即可显著提升视频推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频推理的关键信息集中于少数视觉决定性token，但GRPO等主流后训练方法只给轨迹级粗粒度奖励，忽略该结构，且需多次rollout与昂贵的外部评判者/更大教师模型。

Method: 核心思路是让模型自审其视觉依赖度：比较有无视频输入时下一token分布的差异，量化每个预测对视觉输入的依赖；分别计算学生与"答案特权"教师的依赖度，得到逐token的reliance gap，定位教师利用而学生忽略视觉证据的位置。三个组件将该信号转为密集定向监督：（1）与学生共同演化的EMA教师；（2）鼓励预测依赖视频的对比目标；（3）reliance-gap门控，将学习集中在视觉利用不足的token上。仅需单模型单次on-policy rollout，无需外部奖励、评判者或更大教师。

Result: 在覆盖通用、时序、空间、知识密集与长视频理解的12个基准上，VIOS一致提升三种骨干模型，并优于同期视频推理系统；训练步数减少9倍以上、FLOPs减少60倍、生成token减少111倍。

Conclusion: 逐token视觉依赖差距可作为有效且高效的密集监督信号，替代轨迹级奖励与外部教师，在显著降低计算与采样成本的同时提升视频推理性能，验证了"位置感知"后训练范式的价值。

Abstract: Video reasoning hinges on a small number of visually decisive tokens embedded in otherwise largely language-driven chains of thought. Yet prevailing post-training methods such as GRPO overlook this structure, assigning coarse trajectory-level rewards while requiring multiple rollouts and often costly external judges. We introduce Visually-Aware On-Policy Self-Distillation (VIOS), a post-training framework that identifies where a model under-utilizes visual evidence and concentrates learning at those positions. Our key insight is that a model can audit its own visual reliance: the divergence between its next-token distributions with and without the video reveals how strongly each prediction depends on the visual input. Measuring this reliance for both a student and an answer-privileged teacher exposes a per-token reliance gap, identifying positions where the teacher exploits visual evidence that the student overlooks. VIOS translates this signal into dense, targeted supervision through three complementary components: an exponential moving average (EMA) teacher that co-evolves with the student, a contrastive objective that encourages predictions to depend on the video, and a reliance-gap gate that focuses learning on visually under-utilized tokens. Crucially, VIOS requires only a single on-policy rollout from a single model, without external rewards, judges, or larger teachers. Across 12 benchmarks spanning general, temporal, spatial, knowledge-intensive, and long-video understanding, VIOS consistently improves three model backbones and outperforms contemporary video-reasoning systems, while training in over 9x fewer steps, 60x fewer FLOPs, and 111x fewer generated tokens. Project page: https://vision.cs.utexas.edu/projects/VIOS/

</details>


### [195] [UltraVoxelGS: Voxel-First Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting for 3D Ultrasound Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11951)
*Yue Wang,Haoxin Chen,Xiaoqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UltraVoxelGS 提出前馈式体素到高斯的三维超声重建方法，首次在数十秒内完成重建（较逐场景优化快35倍以上）并实现物理可信渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有前馈高斯方法依赖自然图像中的稠密重叠与光度一致性，而超声切片之间最多只有一维交叠，并因声学传播与衰减呈视角依赖外观，导致先前的逐场景优化方法只能离线使用，无法在2D扫查中提供实时空间反馈。

Method: 提出前馈体素到高斯通路：将所有已知位姿切片提升到固定尺寸体素支撑中，直接预测高斯参数，使表示规模与输入帧数解耦；渲染端引入超声感知外观渲染策略，在训练中分解反射与累积衰减，并利用空间相邻切片的外观连续性进行高效的渲染时外观自适应（Render-time Appearance Adaptation）。

Result: 在真实与仿真数据集上，PSNR/SSIM 均优于逐场景优化基线，推理时间从10–20分钟降至约10秒（提速逾35倍），并可扩展至超过1000帧的序列。

Conclusion: 前馈体素—高斯表示结合超声感知外观建模，可同时满足快速重建与物理可信渲染，为超声扫查中的实时空间反馈提供可行路径。

Abstract: We present UltraVoxelGS, a 3D ultrasound reconstruction method that achieves both fast reconstruction and physically faithful rendering for the first time, bringing live spatial feedback during 2D ultrasound scanning within reach. This is made possible by a feed-forward design that predicts a complete Gaussian scene representation from posed ultrasound images directly, entirely bypassing the per-scene optimization bottleneck that has confined prior methods to offline use. Realising such feed-forward prediction in this setting is non-trivial: unlike natural images, ultrasound slices share at most one-dimensional intersections and exhibit view-dependent appearance due to acoustic propagation and attenuation, violating the dense overlap and photometric consistency assumed by existing feed-forward Gaussian methods. Hence, to achieve fast reconstruction, we introduce a feed-forward voxel-to-Gaussian pass that decouples representation size from input count by lifting all posed slices into a fixed-size voxel support and predicting Gaussian parameters directly. For faithful rendering, we propose ultrasound-aware appearance rendering strategy factorize reflection from cumulative attenuation during training and exploit appearance continuity among spatially adjacent slices for efficient Render-time Appearance Adaptation. On both real-world and simulated datasets, UltraVoxelGS achieves best PSNR/SSIM compared with per-scene optimization baselines while reducing inference from 10-20 minutes to approximately 10 seconds (over 35 times faster) and scales to sequences exceeding 1,000 frames. Code is available at https://github.com/YuebyYue/UltraVoxelGS-camera-ready.

</details>


### [196] [3R-Adapter: Retrieval, Rewiring, and Refinement for Efficient Adaptation of 3D Reconstruction Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11964)
*Zixiang Ai,Zhenyu Cui,Xu Zou,Jiahuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3R-Adapter，一种面向长尾3D重建的PEFT范式，将域偏移下的适配建模为"对应关系恢复"，通过可变形检索、关系重连与一致性精炼修复跨视图关系结构塌缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈3D重建模型在大规模预训练下泛化良好，但在低光照重建、人-场景联合重建、测试时自适应等长尾场景中表现不佳。作者观察到主因是"关系注意力结构塌缩"，即跨视图对应关系失效；而现有PEFT方法只做通道级特征调整，缺乏显式适配关系结构的机制，无法解决对应失效问题。

Method: 将3D重建模型适配形式化为域偏移下的对应关系恢复问题，提出3R-Adapter，包含三部分：(1) Deformable Retrieval，任务自适应的关系候选检索；(2) Relational Rewiring，重建跨视图关系结构；(3) Consistency Refinement，将重连后的关系注入稳定预测。

Result: 在低光照重建、人-场景联合重建与测试时自适应三类任务上大量实验表明，方法在保持轻量高效适配的同时，鲁棒性显著优于现有PEFT方法。

Conclusion: 关系结构塌缩是前馈3D重建在长尾场景失效的关键原因，显式恢复跨视图对应关系的PEFT设计可有效提升鲁棒性，且保持参数高效；代码已开源。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models have demonstrated strong generalization under large-scale pre-training, yet they struggle in long-tail scenarios such as low-light reconstruction, joint human-scene reconstruction, and test-time adaptation. We observe relational attention structure collapse, manifested as the failure of cross-view correspondences, as a primary cause of these limitations. While adaptation offers a practical way to bridge this gap, existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) approaches mainly perform channel-wise feature adjustment and lack mechanisms to explicitly adapt relational structures, leaving correspondence failures unresolved. In this work, we formulate adaptation of 3D reconstruction models as a problem of correspondence recovery under domain shift and propose 3R-Adapter, a PEFT paradigm for long-tail 3D reconstruction scenarios. Our method consists of three components: Deformable Retrieval for task-adaptive relation candidate retrieval, Relational Rewiring for reconstructing cross-view relational structures, and Consistency Refinement for injecting the rewired relations into stable predictions. Extensive experiments on low-light reconstruction, joint human-scene reconstruction, and test-time adaptation demonstrate that our approach significantly improves robustness over existing PEFT methods while maintaining efficient lightweight adaptation. Our code is released at https://github.com/PKU-OV3-LAB/3R-Adapter.

</details>


### [197] [AnyBreak: Towards Jailbreaking Any T2I Model for Any Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.11980)
*Qi Lu,Zehui Guo,Yucheng Zhao,Zijing Li,Chuhan Song,Wenrui Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出黑盒越狱框架 AnyBreak，通过多尺度渐进语义对齐构造对抗图像，在仅用干净文本提示条件下诱导 T2I 模型生成 NSFW 内容，并给出可绕过事后安全检测器的增强版 AnyBreak-V。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 T2I 模型可生成高质量图像，但存在被诱导生成色情、暴力等 NSFW 内容的越狱风险；已有攻击多受提示过滤器限制且依赖白盒假设，缺乏在干净文本与黑盒条件下的可行攻击路径，因此需要揭示跨模态攻击面。

Method: 构建对抗图像，采用多尺度渐进语义对齐，在生成过程中将潜空间生成轨迹引导至不安全数据流形，实现恶意概念由文本模态向图像模态的跨模态迁移；推理阶段仅需干净文本提示与对抗图像，适用于任意 T2I 模型与任意恶意概念的全黑盒场景；并提出增强变体 AnyBreak-V，引入补偿项以绕过事后安全检查器。

Result: 大量实验表明，在黑盒与干净文本约束下，AnyBreak 取得较高攻击成功率，同时保持视觉保真度与输入提示的语义一致性；AnyBreak-V 可有效规避事后安全检测。

Conclusion: 研究揭示现有 T2I 防御未充分覆盖关键的跨模态攻击面，提示过滤器与事后检查器不足以应对基于对抗图像的越狱，需针对跨模态迁移路径设计防御。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in image generation and editing, yet this progress has also introduced significant security risks. Prior work has shown that adversarial prompt can induce the generation of Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content, including pornographic, violent, and graphic imagery. However, such attacks are typically mitigated by prompt filters and often rely on white-box assumptions. In this paper, we propose AnyBreak, a novel jailbreak framework that constructs adversarial images via multi-scale progressive semantic alignment. During the generation process, these adversarial images steer the generation trajectory in latent space towards unsafe data manifolds, enabling cross-modal transfer of malicious concepts from the text modality to the image modality. AnyBreak operates using only clean textual prompts and adversarial images at inference time, and can jailbreak any T2I models towards any malicious concepts in a fully black-box setting. We further introduce AnyBreak-V, an enhanced variant that incorporates a compensation term to bypass post-hoc safety checkers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AnyBreak achieves high attack success rates while preserving visual fidelity and semantic consistency with the input prompts under black-box and clean-text constraints. Our findings reveal that existing T2I defense leave critical cross-modal attack surfaces insufficiently addressed. The code is available at https://github.com/luqi-glory/AnyBreak.

</details>


### [198] [HIST3R: Decomposing and Rectifying State Updates with History for Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12011)
*Kenneth Yang,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HIST3R 通过"方向—幅度"分解统一分析 CUT3R 类循环状态更新，提出免训练框架同时用历史信息校正更新方向并调制更新幅度，在有限内存下显著缓解长时序几何漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式三维重建依赖循环状态实现有界内存的长视频理解，但每帧更新会写入紧凑场景记忆并影响后续所有预测，不可靠更新会覆盖历史上下文并累积成长期几何漂移。现有免训练扩展仅做幅度控制，只能衰减不可靠更新而无法纠正其方向。

Method: 将 CUT3R 式循环更新分解为方向与幅度：幅度为更新增益（观测写入状态的强度），方向为几何意图（状态被推向何处）。基于该视角，HIST3R 利用局部一致的历史状态校正更新方向，并依据当前观测与其历史之间的视觉差异调制更新幅度，抑制冗余更新同时保留互补证据，全程无需训练。

Result: 在相机位姿估计、三维重建与视频深度估计任务上均一致提升长时序性能，同时保持 CUT3R 的内存占用与推理速度。1000 帧输入下，ATE 在 ScanNet 上相对最强对比方法降低 23.87%，在 TUM-Dynamics 上降低 33.65%；500 帧下，中位重建精度在 7-Scenes 上相对最强 CUT3R 变体提升 56.52%，在 NRGBD 上提升 33.96%。

Conclusion: 方向—幅度分解为循环式流式重建提供了统一分析框架，并揭示方向纠正是现有方法的空缺；HIST3R 以零训练成本同时控制两分量，在不增加内存与计算的前提下有效抑制长程漂移，验证了历史一致性作为更新调控信号的有效性。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction with recurrent states enables long-video scene understanding under bounded memory, but turns reconstruction into a state-evolution problem: each incoming frame updates a compact scene memory on which all subsequent predictions depend, so unreliable updates overwrite historical context and accumulate into long-horizon geometric drift. We analyze CUT3R-style recurrent updates through a direction--magnitude decomposition, in which the magnitude sets the update gain, i.e., how strongly an observation is written into the state, and the direction encodes the geometric intent, i.e., where the update drives the state. This decomposition provides a unified view of CUT3R and its variants, revealing that existing training-free extensions perform magnitude-only control: they can attenuate an unreliable update but cannot correct its direction. We propose HIST3R, a training-free framework built on CUT3R that controls both components using history. HIST3R rectifies the update direction using locally consistent historical states, and modulates the update magnitude according to the visual discrepancy between the current observation and its history, suppressing redundant updates while preserving complementary evidence. Across camera pose estimation, 3D reconstruction, and video depth estimation, HIST3R consistently improves long-horizon performance while retaining the memory footprint and inference speed of CUT3R. With 1000 input frames, it reduces ATE by 23.87% on ScanNet and 33.65% on TUM-Dynamics relative to the strongest competing method; with 500 frames, it improves median reconstruction accuracy over the strongest CUT3R-based variant by 56.52% on 7-Scenes and 33.96% on NRGBD.

</details>


### [199] [TIRAD: Text-guided Image Residual Learning for Generalist Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12023)
*Seongsu Lee,Seunghyun Oh,Seungjae Moon,Youngmin Ro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TIRAD——一个多模态残差学习框架，将“正常/异常状态文本描述之差”定义为文本残差特征，并用其门控调制图像残差特征，从而获得更具判别性的异常表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于残差的异常检测主要依赖图像表征得到的视觉差异，而可通过语言显式表达的语义差异（semantic discrepancy）尚未被充分挖掘；仅用视觉残差会引入与缺陷无关的冗余差异。

Method: 文本残差特征（正常态与异常态文本表示之差）经Text Fusion Module转换为patch级门控，对每个patch的图像残差进行调制：保留文本残差判定为缺陷相关的视觉差异，抑制其余部分。

Result: 在工业基准数据集以及工业到医学的迁移设定上，方法优于现有以图像为中心的残差学习方法。

Conclusion: 显式引入语言语义残差可有效引导图像残差学习，提升通用异常检测（generalist AD）的判别力与跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent anomaly detection studies have shown that residual features, which model the discrepancy between normal and abnormal patterns, provide strong generalization to anomalies unseen during training. However, existing residual-based approaches mainly rely on visual discrepancies derived from image representations, while semantic discrepancies that can be explicitly expressed through language remain largely underexplored. To address this problem, we propose TIRAD, Text-guided Image Residual Learning for Generalist Anomaly Detection, a multimodal residual learning framework that defines the discrepancy between textual representations of normal and abnormal states as a Text Residual Feature and uses it to guide the Image Residual Feature. Specifically, a Text Fusion Module converts patch-level text residuals into a gate that modulates the image residual of each patch. The gate retains the visual discrepancies that the text residual identifies as defect-related and attenuates the others, leading to more discriminative anomaly representations. Extensive experiments on industrial benchmarks and industrial-to-medical transfer demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing image-centric residual learning methods.

</details>


### [200] [Single Image Super-Resolution via Semantic Prompt and Cross-Modal Training on Content Area](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12025)
*Xiao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种将像素强度与语义信息融合的超分辨率方法：抽取文本描述与空间定位等语义数据并编码，通过跨模态特征融合模块生成语义特征图，并用提示牵引头将语义注入主干网络以细化图像细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CNN与Transformer的深度超分模型擅长像素级增强，但忽视图像中丰富的语义上下文，限制了细节恢复效果。

Method: 提取语义数据（文本描述符与空间位置信息）并编码；设计Cross-Modal Feature Fusion模块，通过多模态训练生成语义特征图；设计Prompt Traction Head将语义信息注入主干网络。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验验证了该方法相较现有方法的优越性（摘要未给出具体量化指标）。

Conclusion: 将语义先验与像素级建模协同结合，可有效提升超分辨率的细节恢复质量，说明语义引导是多模态超分的有前景方向。

Abstract: Enhancing image quality through super-resolution is crucial in image and video processing. While contemporary deep learning models, such as CNNs and Transformers, excel in pixel-level enhancements, they often overlook the rich semantic context within images. To address this, we introduce a novel approach that synergizes pixel intensity with semantic information for superior detail recovery. Our method involves extracting semantic data, including textual descriptors and spatial positioning, and encoding this information. We also present a Cross-Modal Feature Fusion module for generating semantic feature maps via multimodal training, and a Prompt Traction Head to augment the backbone network with semantic insights, thereby refining image details. Our empirical results across various datasets validate the superiority of our approach.

</details>


### [201] [SAFE-Hair: Scalp-Anchored Fields for Exportable Single-View Hair Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12037)
*Yucheng Wang,Zedong Wang,Yuetong Wu,Yue Ma,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAFE-Hair 通过规范头皮-UV 域上的持久毛囊解码显式发丝 groom，实现单视图发型重建的可直接导出资产化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单视图 3D 发型重建多追求视觉合理性，产物难以直接用于 grooming、编辑与仿真管线；缺乏兼顾重建保真度与资产可用性的评测基准。

Method: 以拟合模板头为输入，将自监督图像特征投影到头皮；用条件 Rectified Flow 模型生成潜在头皮场；在固定毛囊集合上解码占据、分段方向、长度分配与总长度；并引入贴合、碰撞与弯曲先验，无需逐受试者测试时优化；同时构建 HairBench-3D 基准。

Result: 相较最强基线：Chamfer 距离 3.74→3.28 mm，F-score 77.9→81.7，不可见区域 F-score 66.1→69.8，穿插比例 4.8%→3.9%。

Conclusion: 该方法以毛囊为根的显式表示天然保证发丝身份一致性并支持带毛囊 ID 与 guide-family 元数据直接导出，结合几何先验在保真度与资产可用性上均优于基线。

Abstract: Single-view 3D hair reconstruction has progressed rapidly, but many methods optimize for visual plausibility rather than producing strand assets that can be directly used in grooming, editing, or simulation pipelines. We propose SAFE-Hair, a framework that reconstructs explicit strand grooms from a single portrait by decoding hair from persistent follicles in a canonical scalp-UV domain. Given a fitted template head, SAFE-Hair projects self-supervised image features onto the scalp, uses a conditional Rectified Flow model to generate latent scalp fields, and decodes occupancy, segment directions, length allocation, and total length at a fixed set of scalp follicles. This representation enforces scalp-rooted strand identities by construction and enables direct export with follicle IDs and guide-family metadata. We further introduce attachment, collision, and bending priors to improve geometric validity without per-subject test-time optimization. To evaluate both reconstruction fidelity and asset usability, we introduce HairBench-3D, a benchmark built from public 3D hair assets with standardized alignment, rendering, resampling, and metrics for geometry, direction consistency, invisible-region completion, silhouette agreement, and collision. SAFE-Hair improves Chamfer distance from 3.74 mm to 3.28 mm, F-score from 77.9 to 81.7, invisible-region F-score from 66.1 to 69.8, and penetration ratio from 4.8% to 3.9% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [202] [Leveraging Complementary Event Frame and Voxel Representations for RGB-Guided Multimodal Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12040)
*Wentao Wu,Xiao Wang,Yi Wang,Chenglong Li,Yonghong Tian,Bin Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CM3AE 2.0，一个统一的RGB帧与事件体素/事件帧多模态预训练框架，通过跨模态生成挖掘潜在跨模态依赖，提升多模态信息交互与协同建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-Event多模态预训练对跨模态交互与协同建模不足，缺乏对潜在跨模态依赖的利用，需统一框架充分学习共享表示与互补信息。

Method: 提出两项策略：(1) 事件感知时空掩码重建（ESMR）：利用事件响应引导掩码聚焦动态显著区域，通过时空掩码重建学习空间结构及其时序变化，并引入多模态融合重建与跨模态对比学习建模互补信息、学习共享表示；(2) 分层跨模态生成（HCG）：视觉层面用预训练扩散模型做事件条件RGB生成以捕获视觉关联，语言层面基于联合RGB-Event表示生成视频级文本描述，连接多模态视觉特征与语言语义。

Result: 在四个下游任务上，CM3AE 2.0均取得一致性能提升，验证了预训练框架的有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 联合多模态时空表示学习与跨模态生成可有效增强RGB-Event信息交互，统一预训练框架具有良好有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose CM3AE 2.0, a unified RGB Frame and Event-Voxel/-Frame pre-training framework. By jointly learning multimodal spatiotemporal representations and exploiting latent cross-modal dependencies through cross-modal generation, the framework enhances multimodal information interaction and collaborative modeling capabilities. Specifically, we first propose an Event-aware Spatiotemporal Masked Reconstruction (ESMR) strategy to learn unified multimodal spatiotemporal representations. It leverages event responses to guide masking toward dynamically salient regions. Through spatiotemporal masked reconstruction, it learns spatial structures and their temporal variations from continuous RGB-Event observations. In addition, it incorporates multimodal fusion reconstruction and cross-modal contrastive learning to facilitate the modeling of cross-modal complementary information and the learning of shared representations. Building upon this, we further propose a Hierarchical Cross-modal Generation (HCG) strategy to learn latent cross-modal dependencies through visual and language generation. At the visual level, Event-conditioned RGB generation is performed using a pre-trained diffusion model to capture visual associations between the two modalities. At the language level, video-level text description generation based on joint RGB-Event representations establishes connections between multimodal visual features and linguistic semantics, further enriching the learning of cross-modal dependencies. Extensive experiments on four diverse downstream tasks demonstrate that CM3AE 2.0 consistently improves performance, validating the effectiveness and generalization capability of the proposed pre-training framework.

</details>


### [203] [Four-Step Text-Bearing Image Generation via Distillation with Local Response Regularization and Teacher-Guided Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12042)
*Kairui Wen,Sheng Ye,Yong-Jin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三步式四步蒸馏框架：局部响应正则化+教师引导文本训练，将GLM-Image DiT蒸馏为4步学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型文生图模型靠多次去噪同时提升场景连贯性与字符准确率，但采样开销大；少步蒸馏虽降成本，却迫使学生在极短轨迹内同时组织场景与精修字形，难以兼顾。

Method: 基于分布匹配（distribution matching）的四步蒸馏框架，保留GLM-Image的AR视觉先验、字形编码器与VAE；引入路径长度惩罚（path-length penalty）促使条件去噪场的局部响应一致；采用渐进式训练，混合合成字符串与教师生成图像，以保持字符准确率与场景融合排版；并在Qwen-Image上验证正则项与文本监督的通用增益。

Result: 学生模型GenEval Overall 0.84、CVTG-2K词准确率0.5045，对比四步TDM的0.81与0.1690；系数研究倾向1e-5；单图推理1.742 s vs 教师5.818 s，3.34×加速，显存需求同为40 GB。

Conclusion: 局部响应正则化与教师引导文本训练可在极少步数下有效保留文字渲染精度，实现低成本高质量文本图像生成，且方法具跨模型可迁移性。

Abstract: Generating text-bearing images requires coherent scenes and accurate characters. Large text-to-image models improve both capabilities through repeated denoising, at substantial sampling cost. Few-step distillation reduces this cost but requires the student to organize scenes and refine glyphs within a short trajectory. We present a four-step distillation framework combining local response regularization with teacher-guided text training. Built on distribution matching, the framework distills the GLM-Image DiT while retaining its autoregressive visual prior, glyph encoder, and VAE. A path-length penalty promotes consistent local responses of the conditional denoising field. Progressive training combines synthetic strings with teacher-generated imagery to preserve character accuracy and scene-integrated typography. The student achieves GenEval Overall 0.84 and CVTG-2K word accuracy 0.5045, compared with 0.81 and 0.1690 for four-step TDM. A coefficient study favors $10^{-5}$, and Qwen-Image experiments show additional gains from response regularization and text supervision. Single-image inference takes 1.742 s, compared with 5.818 s for the teacher, yielding a $3.34\times$ speedup at the same 40 GB GPU memory requirement.

</details>


### [204] [Attention-Guided Sequence Feature Fusion and Mixed-Space Metric Learning for Cross-View Image Sequence Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12051)
*Nan Wu,Chunfang Yang,Jiangshan Li,Xiangyang Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ASML跨视角图像序列地理定位方法，通过注意力引导的序列融合与度量学习抑制冗余、保持空间结构，并构建CrossRoad-66K新数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面序列图像与卫星影像匹配可提供互补信息，但相邻图像高度重叠带来特征冗余；常规融合策略会削弱跨视角匹配所需的序列空间关系。

Method: (1) Sequential Content Fusion (SCF)：建模跨图像特征相关性，抑制冗余内容，自适应保留具有地理判别性的非重叠区域；(2) Spatial Feature Aggregation (SFA)：捕获并聚合局部特征的空间与语义信息，保持序列空间布局；(3) Geo-InfoNCE损失：同时考虑特征距离与地理距离，进行混合空间度量学习，增强对难负样本的区分能力；(4) 构建CrossRoad-66K序列数据集。

Result: 在SeqGeo与CrossRoad-66K数据集上，R@1分别比SOTA提升3.37%和2.10%，实验与可视化验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 注意力引导的序列融合与地理感知度量学习能有效缓解序列冗余并保持空间结构，是提升跨视角序列定位性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Cross-view image sequence geo-localization aims to determine the geographic location of a sequence of ground images by matching it against geo-tagged satellite imagery. Although sequential observations provide complementary scene information, substantial overlap between adjacent images introduces feature redundancy, while conventional fusion strategies may weaken spatial relationships essential for cross-view matching. Therefore, we propose a cross-view image sequence geo-localization method via Attention-guided Sequence fusion and Metric Learning (ASML). Specifically, a Sequential Content Fusion (SCF) module models cross-image feature correlations to suppress redundant content and adaptively preserve geographically informative non-overlapping regions. Next, a Spatial Feature Aggregation (SFA) module is used to capture and aggregate the spatial and semantic information of local features, so as to maintain the spatial layout of the image sequence. Finally, a Geo-InfoNCE loss function is proposed, which comprehensively considers the feature distance and geographic distance for mixed-space metric learning, thus improving the ability of the model to distinguish hard negative samples. A new cross-view image sequence dataset CrossRoad-66K is established to provide more complex scenarios. Extensive experiments and visualizations on the SeqGeo and CrossRoad-66K datasets demonstrate that compared to state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods, the proposed method ASML achieves an improvement of 3.37% and 2.10% at R@1, respectively.

</details>


### [205] [Weight-Space Programming for Medical Imaging: Do We Still Need Training Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12066)
*Maximilian Lindholz,Richard Ruppel,Annette Milnik,Sebastian Halskov,Semil Eminovic,Charlie A. Hamm,Robin Schmidt,Anika Knupfer,Jana Hutter,Johanna P. Müller,Carolina Dominguez Aleixo,Jawed Nawabi,Jens Vogel-Claussen,Tobias Penzkofer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出用“权重空间编程”（WSP）让LLM仅凭医学知识、不接触任何图像数据，直接生成小规模LeNet式CNN的全部权重与偏置，并在4种模态7个分类任务上检验其判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类器通常依赖大量标注数据，标注成本高且常不可得。作者希望探究：LLM能否仅凭医学知识直接“写出”分类器的参数，从而绕开训练数据，并明确这种方式的性能边界。

Method: Claude Opus 5.5 与 GPT-6 Astra 在单次回复中生成小型LeNet风格CNN的参数（无工具调用、无图像输入）。基线包括零样本MedSigLIP以及同架构用Adam在标签上训练的模型。任务涵盖MedMNIST的胸片、乳腺超声、腹部CT共6个任务，以及SynthUterus盆腔MRI任务，以测试AUC为主要指标。

Result: LLM生成的网络在全部6个MedMNIST任务上均高于随机水平，平均测试AUC分别为0.719（Opus）与0.711（Astra）；两者在6个任务中的5个上优于MedSigLIP，且参数量少逾1万倍。但同架构仅用256张标注图像经Adam训练即在所有MedMNIST任务上取得更高AUC（均值0.828）。SynthUterus上Opus的AUC为0.764，Astra因系统性错误低于随机水平（0.136）。

Conclusion: LLM能够生成具备实际判别能力的医学图像分类器，在极小参数量下展现出强归纳偏置与知识迁移潜力，但仍无法替代真实训练数据；作者公开了协议、日志与全部生成权重。

Abstract: Can a large language model (LLM) write the parameters of a medical image classifier from medical knowledge alone, without any training data? Such classifiers normally require labelled images, which are costly and often unavailable. We investigate this question through weight-space programming (WSP), in which an LLM writes or adapts the weights and biases of a neural network directly, without optimisation on training data. Claude Opus 5.5 and GPT-6 Astra each generate the parameters of a small LeNet-style CNN in a single reply, without tool use and without access to any image. We compare these networks against zero-shot MedSigLIP and against the same architecture trained on labels using the Adam optimiser, across 7 classification tasks in four modalities: chest X-ray, breast ultrasound and abdominal CT (all from MedMNIST), and pelvic MRI (SynthUterus). The LLM-written networks perform above chance on all six MedMNIST tasks, with a mean test AUC of 0.719 for Opus and 0.711 for Astra.
